teyvat tiruuruvaṅkaḷai aḻaku ceyyum poruṭṭu avaṟṟiṟku aṇikalaṉkaḷ cūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.  perumpālum aracarkaḷ aṇiyum vilaiyuyarnta maṇikaḷ vaittu iḻaitta poṉ aṇikalaṉkaḷē cūṭṭap peṟṟaṉa.  teyvaṅkaḷ aracarkaḷukkum aracaṉāka irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu eṇṇi aracarkaḷ aṇiyum vilaiyuyarnta maṇikaḷ patitta aṇikalaṉkaḷ teyvaṅkaḷukku amaikkap peṟṟaṉa.  avai, makuṭam, kuṇṭalam, mālai, kēyūram, vākumālai, kaṅkaṇam, yakñōpa vītam, utrapantaṉam caṉṉa vīram kaṭipantaṉam, kacca pantaṉam, aruṇōṉmālai, ūrumālai pōṉṟa palavākum.
talai aṇikalaṉ:-  talaiyil tarippatu makuṭamākum.  inta makuṭam, caṭāmakuṭam, kirīṭamakuṭam, karaṇṭa makuṭam, maṇimakuṭam, muttu makuṭam eṉap palavakaippaṭum.  kirīṭa makuṭamum maṇi makuṭamum tirumāl pōṉṟa pōka mūrttikaḷukkum, karaṇṭa makuṭam kaṇēcar, tēvi mutaliyavarkaḷukkum muttu makuṭam aṅkayaṟ kaṇṇi mutaliya tēvikaḷukkum caṭāmakuṭam civaperumāṉ mutaliya tēvarkaḷukkum uriyaṉavākum.
kātaṇikalaṉ:-  kuṇṭalam kātil aṇiyum aṇikalaṉākum.  kuṇṭalaṅkaḷ, pattira kuṇṭalam, makarakuṇṭalam, caṅkupattira kuṇṭalam, maṇikuṇṭalam, irattiṉa kuṇṭalam, pāmpuk kuṇṭalam carppa kuṇṭalam eṉap pala vakaippaṭum.  pattira kuṇṭalam ōlaic curuḷ, iḷantuḷirkaḷ pōṉṟa kātaṇi.  makarakuṇṭalam makarat talaiyaiyum pāmpu uṭalaiyum uṭaiya vaṭivilāṉa kātaṇi, caṅkupattira kuṇṭalam, caṅkiṉpuri pōṉṟa kātaṇikalaṉ maṇikuṇṭalam maṇikaḷālum, poṉṉālum ceyyappaṭṭa kātaṇikalaṉ.  pāmpuk kuṇṭalam, nākappāmpu vaṭivil ceyyappeṟṟa oru kuṇṭalam.
kaḻuttaṇi:-  kaḻuttaṇikaḷ pala vakaippaṭum.  avai āram(hāram) allatu mālai eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭum.  mālai kaḻuttiṉiṉṟu mārpu varai toṅkum oru aṇikalaṉākum.  vāku mālai iru tōḷkaḷilum iruntu mārpu varai toṅkum āramākum.  kēyūram mēṟkaiyiṉ naṭuvil aṇiyum aṇi
kalaṉākum.  yakñōpa vītam, iṭatu tōḷiṉiṉṟu valappakkam iṭuppu varai uṭampiṉ irupakutikaḷilum toṅkum nūlākum.  yakñōpa vītam kuptar aracarkaḷ kālattiṟkup piṉ eḻunta kālattil uruvaṅkaḷiltāṉ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  caṉṉavīram yakñōpavitattiliruntu iṭatu pakkam iṭuppuvarai toṅkum aṇikalaṉ utarapantaṉam mārpiṟkum vayiṟṟiṟkum iṭaiyil aṇiyum aṇikalaṉ kaṭipantaṉam iṭuppil aṇiyum oṭṭiyāṇam pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉ.  itaṉ naṭuvil yāḻimukam allatu ciṅka mukam amaikkappaṭṭirukkum.  aruṇōṉ mālai, kaṭipantaṉattiṉiṉṟu toṅkum oru aṇikalaṉ.  ūrumālai kaṭipantaṉattiṉiṉṟu toṭai varai toṅkum oru nīṇṭa mālaiyākum.  kaccu pantaṉam, tēvikaḷiṉ tiru uruvaṅkaḷil mārpiṉ mītu aṇiyappaṭum cīlaik kaṭṭu ākum.
srī caravatīya cittira kāmcātirattil , iṭatu kātil karṇikai (karṇikai - tāmarai malariṉ naṭuvilirukkum kāy pōṉṟa vaṭivamuḷḷa tōṭu vaṭivil uḷḷa kātaṇi) allatu caṅkapattiramum (caṅku vaṭivilāṉa aṇikalaṉ) valatu kātil makarakuṇṭalam allatu kēcari kuṇṭalam allatu viyāḷa kuṇṭalamum aṇintiruppatām (makara kuṇṭalam, makara matcayam pōṉṟa vaṭivilāṉa kātaṇi kēcari kuṇṭalam - ciṅkam pōṉṟa vaṭivilāṉa kātaṇi viyāḷa kuṇṭalam - pāmpu pōṉṟa vaṭivamuḷḷa kātaṇi.
caṭāmakuṭam allatu kirīṭa makuṭam tarittiruppatu aḻakāka irukkum.  makuṭattiṉ uyaram 27,27,26,25 allatu 23 aṅkula mākum.  inta uyaramāṉatu talaiyiṉ ucciyiliruntu kūṟappaṭṭa tākum.
makuṭattiṉ aṭippakkamāṉatu talaiyiṉ akalattai viṭa ½  aṅkulam atika uyaramāka irukka vēṇṭum.  kirīṭattiṉ ucciyil akalamāṉatu ataṉ aṭiyiṉ akalattaiviṭa 6,5,4 aṅkulam kuṟaintirukka vēṇṭum allatu aṭiyiṉ akalattil 6-il 1 paṅku, 5-il 1 paṅku allatu 4-il 1paṅku kuṟaintirukkavēṇṭum.  kirīṭattiṉ aṭiyil muṉpakkam piṉpakkam valatupakkam iṭatu pakkam ākiya nāṉku pakkaṅkaḷilum pūrimam eṉṟa alaṅkārattuṭaṉ kūṭiyirukkavēṇṭum.  (pūrimam - ilai allatu pū pōṉṟu aḻakākat tōṉṟum eḻilārnta amaippu.)
makuṭattiṉ iṭatu pakkattil iḷampiṟai vaṭivamum valatu pakkattil pāmpiṉ vaṭivamum uḷḷa pūviṉāl aḻaku ceyyap paṭṭirukkavēṇṭum.
iṭuppil (kīrttimānattil)aḻakuṟa viḷaṅkum araiñāṇ (kaṭṭuvāl) iṭam peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum.  (araiñāṇ mukappu ciṅkam allatu yāḻi pōṉṟu irukkavēṇṭum.
kēyūram (tōḷvaḷai) aṅkatam (pāhuvalayam) kaṭakattirāyam (mūṉṟu vitamāṉa allatu mūṉṟu vaḷaiyal) muttirikai (mōtiram) utarapantam (iṭuppil kaṭṭum vāl) ivaikaḷiṉāl aṇi ceyyappaṭṭirukkavēṇṭum.  iṭuppil aṇiyum vālil veḷip
pakkaṅkaḷil ilaikaḷ pōṉṟa alaṅkāraṅkaḷ toṅkavēṇṭum.  ūrutāmattuṭaṉ kūṭiya āṭai pātamūlattilē pāta jālam (pāta caram) pōṉṟa aṇikaḷum aṇiyappaṭṭirukkavēṇṭum.
(kēyūram - tōḷvaḷai.  itu tōḷil (puyattil aṇiyum aṇikalaṉākum.  aṅkatam puyavalaiyam.  itu puyattil aṇiyappaṭum aṇikalaṉākum.  kaṭakattirayam - mūṉṟu allatu mūṉṟu vitamāṉa vaḷaiyalkaḷ.  ivaikaḷ kaṇukkaikku mēlē aṇiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷākum.  muttirikai mōtiram, naṭuvil nīṅkiya ēṉaiya viralkaḷil aṇiyappaṭum aṇiyum aṇikalaṉ.  utarapantam iṭuppil iḻuttuk kaṭṭum vār.  pattiram ilai vaṭivāṉa aṇikalaṉ itu vāril alaṅkāramākat toṅka viṭappaṭum.  ūrutāmam toṭaiyil toṅkaviṭappaṭum kayiṟu allatu kuñcam.  itu āṭaiyiṉmītu oru pakkattilāvatu allatu irupakkaṅkaḷil toṭai varai toṅkaviṭappaṭum.  aṇikalaṉ pāta jālam - pātasaram)
caṉṉa vīrapiralampam - yakñōpavītam -hāram - kiraivēyakam, nākayañña cūttiram - ratṉa pantam - upakirīvam - nitāvali ivaikaḷāl aṇiceyyappaṭṭiruppatākum.  (caṉṉavīra piralampam - ūrtuvakāyattil hāram mutaliyavaikaḷ pakkaṅkaḷil toṅkum kuñcam mutaliyavaikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya kēyūram (tōḷvaḷai kaṭakam pattira pūrimam mutaliyavaikaḷ.  ivaikaḷiṉ naṭuvil toṅkum mālaikaḷ - ivaikaḷukkuc caṉṉavīrapiralampam eṉṟu peyar - māṉasāram) yakñōpa vītam - pūṇūl hāram kaḻuttiliruntu mārpukkuk kīḻ varai toṅkum muttumālai mutaliyaṉa.  kiraivēyakam - kaḻuttiliruntu mārpukku mēlē toṅkum kācumālai, kārai, aṭṭikai pōṉṟa uḷ kaḻuttaṇikaḷ.  nārayañña cūttiram paṭattuṭaṉ kūṭiya pāmpu vaṭivamuḷḷa pūṇūl.  ratnapanta mupakirīvam - maṇikaḷ patitta uḷ kaḻuttaṇikaḷ.  nitalāvaḷi ivaikaḷ kaḻuttiliruntu 2 aṅkulam varaiyil oṉṟōṭoṉṟu iṇaintu iruppavaikaḷām.1
āṭaikaḷ
aṇikalaṉkaḷaip pōṉṟu teyvat tiruuruvaṅkaḷukku avaṟṟiṟku uriya āṭaikaḷum puṉaintē uruvaṅkaḷaic ciṟpikaḷ amaikkiṉṟārkaḷ.  ākamaṅkaḷil ovvoru teyva uruvaṅ kaḷukkum iṉṉiṉṉa āṭaikaḷ iṉṉiṉṉa aṇikalaṉkaḷ, iṉṉiṉṉa āyutaṅkaḷ irukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟatu.  teyva uruvaṅkaḷ puṉaintuḷḷa āṭaikaḷum, āyutaṅkaḷum, aṇikalaṉkaḷum ataṉ toṉmaiyāṉa tōṟṟam ataṉ taṉmai mutaliyavaikaḷai kuṟippiṭattakkatāy kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.
mikamikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattil tōṉṟiya tāyt teyvam āṭaikaḷiṉṟi, nirvāṇiyāy, pala aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyum, āyutaṅkaḷaiyum uṭaiyaṉavāyp payaṅkara vaṭivil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟāḷ.  tamiḻarkaḷiṉ paḻam perun teyvamākiya koṟṟavai erumaiyai vākaṉamāka uṭaiyavaḷ. kaikaḷil palavitamāṉa āyutaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiyavaḷ, karuppu niṟam uṭaiyavaḷ evvita āṭaiyumaṟṟavaḷ eṉṟu ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
civaperumāṉ pulittōlai āṭaiyākavum yāṉait tōlaip pōrvaiyākavum uṭaiyavar māṉ, maḻu, pōṉṟa āyutaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiyavar.   pāmpai aṇikalaṉākak koṇṭavar.  talaiyil erukkampūvai aṇintavar.  māṭṭai vākaṉamākak koṇṭavar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
murukaṉ, kuḻantai vaṭiviṉaṉ, mayilai vākaṉamākavum vēlai āyutamākavum koṇṭavar.  ciṟiya kaupīṉattai āṭaiyākak koṇṭavar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
tirumāl, karuṭaṉai vākaṉamākak koṇṭavar.  caṅku, cakkaram tāṅkiyavar.  pītāmparattai āṭaiyākak koṇṭavar.  māṭu, āṭukaḷaip mēyppavar.  kuḻal ūtupavar eṉṟellām kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.1 
ōviyaṅkaḷ
icai, nāṭṭiyam, kaṭṭiṭakkalai ciṟpam mutaliya ellāk kalaikaḷukkum toṉmai vāynta naṉmaiyāṉa kalai ōviyak kalaiyākum.  namatu vari vaṭivakkalaikku aṭippaṭaiyāy iruntatu ōviyakkalaiyēyākum.  ōviyam eṉpataip paṇṭaiyat tamiḻar eḻuttu eṉṟē kūṟiṉar.  intiyāvilē eḻunta kalaikaḷil mutaṉmai peṟṟatu ōviyakkalaiyēyākum.
pimpaṭkā ōviyam
intiyāvil uḷḷa maiya mānilattil (mattiyap piratēcattil) kōcaṅkapāttiṟkum pōpālukkum iṭaiyēyuḷḷa pimpaṭkāvil kuṉṟukaḷil uḷḷa kukaikaḷil pala paḻaṅkāla ōviyaṅkaḷ kaṇṭu
piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  iṅkuḷḷa kukaikaḷil maiya araciṉ tol
poruḷ āyvuttuṟaiyiṉar ceṉṟu kukaikaḷil nūṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa ōviyaṅkaḷ pala kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  camīpattil iṅku 60,000-m āṇṭukaḷukku vāḻnta maṉitaṉiṉ maṇṭaiyōṭum kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
itu paṇpu aṭippaṭaiyil vakuttu uṇarappaṭum  oru arumperum ūḻippirivai uruvākkum ōr ariya ceyti eṉṟu ulakiṟku uṇarttap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  intak kukaikaḷil oru ciṟappāṉa nalla cūḻaliṉ kāraṇamāka varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟkum muṟpaṭṭu paḻaṅkaṟkālattil makkaḷiṉ iṭaiviṭāta varicai muṟaiyāṉa ceyal kūṟukaḷiṉ nilaimāṟātirukkum paṇpāṭu paravalāka nilaviyuḷḷatu.  atu iṉṟum ataṉ talai poṉṟātu nilai kuṉṟātu iruntu varukiṟatu.  ippoḻutu aṅku naṭaipeṟṟa akaḻ āyvil varalāṟṟu kālattiṟku muntiya paḻaṅkāla intiyāviṉ niṟaivāṉa vāḻkkaip paṭattai mīṇṭum iṇaittu uruvākkuvataṟku utavum eṉṟu akaḻ āyvut tuṟai vallunarkaḷ nampukiṉṟaṉar.
iṅku kaṇṭa kukai ōviyaṅkaḷ pala nūṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟum ataṉ aḻaku kuṉṟātu ataṉ putumai tiṟampātu ataṉ polivu māṟātu putuppaṭam pōl tikaḻkiṉṟatu.  patiṉāyirak kaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ tirāviṭa makkaḷ yāṉaiyaiyum maṭṭumalla kutiraiyaiyum aṟintavarkaḷ ataippaḻakki ōṭṭavum kajacātiram acuvacātiram maṭṭumalla ōviyakkalaiyaiyum uṇarntavarkaḷ eṉpatai meyppikkiṉṟatu.  itaṉāl eṉṉāl eḻutap peṟṟu accēṟavirukkum tamiḻnāṭṭu ōviyam eṉṟa nūlil virivāka eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ.
patiṉāyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa tirāviṭa makkaḷ tīṭṭiya maiya mānilattil (mattiyap piratēcattil uḷḷa irāmkār malait toṭarkaḷiluḷḷa cōkimārā kukaikaḷilum karṉāṭaka mānilattil uḷḷa cittal turkkattiṟku aṇmaiyil uḷḷa aṅkālamat eṉṉum periya kukaiyil orupuli ōviyam kaivaṇṇam peṟāta karaṭu muraṭāṉa kaiyāl kariyāl varaiyap paṭṭatu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
vaṇṇaccimalai ōviyam
tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattil kaṭaiya nallūrukku aṇmaiyil uḷḷa vaṇṇaccimalai eṉṟa tirumalaik kōyilil pāṇṭiyar kālattu ki.pi. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tīṭṭappeṟṟa ōviyaṅkaḷ cila kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  kōyiliṉ uḷ vitāṉattil kāṇappaṭum teṟkuc cuvaril kāṇappaṭum cuvar ōviyaṅkaḷ oṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭil pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil aracakaṭṭilil amarntirunta avaṉiya cēkaraṉ cirivallapaṉ (ki.pi. 880-862) kālattil tīṭṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
intak kōyilil tīṭṭappeṟṟa cuvar ōviyam iṉṟu maṅki maṟaintu viṭṭatu.  inta ōviyattai mutaṉmutalākak kaṇṭupiṭittavar putuccēriyil irunta pirañcup pērāciriyar cōviyōtuprayal 1935-ām āṇṭil kaṇṭupiṭittu ulakiṟku uṇarttiṉār.  ivaratu vēṇṭukōḷiṉ mītu tamiḻnāṭṭut tol poruḷ kāṭcicālai iyakkunar ivaikaḷai paṭiyeṭuttuḷḷaṉar.
tirumalai purattuk kōyil ōviyam aṇṇal vāyil aṟivaṉ kōyil ōviyattiṟku oppāṉatu eṉṟu colvataṟkillai eṉṟālum ciṟappuṭaiyatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  pāṇṭiya nāṭṭu paḻaiya ōviyaṅkaḷil itu oṉṟē taramāṉatu eṉṟu kūṟak kūṭiyatu atōṭu itu caiva neṟiyiṉar tīṭṭiya ōviyamākum.
iṅku kāṇappaṭum ōviyaṅkaḷil āṭum arampaiyiṉ aḻakiya uruvam oṉṟum  mattaḷam taṭṭum maṅkaiyiṉ maṅkiya uruvam oṉṟum cevviyatāyk kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.  ikkōyilil kāṇappaṭum eḻil ōviyaṅkaḷil itutāṉ ēṟṟam peṟṟa eḻil ōviyam eṉṟu kūṟattakkatu.  maṟṟoru ōviyam tāṭiyuṭaiya muṉivar uruvamum ataṉ arukēyuḷḷa cila āṇpeṇ uruvaṅkaḷumākum. ovvoru āṇ uruvaṅkaḷukkum aṇmaiyil oru aṇaṅkiṉ uruvam amaintuḷḷatu.  oruvēḷai itu āṇum peṇṇum kalantu āṭum oruvakai naṭamāka iruppiṉum irukkalām.
ataṟkaṭuttāṟpōl kāṇappaṭuvatu aḻakiya vēlaip pāṭukaḷuṭaṉ aṇipeṟat tikaḻum pūṅkoṭikaḷiṉ kaviṉpeṟum ōviyam.
maṟṟoru ōviyam oru vāttiṉ ōviyam muṉṉōkkic ceṉṟu koṇṭirunta ētō oru oliyaik kēṭṭu piṉṉē tirumpip pārkkiṟatu.  ataṉ arukē cila malarkaḷum arumpukaḷum uḷḷaṉa.  maṟṟoṉṟu ciṅkattiṉ mītu amarntirukkum oru peṇ ōviyamākum.  itu koṟṟavai uruvāka irukkalām.  pala ōviyaṅkaḷ urut teriyāmal maṅki maṟaintu pōyviṭṭaṉa.
tirupparaṅkuṉṟattu ōviyam
pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ talainakarākiya maturaikku aṇmaiyil  tirupparaṅkuṉṟattil pukaḻpeṟṟa murukaṉ kōyil uḷḷatu.  iṅku vicittiramāṉa cittirakūṭam oṉṟuḷḷatu.  paripāṭalil kuṉṟam pūtaṉār eṉṉum pulavar inta ōviyat tirukkūṭattaip piṉvarumāṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟār:
....................................... niṉkuṉṟattu
 eḻu toḻil ampalaṅ kāmavēḷ ampiṉ
 toḻil vīṟṟirunta nakar 		- pari:18:27-29
intap pāṭalukku uraikaṇṭa pērāciriyar, niṉ tirupparaṅ kuṉṟattiṉ kaṇ eḻutiya eḻilvāynta eḻuttu ampalam (tirukkōyil) irukkiṉṟatu.  atu kaṇṭārkku mēṉmēlum perum viruppattait taravallatu.  maṉmataṉukkuriya ampukaḷ vaḷarttalāl atu kāmavēḷiṉ malar ampukaḷ niṟaintuḷḷa āyutac cālaiyai yokkum eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  malarkaḷiṉ nāṟṟam avaṟṟiṉ toḻilai makkaḷ uḷḷattil pukuntu ācaiyai vaḷarppatākum. niṉ kōyililum taṉṉaik kaṇṭārkku attakaiya ācaiyai eḻuppukiṟatu eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟār.
tirupparaṅkuṉṟattil naṭaipeṟum viḻākkālattu makkaḷ āṇum peṇṇum celvar.  aṅku cellum makkaḷ tirupparaṅkuṉṟattiluḷḷa pāṟaikaḷukkiṭaiyē tīṭṭappaṭṭa eḻuttu ampalattaiyum kaṇṭukaḷippar.  inta cittiracālai eḻuttunilai maṇṭapam eḻutoḻil ampalam eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭatu.
iṅku turuva cakkaram - atāvatu vāṉil uḷḷa oru ōviya vaṭṭam ōviya vaṭṭamākat tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  atu acuviṉi paraṇi mutaliya irupattēḻu kōḷkaḷaiyum vēṟupala kōḷkaḷai yum aṭaṅkiyuḷḷa vaṭṭam.  inta oḷi vaṭṭattiṉai ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ mutaliya kirakaṅkaḷ cuṟṟi varuvatu eṭuttukkāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
aṭutta ōviyam kāmaṉum iratiyum kūṭikkulāvi niṟkum ōviyam:-
iṅku akalikaiyiṉ cāpam eṉṟa purāṇak katai ōviyamākat tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  intiraṉ kāmaveṟikoṇṭu muṉivarai ēmāṟṟi avar maṉaiviyiṉ kaṟpaic cūṟaiyāṭi taṉperumaiyai iḻantu pūṉaiyāka ōṭuvatum kaṇkaḷil kaṉal paṟakkum nilaiyil kautama muṉivaṉ niṟkum kāṭciyum, kāmavēṭṭaikkup paliyāṉa akalikai kallākac capikkappaṭṭa kāṭciyum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  itu kātalarkaḷ uḷḷattai urukkak kūṭiya ōviyam ittakaiya ōviyam pala iruntaṉa eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  inta eḻuttu nilaimaṇṭapam tirupparaṅkuṉṟattil eṅkum kāṇappaṭavillai. itu maṟaikkappaṭṭu viṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar.  āṉāl iruntataṟkup pala cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
maturai nāyakkar kāla ōviyam
ki.pi. 17-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil maturaip pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ iḷavaracar iruvarkaḷil aracakaṭṭil yārukku uriyatu eṉṟa caṇṭai eḻavē kulacēkara pāṇṭiyaṉ paṭṭattaraciyiṉ makaṉukku utaviyāka vanta ṭilli cultāṉiṉ paṭait talaivaṉ mālikkapūr eṉpāṉ aracu kaṭṭilai aṭaiya muyaṉṟa irupāṇṭiya iḷavaracar
kaḷai viraṭṭiviṭṭu maturaiyilum paḻaiya kāyalilumuḷḷa poṉṉaiyum maṇiyaiyum ēṉaiya vilai uyarnta poruḷ
kaḷaiyum koḷḷaiyaṭittu ṭilli cultāṉukku aṉuppiviṭṭu mālikkapūr araca kaṭṭilil ēṟi maturaic cultāṉāka āṭci purintāṉ.  tamiḻ nāṭṭil muculim āṭci naṭappatu tirāviṭa makkaḷukkē avamāṉam eṉṟu vicayanāyakka maṉṉarkaḷ paṭai
yeṭuttu vantu cultāṉkaḷ āṭcikku muṭivu kaṭṭiviṭṭu pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ kaṭṭilil nāyakkarkaḷ amarntu koṇṭaṉar.
nāyakkarkaḷ āṭciyum aṉṉiyar āṭciyē eṉṟālum avarkaḷ kōyilkaḷaip paḻutu pārttaṉar.  putukkōyilkaḷaik kaṭṭiṉar.  ki.pi. 17-ām āṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil maturai aṅkayaṟ kaṇṇi (mīṉāṭci) kōyil ūñcal maṇṭapattiṉ vitāṉattil tēviyiṉ aṭṭatikku vijayattiṉ mīṉāṭci cuntarēcuvarar tirumaṇak kāṭciyaiyum ōviyamākat tīṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.  āṉāl ataṟkumuṉ tamiḻnāṭṭuc ciṟpikaḷ kallil tirumaṇak kāṭciyai aḻakiya ciṟpamākac cetukkiyuḷḷaṉar.
inta nāyakkar maṉṉarkaḷ kālattil tīṭṭiya mīṉāṭci cuntarar tirumaṇak kāṭci nal ōviyamāka uḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu.  ittirumaṇak kāṭciyai nāyakkar kula araci maṅkammāḷ oru puṟamāka niṉṟu kaṇṭukaḷippatu pōl tīṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  araci maṅkammāḷ mati muka maṭantai pōla tikaḻkiṉṟāḷ. avaḷ ōviyattiṉ mēṟpuṟattil tamiḻ teluṅku varivaṭivil makārāca māṉiya makārāca rāca ciri maṅkammā eṉṟu tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  avaḷ aṇmaiyil avaḷ pēraṉ niṉṟu koṇṭirukkiṟāṉ.  avaṉ ciṟuvaṉāka iruppatāl avaṉatu pērāḷākavē (piratinitiyākavē) maṅkaḷ āṭci purintu vantāḷ.  avaṉ eṭṭu vayatuc ciṟuvaṉāka irukkum poḻutē muṭicūṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷāṉ.  avaṉ peyar makarācarāca kiri vijaya raṅka cokkanātar  mīṉāṭci tēvikkup piṉṉāl vēṟu oru ciṟuvaṉ niṟkiṟāṉ avaṉ peyar taḷavāy irāma cuppaiyā.
nāyakkar kālattil mīṉāṭci kōyilil varaiyappaṭṭa ōviyam avvaḷavu taramāṉataṉṟu eṉṟālum putuccēriyiluḷḷa pirañcu aṟiñar paṭampiṭittu oru aḻakiya nūlāka veḷi yiṭṭuḷḷār.1
aṇṇal vāyil aṟivaṉ kōyil ōviyam
putukkōṭṭaikku aṇmaiyil uḷḷa kuḷattūr vaṭṭārattil uḷḷa aṉṉavācal. aṉṉavācalil  iruntu oru kal tolaivil aṇṇal vāyil aṟivaṉ kōyil kukai ōviyam uḷḷatu.  itu ciṟu kuṉṟaik kuṭaintu camaṇa muṉivarkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa kuṭaivaraik kōyil.  ikkōyil muḻuvatum neyvaṇṇa ōviyam tīṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  itu intiyāviṉ oppaṟṟa ōviyak karuvūlamāka matikkappaṭukiṟatu.
intak kuṭaivaraik kōyilil muṉṉar oru ciṟu muka maṇṭapamum piṉṉar tiru uṇṇāḻikaiyum uḷḷatu.  ki.pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil caivantaḻuviya makēntiravarmaṉāl viraṭṭiyaṭikkap paṭṭa camaṇarkaḷāl intak kukaik kōyil tōṇṭappaṭṭu appāl ōviyam tīṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷatu.  appāl ikkōyil paḻutupaṭṭatum apaṉīpa cēkaraṉ cīvallapaṉ eṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ (ki.pi. 830-862) utavi peṟṟu mukamaṇṭapam ceppaṉiṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu. kōyiliṉ aṇmaiyil uḷḷa pāṇṭiyaṉ kalveṭṭu itai uṟutippaṭuttukiṟatu.
ikkukaik kōyiliṉ mukamaṇṭapattil uḷḷa tūṇkaḷil iṭatupakkamuḷḷa tūṇil irupeṇkaḷ nāṭṭiyamāṭum nilaiyil neyvaṇṇa ōviyam uḷḷaṉa.  valatu pakkattuttūṇi luḷḷa oru kaṉṉi kārmēkam pōṉṟa karuṅkūntalaiyuṭaiyavaḷāy aṇikalaṉ
kaḷai aṇintu malarcūṭi āṭuvataṟku atam piṭittu niṟkiṉṟāḷ. ippeṇkaḷ aṭikaḷmār eṉa aḻaikkap paṭṭaṉar.  valatu puṟattiluḷḷa tūṇiṉ uṭpuṟam pāṇṭiyaṉum avaṉ maṉaiviyum oṉṟāka iṇaintu niṟkum eḻil ōviyam ēṟṟamuṭaṉ tikaḻkiṟatu.  muka maṇṭapattiṉ kūrai ōviyam ciṟappuṭaṉ tikaḻkiṟatu.
mukamaṇṭapattil camaṇamuṉivarkaḷiṉ puṭaiciṟpam (pārcuvanātar uruvam) tavak kōlattil iruppatupōl uḷḷatu itaṉ nīḷam 2 aṭi akalam 1 ½   aṭi.
tiruuṇṇāḻikai 10 ½  aṭi nīḷamum 10 ½   akalamum uḷḷatu.  itaṉ vāyil 5 aṭi 7 aṅkula uyaramum 2 ½   akalamum uḷḷatu. cuvaril camaṇa muṉivarkaḷiṉ puṭai ciṟpam uḷḷatu.
intak kōyiliṉ kūraiyil kāṇappaṭum tāmaraip poykaiyiṉ ōviyattaik kātikā pūmi atāvatu camaṇarkaḷ tuṟakkam (mōṭcam) eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  intat tāmarait taṭākam eḻil mika vāyntatu.  intiyāvil ciṟanta ōviyak karuvūlamāka matikkappaṭum ajantāviṟku oppāṉatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
tiruuṇṇāḻikaiyil uḷḷa 3 puṭaiciṟpaṅkaḷum kūrai mītu tīṭṭappaṭṭa acumāṉa kiri virippu eṉṟa aḻakiya ōviyamum kaviṉpeṟu vaṇṇamum vaṉappum vāyntaṉa. 1300 āṇṭu ceṉṟum palavitat tākkutalukku āḷākiyum putumaip polivu kuṉṟātu kempīramākak kāṭci aḷikkiṟatu.
peruvuṭaiyār kōyil ōviyam
tañcāvūr peruvuṭaiyār kōyil tiruuṇṇāḻikaiyaic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa pirakārattil muṟkālattil  cuntarar cēramāṉ kayilāca yāttirai ōviyamākat tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  itaṉ mītu nāyakka maṉṉar kālattil veḷḷaic cuṇṇam tīṭṭappaṭṭatu.  appāl āṇṭutōṟum cuṇṇam tīṭṭappaṭṭu kaṉatta takaṭu pōlāy viṭṭatu. oru nāḷ atil oru pakuti porintu kīḻē viḻuntukiṭantatu. atai aṇṇāmalaip palkalaik kaḻakap pērāciriyar kaṇṭu eḷitāka varum pakutikaḷai peyarttup pārkkavē uḷḷirukkum cōḻar kāla ōviyam kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu ippoḻutu veḷḷaiyaṭikkap
paṭṭa pakutikaḷ pāḷam pāḷamāka eṭukkappaṭṭu mūla ōviyam kiṭṭattaṭṭa muḻu uruvamākak kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
I.  kayilaiyil civaperumāṉ vīṟṟirukkiṟār avaraic cuṟṟi pūta kaṇaṅkaḷ cūḻntu niṟkum kāṭci arukē nanti kālkaḷai maṭakki amarntirukkiṟatu aṇmaiyil nilaivaṇṇattil muṉivarkaḷ amarntuḷḷaṉar.
II. cuntarar tirumaṇam civaperumāṉ kiḻavētiyar uruvil tōṉṟi ivaṉ eṉakku aṭimai.  ivaṉatu pāṭṭaṉ eḻutit tantirukkiṟa ōlaip pattirattaik kāṭṭutal.
III. kuṭiyāṉavarkaḷ illaṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam. makaḷir maṭpāṉaiyil cōṟu camaikkiṉṟārkaḷ paṇiyāḷ oruvaṉ aṇmaiyil pāṅkuṭaṉ ceṉṟu uṇavu peṟukiṟāṉ.
IV. cuntarar veḷḷaiyāṉai mītu civaperumāṉ āṇaippaṭi kayilāca yāttirai cellal. avarukku muṉpāka cēramāṉ perumāḷ veḷḷaik kutirai mītu cellutal pōṉṟa aḻakiya kāṭci.
V. tēvarkaḷum, teyvalōkap peṇkaḷum malartūvi muracu muḻakki āṭippāṭi cuntararaiyum cēramāṉ perumāḷaiyum varavēṟkum kāṭci.
VI. muṉivarkaḷ viṇṇil kār mēkaṅkaḷiṉ mītu niṉṟu viruntiṉarāṉa cuntararaiyum cēramāṉ perumāḷaiyum pūmāri corintu varavēṟṟal.
VII. ampalak kūttaṉ amarntuḷḷa kayilāca malaikkuc ceṉṟu avaratu pātaṅkaḷaiyum iruvarum aṭaintu eṉṟum poṉṟā iṉpattai aṭaital.
VIII. civaperumāṉ muppuram erittal,
itupōṉṟa aḻakiya vaṇṇaṅkaḷ poṟitta ōviyaṅkaḷ cōḻarkaḷiṉ ōviyap pulamaikku eṭuttukkāṭṭākat tikaḻkiṉṟatu.  ivaikaḷ aḻakumikkaṉa. viyattaku peṟṟi vāyntaṉa.
kāñci kailācanātar kōyil
pallavarkaḷ ki.pi. nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭil cōḻa nāṭṭil pukuntaṉar aṅkirunta kaḷamarai viraṭṭiyaṭittu kāñcipurattait talainakarākak koṇṭu pallava aracaṉ tam āṭciyai kantavarmaṉ āṭciyai niṟuviṉāṉ.  ki.mu. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkam varai kāñci camaṇa kāñciyākat tikaḻntu ki.pi. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭu toṭakkakālattiliruntu atāvatu makēntiravarmaṉ kālattiliruntu (ki.pi. 600-630) caivak kāñciyāka eḻuntatu.  makēntiravarmaṉ kālattilum avaṉukkup piṉ vanta māmallaṉ naracimmavarmaṉ kālattilum caiva camaya eḻuccikkup perumpaṇi tuvakkappaṭṭatu pala kaṟṟaḷikaḷ eḻuppappeṟṟeṉa ciṟpaṅkaḷum ōviyaṅkaḷum ērāḷamāka eḻuntaṉa.  pallava maṉṉaṉ irācacimmaṉ (665-705)il kailācanātar kōyilaik kaṟṟaḷiyāka  eṭuppittu cuvarkaḷiṉ mītu pala oppaṟṟa ōviyaṅkaḷait tīṭṭiṉāṉ. avaṉ kālattil tīṭṭappeṟṟa ōviyaṅkaḷ iṉṟu maṅki maṟaintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.  uruppaṭiyāṉa pallavar kāla ōviyam oṉṟu kūṭa kāñciyil namakkuk kiṭaikkavillai.  kāñci kailācanātar kōyilil (irāca cimmēcuvarattil) eñci niṟkum araikuṟaivāṉa ōviyaṅkaḷ cilatāṉ namakkuk kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.  iṅku kiṭaittiruppavai oru ciṟanta ōviyattiṉ ciṟu pakutiyēyākum.  eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka ōr  iḷaṅkāḷaiyiṉ mukattil pātitāṉ pulappaṭukiṟatu.  ataṉ aḷavu cumār iraṇṭu aṅkulam irukkum.  inta ōviyattiṉ eḻil kuṉṟi vaṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ oḷi poṉṟi cila karuppuk kōṭukaḷ tāṉ pulappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  itai nām oru puṉaiyā ōviyam eṉṟu kūṟalām.  inta ōviyam aḻiyātu iruntāl pallavar kālattil tīṭṭappaṭṭa ōviyac ciṟappu muḻuvataiyum nām naṉku cuvaittu ulakiṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyirukka muṭiyum.  tamiḻakattiṉ tavak kuṟaivu pallavarkaḷ kālattil tīṭṭiya ōviyattiṉ muḻucciṟappaiyum nammāl kaṇṭukaḷikka muṭiyāmaṟ pōyviṭṭatu eṉṟu collalām.  pallava maṉṉarkaḷ civa neṟiyait taḻuviyataṉ mēl tamiḻk kalaikaḷ maṟumalarcci peṟṟu eṅkum eḻuntaṉa.  avarkaḷ kaṭṭiṭak kalaikku āṟṟiya toṇṭu ulakap pukaḻpeṟṟuḷḷatu.  māmallapurattil eṭuppitta oṟṟaik kal tērkaḷum kāñci kaṟṟaḷikaḷum āṅkāṅkē uruvākkiya ciṟpaṅkaḷum ciṟappuṟṟavaikaḷ.  pallava maṉṉaṉ icaik kalaiyiṉ vaḷarccikkē aṭippaṭaiyiṭṭu viṭṭāṉ.  avaṉ makkaḷum pērarkaḷum atai vaḷarttuḷḷaṉar.
paṉaimalai ōviyam
pallavarkaḷ tīṭṭiya paṉaimalai ōviyam araikuṟai yākattāṉ namakkuk kiṭaittuḷḷatu eṉṟālum ataṉ ciṟappai ōraḷavu cuvaikkak kūṭiyatāka irukkiṟatu.
paṉaimalai teṉ ārkkāṭu māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa viḻuppurat tiṉiṉṟu ceñcikkuc cellum vaḻiyil, 16-āvatu kal tolaivil irukkiṟatu.  itu aṉantapurattiṉ aṇmaiyil oru malai aṭivārattil paṉaimalai eṉṉum ciṟṟūr uḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
paṉaimalaiyil pallavarkaḷāl eṭuppikkappaṭṭa pāṅkāṉa kōyil oṉṟu ciṟappuṟṟu eḻuntu niṟkiṉṟatu. ippoḻutu ikkōyilai intiya maiya araciṉ tolporuḷ āyvut tuṟaiyiṉar oru paḻam perum ciṉṉamāka eṇṇi taṅkaḷ celavil pātukāttu varukiṉṟaṉar.
tamiḻaka araciṉar kōyilkaḷāl tamiḻarkaḷ keṭṭup pōyviṭṭaṉar.  eṉavē tamiḻarkaḷiṉ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku kōyilkaḷ muṭṭukkaṭṭaiyāka irukkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiya ī.ve.irāmacāmip periyār kaiyil akappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  ivarkaḷ uṇmaiyil periyāriṉ uṇmaiyāṉa cīṭarkaḷē āṉāl makkaḷiṉ vākkukaḷaip peṟṟu āṭci pīṭattil amarvataṟkāka oṉṟē kulamum oruvaṉē tēvaṉum eṉṟa pāṭṭaip pāṭik koḷvar.  tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kōyilkaḷaip pēṇuvatilō, ciṟpaṅkaḷaip pātukāppatilō paḻaiya ōviyakkalaiyaic cemmai peṟa vaippatilō kaṭukaḷavum akkaṟai kiṭaiyātu.  āṉāl avaṟṟai maiya aracu eṭuttup pātukākka muṟpaṭṭāl itu tamiḻaṉ cottu; itait tamiḻaka araciṭam oppuvittuviṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu ōyātu kūppāṭu pōṭuvar.
ki.pi. 700-ām āṇṭil iraṇṭām naracimmavarmaṉ paṉaimalai tāḷakirīcuvarar kōyilai eṭuppittāṉ.  itaṉ vimāṉam eḻilvāyntatu.  itu cama catura tiritaḷam vimāṉam eṉṟa ciṟappup peyarāl aḻaikkappaṭukiṟatu. caturavaṭivāṉa 3 māṭaṅ kaḷaik koṇṭa ōr civaṉ kōyilil aḻakiya ōviyattait tīṭṭac ceytāṉ.  piṉ vantavarkaḷ ataṉmītu cuṇṇantīṭṭi maṟaittu viṭṭaṉar.  atirṣṭavacamāka mēlē tīṭṭiya cuṇṇampoṟintu viḻuntu  uḷḷē irukkum ōviyam naṉku teriyat toṭaṅkiyatu uḷḷirukkum pārvati tēviyiṉ mārpiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa pakuti teḷivākat terikiṟatu.  pārvatiyiṉ maṇimakuṭamum kaḻuttil uḷḷa maṇimālaiyum kātukaḷil oḷirum kuṇṭalamum kuḻaiyum eḻil peṟṟilaṅkukiṟatu. pārvati tēvi valatu kālai ūṉṟi iṭatu kālai maṭakki mika oyyāramāka niṟkum kāṭciyum iṭaiyil uḷḷa āṭaiyum tōḷmītu tuvaḷum muntāṉaiyum kaiyil uḷḷa kuṭaiyum kuṭattiṉ mītu māṇpuṟat tikaḻkiṉṟatu. nīlavaṇṇattil kuṭaiyum kaṟuppu niṟa meṉkōṭṭāl makuṭamum iḷa mañcaḷ vaṇṇattāl mukamum iḷañcivappu niṟattāl mēṉiyum civappum mañcaḷum kalanta vaṇṇattil mēṭaiyum viyattaku muṟaiyil aṟputamāka kalaiñaṉ inta ōviyattai aḻaku peṟat tīṭṭi yuḷḷāṉ.
āṭavallāṉiṉ tāṇṭavattai pārvati tēvi kaṇṭu kaḷittut taṉṉai maṟantu niṟpatu pōl kāṇappaṭukiṟāḷ. avaḷatu kaṇkaḷaip pārttālē itaṉ uṇmai naṉku terikiṟatu.  intak kaviṉ peṟum ōviyam ki.pi. 2-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tīṭṭap peṟṟa amarāvati ciṟpattaiyum ki.pi. 5-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tīṭṭappeṟṟa acantā ōviyattaiyum ottatākat terikiṟatu.  pallava maṉṉarkaḷ tīṭṭiya ōviyaṅkaḷil itu oṉṟutāṉ uruppaṭiyāka namakkuk kiṭaittuḷḷatu eṉṟālum muḻu uruvamum namakkuk kiṭait tuḷḷatu eṉṟu colvataṟkillai.  paṉaimalaiyil pallavar kaḷāl pāṅkuṟattīṭṭappaṭṭa inta ōviyam 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil uḷḷatu eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.
paṉaimalaik kōyiliṉ kīḻpuṟattil uḷḷa aṟaiyil ciṟpam ōviyam mutaliya kalaikaḷil vallavarkaḷāṉa pallavarkaḷ pārvati tēviyiṉ tiruuruvam kailācanātar kōyilaic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa ciṟumaṇṭapaṅkaḷil tīṭṭiya ōviyac ciṉṉaṅkaḷum āyirattu irunūṟu āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ tamiḻnāṭṭu ōviyaṉ kaiyāṇṭa variyiṉ meṉmaikaḷaiyum aḻakaiyum naṉku eṭuttukkāṭṭu kiṉṟaṉa.  pallavarkālattu ōviyaṅkaḷil vaṇṇaṅkaḷ evvaḷavu maṅki maṟaintuviṭṭa pōtilum amutu oḻukuvatu pōl oḻukum variyiṉ uyarvu māmaṇṭūrt tāmarai malar kailācanātar kōyilil uḷḷa pāti mukaṅkaḷ maṭṭum teriyum pala uruvaṅkaḷum paṉaimalaip pārvati tēviyiṉ aṟaikuṟai uruvam ākiyavai nam uḷḷattai īrppatāka uḷḷaṉa.
eṉṟu paṉaimalaip pārvati tēviyiṉ ōviyattaiyum pala pallavarkaḷ tīṭṭiya ōviyaṅkaḷaiyum paḻampāṇṭiyar tīṭṭiya vaṇṇaccimalai kuṭaivaraik kōyil ōviyattaiyum kaṇṭu avaṟṟiṉ uṇmaiyāṉa tarattai nēril pārttum pala aṟiñarkaḷ aḷitta cāṉṟaiyum koṇṭu ivai oppilāta ōviyam eṉṟu tīrppaḷittār, nalla ōviyappulamaiyum caṭṭa aṟivum mikka namatu matippiṟkuriya nītipati uyartiru mē.cu. irāmacāmi avarkaḷ, inta ōviyaṅkaḷaippaṟṟik kūṟiyavaikaḷai āḻntu paṭittavarkaḷ nāmum orumuṟai vaṇṇaccimalai, kāñci, paṉaimalai varai ceṉṟu pārttu varuvōmā eṉṟa eṇṇattai eḻuppum.1
cēra nāṭṭu ōviyam
ēṟattāḻa ki.pi. 9 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil kaṉṉiyākumari māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa nantikkaraiyil uḷḷa civaṉ kōyilil uḷḷa ōviyaṅkaḷ aṟputamāṉavaikaḷ.  iṉṟu anta ōviyaṅkaḷ pāti terintum pāti teriyāmalum irukkum maṉita uruvaṅkaḷ malarkaḷ mēkat tiraikaḷ ivaṟṟiṉūṭēyē kaviṉuṟat tikaḻum kampīramāṉa mukamum naṉku terikiṟatu.  mukattai oru puṟam cāyttu mukkāl tōṟṟattil tīṭṭap peṟṟirukkum inta ōviyam tiṭṭavaṭṭamākac civaperumāṉ uruvam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  itu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭup pāṅkil paṇpuṟat tīṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷatu.  teṉ tiruvāṅkūril uḷḷa kuṭai varaik kōyiliṉ uṭpuṟac cuvaṟṟiṉ mītu tīṭṭap peṟṟa ōviyak karuvūlam kālaveḷḷattāl maṅkiviṭṭatu.  kēraḷam, paṇṭaiya muttamiḻ nāṭāṉa cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil oṉṟākat tikaḻntatākum.
iṉṟaiyat tiruvāṅkūr maṉṉariṉ talainakaramāyirunta tiruvaṉantapurattil uḷḷa patmanāpacāmi kōyilil 14-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tīṭṭappaṭṭa cuvar ōviyam oṉṟuḷḷatu.  itu ōviyak kalaimītu mikappaṟṟuḷḷa tiruvāṅkūr aracar ātittiyavarmaṉ carvaṅkanātaṉ ki.pi. 137- 1375-il kaṭṭappeṟṟa kiruṣṇaṉ kōyil eṉṉum uṭpuṟak kōyilil tīṭṭap peṟṟa oṇṭoṭikaḷiṉ oyyāra ōviyam.  inta oviyattil makaḷirkaḷ palvēṟu vitamāṉa āṭaikaḷaiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyum aṇin
tuḷḷārkaḷ.  ip peṇkaḷ palvēṟu icaik karuvikaḷai mīṭṭik koṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ.  inta vaṇṇa ōviyaṅkaḷ kaṇkavar vaṉappu vāyntavaikaḷ.
vaṭa tiruvāṅkūril uḷḷa eṭṭamaṉūr civaṉ kōyilil uḷḷa kōpurattil kāṇappaṭum ōviyaṅkaḷ.  ivaikaḷ ki.pi. 16 āmnūṟṟāṇṭil tīṭṭappaṭṭavaikaḷākum.  ṭākṭar āṉantak
kumāracāmi avarkaḷ intiyak kalaikku oru muṉṉurai eṉṟa nūlil itu tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ vaṇṇa ōviyak kalaikku oru paḻaiya eṭuttuk kāṭṭākum eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  itu 12 aṭi nīḷamum 3 aṭi uyaramum uḷḷatu.  iṅkuḷḷa inta vaṭṭak kōyilai paḻutu pārkkum poḻutu aṭittaḷattil ki.pi. 1542 kuṭamuḻakku viḻāc ceyyum poḻutu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu.
1539-ām āṇṭil vaikkam civaṉkōyil tiruuṇṇāḻikaic cuvarkaḷil tīṭṭiya ōviyaṅkaḷ cila maṅkiya nilaiyil kaṇṭu
piṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  iḵtaṉṟi tiruvāṅkūriṉ paḻaiya talai
nakarāṉa patmanāpapuram araṇmaṉaiyil 17,18 -ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tīṭṭiya cila ōviyaṅkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  17 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil cittirāḷ kuṉṟilum ki.pi. 1725  kiruṣṇapuram araṇmaṉaiyil kacēntiramōṭcam eṉṉum ōviyamum 17-ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa kālattil cēranāṭṭil patmanāpapuram araṇmaṉaiyil piḷḷaiyār piḷḷaiyār pūcai mutaliya ōviyaṅkaḷum cātāviṉ ōviyamum uḷḷaṉa.  tiruccakkarapurattil 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ kiruṣṇaṉum avaṉatu peṟṟōrkaḷum, tirumakaḷ pārvati ōviyamum pārvatiyum civaṉum niṟkum nilaiyil uḷḷa ōviyamum 18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil mūlakkuḷattil piḷḷaiyār uruva ōviyamum tirukkōtittāṉattil tirumāl āticēṭaṉ mītu cayaṉittirukkum nilaiyil 16 - 18-ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil tīṭṭap peṟṟa oviyamum paṇaiyaṅkāvil tamayanti ōviyamumāka paṇṭaiyac cēranāṭṭil 15 cuvar ōviyaṅkaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.  iḵtaṉṟi kocci aracariṉ araṇmaṉaiyil pala cuvar ōviyaṅkaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.
paṇṭaiyak kālattil cōḻanāṭu, pāṇṭiya toṇṭai nāṭukaḷ pōl cēra nāṭṭil ciṟpak kalai vaḷarāviṭṭāl piṟkālattil piriṭṭiṣ āṭcikkālattil araca kuṭumpattaic cērnta irāca ravivarmā mēṉāṭṭup pāṇiyil ciṟpakkalai payiṉṟu tirumakaḷ, kalaimakaḷ, tamayanti, kaṇapati, pōṉṟa kaṇkavar ōviyaṅkaḷait tīṭṭi intiyāvil imayan toṭṭu kumarivaraip pukaḻ peṟṟu ovvoru vīṭṭilum avar tīṭṭiya paṭam iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.  ōviya ulakil irācaravivarmā maṅkāta pukaḻaip peṟṟuviṭṭār.
1947-kkuppiṉ vaṅkattiṉ kavi tākūriṉāl intiya ōviya ulakil oru maṟumalarcci eḻuntatu.  apēṉēntiratākūr mutaliya ōviya viṟpaṉṉarkaḷ palar vaṅkattil eḻuntu irācaravivarmā ittāliyap pāṇiyil ōviyantīṭṭi namatu paḻaiya ōviya marapai viṭṭu vaḻi tavaṟiviṭṭār eṉṟu kuṟai nām ajantā ōviyamarapil iṉi ōviyantīṭṭa vēṇṭum.  cutantira intiyā ōviyattu tuṟaiyil ayalnāṭṭaip pārttu ōviyantīṭṭum aṭimaip puttiyai viṭṭuviṭavēṇṭum.  intiyā kaṭṭiṭakkalai, ciṟpakkalai, ōviyakkalai icaikkalai, kaṇitakkalai, vāṉanūṟkalai mutaliya ellāk kalaikkum tāyakam eṉṟu ulaka aṟiñarkaḷ pōṟṟivarum poḻutu nām irācāravi varmaṉ vaḻiyilittāliya ōviyak kalaiyaip piṉpaṟṟuvatu aṭimaipputtiyai vaḷarppatākum eṉṟaṉar.  cutantira intiyāvil avarkaḷatu vārttaikkup peritum matippu aḷikkap peṟṟu iṉṟu intiya ōviyakkalai paḻaiya marapu vaḻiyil muṉṉēṟi varukiṟatu.

13. kalaimakaḷ tiṅkaḷ veḷiyīṭu (ceṉṉai)

kōyil vaḷartta kalaikaḷ
potuvākak kūṟuvatāṉāl tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kalaikaḷ aṉaittum, kaṭṭiṭakkalai, ciṟpakkalai, paṭimakkalai, ōviyakkalai, maṭakkalai aṇikalaṉkaḷiṉ mutaliya pala kalaikaḷaiyum vaḷarttatu namatu kōyilkaḷē eṉṟālum ciṟappāka namatu kōyilkaḷ icai, icaik karuvikaḷ, nāṭakam, nāṭṭiyam mutaliyavaikaḷaik kaṇṇeṉak karuti vaḷarttu vantuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu karuttu ulakil uḷḷa ellā camayattiṉarum, eṅkaḷ camayattiṉarum eṅkaḷ camayamum icaiyaik kaṇṇeṉak karuti vantuḷḷatu eṉṟu colvārkaḷ.  ulakam atai oppātu.  oppiṉāl nāṉ eṅkaḷ tamiḻar neṟi caivam, vaiṇavam ākiya iraṇṭumē icaiyaik kaṇṇiṉ karumaṇiyākak koṇṭu eṅkaḷ kōyilkaḷ icaiyai icaik karuvikaḷai, nāṭakattai nāṭṭiyattaip pēṇi vaḷarttu vantuḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟuvēṉ.
mutalil eṅkaḷ camayam icaiyai eṅkaḷ kaṭavuḷāy eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyirukkiṟatu.  nāṉ cila anniya camayattāriṭam eṅkaḷ kaṭavuḷ aṉpu eṉṟu collappaṭṭirukkiṟatu eṉṟu, 
aṉpum civamum iraṇṭeṉpar aṟivilār
aṉpē civamāva tārum aṟikilār
aṉpē civamāva tārum aṟinta piṉ
aṉpē civamāy amarntiruppārē
eṅkaḷiṉ tamiḻ ākamamākiya tirumantiram colliyirukkiṟatu eṉṟu coṉṉēṉ.  ataṟku oru camayi  coṉṉa eṉatu camayamum tēvaṉ aṉpākavē irukkiṟār eṉṟu eṅkaḷ vētam kūṟukiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.  eṅkaḷ vētattilum āṇṭavaṉ aḷavaṟṟa aruḷāḷaṉum nikaraṟṟa aṉpuṭaiyōṉumāka irukkiṟāṉ eṉṟu eṅkaḷ puṉita neṟi kūṟukiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟi eṉatu vāyai aṭaittuviṭṭatāka eṇṇic cirittaṉar.  nāṉum teyvam nammītu aṉpuṭaiyatāka irukkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṟīrkaḷ eṉṟēṉ;  ām eṉṟaṉar.   nāṉ kaṭavuḷ makkaḷ mītu aṉpu celuttukiṟavar eṉṟa poruḷil collavillai kaṭavuḷē aṉpu vaṭivil irukkiṟār eṉṟu coṉṉēṉ.  viḷakkamāka āṅkilattil T¿nd‹.”There is no ! God but Love” eṉṟa poruḷil eṅkaḷ ākamam kūṟukiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉēṉ.  avar
kaḷum eṅkaḷ camayamum atē karuttiltāṉ kūṟukiṟatu eṉṟaṉar.  nāṉ kūṟum kaṭavuḷ vēṟu, nīṅkaḷ kūṟum kaṭavuḷ vēṟu eṉpatu eṉatu karuttalla;  camaya aṟivarkaḷ iṟaivaṉaik kaṇṭu uṇarnta poruḷil tāṉ nām vēṟṟumai kāṇkiṉṟōm eṉṟu kūṟi civaṉ el allā ekōvā eṉṟu kūṟum coṟkaḷ vēṟāṉālum oruvaraittāṉ vevvēṟu peyarkaḷil kūṟappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟu camaya orumaippāṭṭiṟku vantōm.
atēpōl tamiḻar neṟiyākiya caiva neṟiyai maṟumalarcci ceyyat tōṉṟiya camaya kuravaril oruvarākiya cuntaramūrtti nāyaṉār tam kaṭavuḷākiya civaperumāṉai 
ēḻicaiyāy icaippayaṉāy iṉṉamutāy 	(7-51-10)
eṉṟu tam tēvārat tiruppatikattil pōṟṟip pāṭiyuḷḷār.  mēlum, 
ēḻicaiyēḻ narampiṉ ōcaiyai 		(7-83-6)
pāṭṭakattu icaiyāki niṉṟāṉai		(7-62-3)
eṉṟum āḷuṭai nampi eṉṉum cuntarar tam tēvārat tiruppatikaṅ kaḷil iṟaivaṉ icaiyāy irukkiṉṟāṉ eṉṉum uṇmaiyai eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
namatu cevikaḷukku iṉpantantu uṇarvūṭṭum nātattai ōcai eṉpar.  anta uṇarvuṭaṉ naṟporuḷai aṟivuṟuttum muṟaiyil makkaḷ uḷḷattiṟpatiyum cevippulaṉām aṇuttiraḷai oliyeṉavum pakuttuḷḷaṉar.  iṟaivaṉ ōcai oli eṉum iruvakai nuṭpaṅkaḷāka piriviṉṟi iyaintu niṉṟu ellā ulakaṅkaḷai iyakki varukiṉṟāṉ itaṉai,
ōcai oli yelām āṉāy nīyē
	ulakuk koruvaṉāy niṉṟāy nīyē
eṉṟa tiruttāṇṭakam iṉitu uṇarttukiṉṟatu.  mēlum icaiyākiya iṟaivaṉ makkaḷ uḷḷattai urukki iṉpa aṉpiṉai perukkum muṟaiyil icaiyuruvāka viḷaṅki tacaikaḷukkup palamūṭṭi nalam nalkukiṉṟāṉ.  icaikku ēṟpuṭaittāy viḷaṅkuvatu tamiḻ eṉṟum iṟaivaṉ paṇṇār iṉtamiḻākavum pāṭṭakattu icaiyākavum viḷaṅki iṉpam nalkukiṉṟāṉ eṉṟum pāṭṭil paṇṇāka iyainta iṟaivaṉ taṉṉai icaiyāl paṇintēttum aṉpar
kaḷukkut tōṉṟāt tuṇaiyāy vantu aruḷ pālikkiṉṟāṉ eṉṟum tēvāra āciriyarkaḷākiya namatu camaya kuravarkaḷ pala iṭaṅkaḷil namakku eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.
paḻattiṉiṟ cuvaiyum kaṇṇiṭai maṇiyum pōṉṟu icai vaḷarkku uṟutuṇaiyāy paṇṇiṟ kalantu icaikkuc cuvaiyum ēṟṟamum aḷikkiṉṟāṉ.  itaṉai
paṇṇiṉārp pāṭalākip paḻattiṉil iratamāki
kaṇṇiṉāṟ pār vaiyāki
eṉṟu appar aṭikaḷum 
paṇṇiṭait tamiḻoppāy paḻattiṉiṟ cuvaiyoppāy
kaṇṇiṭai maṇiyoppāy			(7-229-6)
eṉṟu cuntara mūrtti nāyaṉārum iṟaivaṉai icaikka uvamittup pōṟṟuvatu iṉitu viḷaṅkum.  iṟai paṇṇākavum paṇṇakattē iṉiya ōcaiyai eḻuppum tamiḻākavum tikaḻkiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu, 
paṇṇumpata mēmumpala vōcaittami ḻavaiyum
uṇṇiṉṟa tor cuvaiyumuṟu tāḷat tolipalavum
maṇṇumpuṉa luyirumvaru kāṟṟuñ cuṭar mūṉṟum
viṇṇum muḻu tāṉāṉiṭam vīḻim miḻalaiyē	(1-11-4)
eṉṉāṉavaṉ icaiyāṉavaṉ
iyalum icaiyāṉai				(1-16-6)

paṇṇiṉ ōcai paḻattiṉil iṉcuvai		(5-47-8)

paṇṇavaṉ kāṇ paṇṇavaṟṟiṉ ṟiṟaṉāvāṉ kāṇ
(6-52-1)
muttamiḻum nāṉ maṟaiyum āṉāṉ kaṇṭāy	(6-23-9)
icaiyāy icaivaṭivāy viḷaṅkum iṟaivaṉai tamiḻiṉam icaiyālē paṇṭu toṭṭu vaḻipaṭṭu niṟkiṉṟaṉar.  iṟaivaṉatu pukaḻai virittōtum tōttiraṅkaḷākiya iṉiya pāṭalkaḷai tirukkōyilil nāḷ vaḻipāṭṭil iṟaivaṉukku tirumañcaṉamum naṟumalarum, tiru amutum tūpa tīpamum nāṉmaṟaimuḻakkamum iṉṟiyamaiyātatu
pōl iṉṉicaip pāṭalkaḷākiya nālvarkaḷiṉ tēvāra tiruvācakap pāṭalkaḷum iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉa vākum.  inta iṉiya icait tamiḻ pāṭalkaḷākiya tōttiraṅ kaḷait tāḷattōṭu yāḻ, vīṇai, muḻavu, montai, pañcamuka muracu, kuṭamuḻā, takai, taṇṇumai, cāraṅki, iṭakkai, timilai mutaliya icaik karuvikaḷōṭu aṭiyavarkaḷ pāṭum pāṭṭiṟku iṟaivaṉ noṭippoḻutu cuṇaṅkātu ceviyuṟṟu aruḷpurivāṉ eṉpatu tamiḻakac cāṉṟōr tiruvākku.
tamiḻiṉ nīrmaipēci tāṉam vīṇai paṇṇi nalla
muḻavam montai malku pāṭal ceykai yiṭam ōvār 	(1-73-8)
eṉa campantap piḷḷaiyārum 
yāḻiṉ pāṭṭaiyukanta aṭikaḷē		(5-41-5)
maṉattakattāṉ talaimēlāṉ vākkiṉuḷḷāṉ
vāyārat taṉṉaṭiyē pāṭun toṇṭar
iṉattakattāṉ					(6-7-5)
eṉṟu appar aṭikaḷ aruḷiya aruḷpākkaḷ pāṭuvōr aṉaivarukkum iṟaivaṉ aruḷ tiṟattai iṉitu pulappaṭuttum
 tiruveṇṇey nallūril cuntarar tirumaṇattil iṟaivaṉ oru mutiyavar vēṭattil vantu āruraṉē eṉakku uṉ kuṭi muḻutum aṭimai eṉa vātiṭṭu āvaṇam (pattiram) kāṭṭi ārūraṉai aṭimai
yākak koṇṭu, nī nammuṭaṉ vaṉmoḻi pēciṉai āṉatāl vaṉṟoṇṭaṉ eṉṉum peyaraip peṟṟāy - eṉavē nī aṉpiṉāl namakkuc ceyyattakka vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriya mantiram icai nalam vāynta tamiḻp pāṭalkaḷēyākum.  eṉavē ivvulakil iyalicait tamiḻppāṭalkaḷāl pōṟṟuvāyāka eṉṟār.  innikaḻcci, 
 maṟṟum nī vaṉmaipēci  vaṉṟoṇṭaṉ eṉṉum nāmam
peṟṟaṉai namakkum aṉpiṟ perukiya ciṟappiṉ mikka
arccaṉai pāṭṭēyākum ātalāl maṇmēl nammai
coṟṟamiḻ pāṭu eṉṟār tūmaṟai pāṭum vāyār	 (taṭut 70)
eṉat tiruntoṇṭar purāṇattil okkat teḷivākak kuṟippiṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu.
camayakuravarkaḷākiya appar campantar mutaliyavarkaḷ kālattil kōyilkaḷil vaḻipāṭṭuk kālattil tamiḻ mutaliṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.  iṟaivaṉatu aruḷtiṟam pōṟṟum paktip pāṭalkaḷākiya icait tamiḻp pāṭalkaḷ ciṟappiṭam peṟṟaṉa.  ivai kōvilkaḷukku varum makkaḷ uḷḷattai urukki paktiyaip perukkiyatu.  iṉiya icait tamiḻp pāṭalkaḷākiya tōttiraṅ kaḷai tāḷattōṭum yāḻ, vīṇai, cāraṅki, muḻavu, montai, iṭakkai, takkai, taṇṇumai, timilai mutaliya icaik karuvikaḷōṭum pāṭi vantaṉar.  icaippāṭalai icaik karuvikaḷōṭu pāṭuvatu iṟaivaṉukku ukantatu eṉṟu caiva nāyaṉmārkaḷum vaiṇava āḻvārkaḷum collip pōntaṉar.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka,
tamiḻiṉ nīrmai pēci tāḷam vīṇai paṇṇi nalla
muḻavum montai malku pāṭal ceykai yiṭam ōvār	
1-73-8
vantaṉai niṉṉicai pāṭuvār pāl maṉṉiṉār	       1-8-.5
eṉa campantarum
yāḻiṉ pāṭṭai yukanta aṭikaḷē
paṇṇārnta vīṇai payiṉṟavaṉē
kuḻal koṇṭum yāḻ koṇṭum
eṉṟu apparum,
kuḻal vīṇai koṭu koṭṭi
maṇimu ḻāmuḻak kaaruḷ ceyta
takkai taṇṇumai tāḷam vīṇai
takuṇic caṅkiṇai callari
kokkarai kuṭaimuḻavi ṉōṭicai
eṉṟu cuntararum,
iṉṉicai vīṇaiyar
eṉṟu māṇikkavācakarum pāṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ.  itaippōl āḻvārkaḷum, iṟaivaṉ icaik karuvikaḷōṭum pāṭuvatai kēṭṭu iṉpam nalkupavaṉ eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.
tamiḻ iṉamum, tamiḻ moḻiyum, tamiḻicaiyum 10 āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟivarukiṟārkaḷ.  ātiyicaikaḷ cumār 12,000 eṉpar tamiḻ icaip pulavarkaḷ.  nāppaṭaiyāl uraikkap paṭṭa patiṉōrāyirattu toḷḷāyirattu toṇaṇūṟṟoṉṟākiya āti icaikaḷ eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār cilappatikāra uraiyil kūṟi iruppataik koṇṭu paṇṭaiyat tamiḻar tam nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulattiṉ ciṟappāl kaṇṭa 1199-il tamiḻar aracu vīḻntamaiyālum tamiḻ icaip pulavarkaḷiṉ akkaṟai iṉmaiyālum icaiyaip pēṇātu aḻiyumpaṭi viṭṭuviṭṭaṉar.  tēvāra āciriyar kālattiṟku
muṉ 103 - paṇkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  avaikaḷilum palamaṟaintu viṭṭaṉa.  tēvāra āciriyar tam pāṭalkaḷukku pāṇarkaḷaik koṇṭu paṇ amaittaṉar.  ataṉāl appar, campantar kālattil 23  - tamiḻp paṇkaḷ uyirōṭu nilaviṉa eṉṟu tairiyamāka kūṟalām.  civaneṟi marutanilattil tōṉṟi taṉakkuriya yāḻaiyum muracaiyum 23 - tamiḻp paṇkaḷaiyum pāṭik kāttu vantatu pōl mullai nilattil tōṉṟiya tirumāl neṟi mullaiyāḻaiyum mullaip paṟaiyaiyum tiruvāy moḻikkāka vakukkappaṭṭa paṇkaḷaiyum pēṇip pātukāttu vantatu.  tiruvāy moḻippāṭalkaḷiṉ nūṟpatippiṉuḷḷē pāṭalukkuriya paṇṇum irākamum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  tiruvāy moḻiyil, tēvārattil uḷḷa 23 - paṇkaḷil 13 paṇkaḷ maṭṭum itil uḷḷaṉa.  iḵtiṉṟi tēvārattil kuṟippiṭāta 6 paṇkaḷ tiruvāy moḻiyil uḷḷaṉa. tēvāra āciriyarkaḷ mūlamum tiruvāy moḻi āciriyarkaḷ mūlamum nām 29 paṇkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷaik kāṇ
kiṟōm.  inta 29 paṇkaḷil 15 tamiḻp paṇkaḷai marapu vaḻuvātu pāṭa tamiḻ nāṭṭil āḷ uṇṭā eṉpatu aiyam.
tirunelvēli nellaiyappar- kāntimati ammaṉ kōyilil cāraṅkiyum, pāṇiyum uḷḷatu.  mīṭṭuvārillai.  cāmi caṉṉitiyilum, ammaṉ cannitiyilum icait tūṇkaḷ mīṭṭuvāriṉṟi niṉṟaṉa.  ematu aṉpiṟkum matippiṟkum uriya eṉatu uṟaviṉarum tōḻarumāṉa icaimaṇi tiru. kā. caṅkaraṉār atait taṭṭi curam pāṭac ceytu ṭēp rikkāṭum- icaip pativum ceytuḷḷār.  tirucci vāṉoli nilaiyam atai oli parappiyuḷḷatu.  palar ataik kēṭṭirukka
māṭṭārkaḷ.  viraivil tirunelvēli vāṉoli nilaiyam mīṇṭum pativu ceytu tirunelvēli, kaṉṉiyākumari, irāmanātapuram māvaṭṭa makkaḷ kēṭṭu iṉpuṟumāṟu viraivil ceyyum eṉṟu etirpārkkiṟēṉ.  nellaiyappar kōyilil pāṇi eṉṉum tōl karuviyum uḷḷatu.
tiruvaraṅkam kōyilil iṉṟum paḻaiya icaik karuvikaḷil oṉṟāṉa takkai irukkiṟatu.  vācikkappaṭṭum varukiṟatu.  takkai tēvāra āciriyarkaḷ kālattil iruntuvarum oru paḻaiya tōṟ karuviyākum.  itu iṭai curuṅkiya paṟai iṉattaic cērntatu.  takkai eṉṉum tōṟkaruviyai iṉṟuḷḷa pala icaip pulavarkaḷ kaṇṭum kēṭṭum aṟiyārkaḷ.
ikkaruvi ovvoru āṇṭilum tiruvaraṅkattil tirumāl kōyilil kārttikait tiṅkaḷil amāvācai kaḻitta 16 - ām nāḷil kavucik ēkātaciyil araiyar tiru neṭuntāṇṭavam cēvippār.  appoḻutu takkai tuṇaik karuviyāka vācikkap paṭum.  mārkaḻit tiṅkaḷil varum amāvācai kaḻitta 14  -ām nāḷ vaikuṇṭa ēkātaciyil araiyar tivviyap pirapantam  cēvittu āṭum poḻutu takkai tuṇaikkaruviyāka vācikkappaṭum.  iṅku tavaḷai mattaḷamum tuṇaik karuviyāka vācikkappaṭukiṟatu.  ottu muṭintatum patikat toṭakkattiṟku takkai vācikkappaṭum.  piṉ aṟuti vaittu muṭikkappaṭum.  appāl cutta mattaḷam takkai eṉa māṟi māṟi vācippatu marapu.  iṉṟu tiruvaraṅkattil cītārāmaṉ makaṉ kumāracāmi takkai vācittu varukiṟār.  vēṟu eṅkum takkai iruppatāka eṉakkut teriyavillai.
kuṭa muḻāvum pañcamuka muracum
 tamiḻ nāṭṭil pañca muka muracaip pārppatu aritu.  mottattil nāṭṭil nāṉkiṟku mēl kāṇpataritu.  itaṉai aimuka eḻiṉi eṉṟum mattaḷattai irumuka eḻiṉi eṉṟum puṣkarattai mummuka eḻiṉi eṉṟum kuṭamuḻāvai orumuka eḻiṉi eṉṟum kūṟuvar.  pañcamuka muracu tiruvārūrk kōyilum tiruttuṟaip pūṇṭi, tiruvāṉaikkā kōyilkaḷil uḷḷaṉa.  oru periya kutil pōṉṟa periya vaṭivil 5 nāḷi pōṉṟa mukattil tōl kaṭṭap peṟṟirukkum.  itaṉaip pañca muracu eṉpar.  itu muṉṉāḷil maṇṇāl ceyyappaṭṭatu.  ippoḻutu ulōkat takaṭukaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭu icaikkappaṭukiṟatu.  itaṟkut tuṇaik karuviyāka kuṭamuḻā vācikkappaṭukiṟatu.  itu cuṭu maṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu mēlē tōl pōrttappaṭṭirukkum. 
icaiyum icaik karuvikaḷum
eṉavē tamiḻ nāṭṭil uḷḷa kōyilkaḷil vaṭamoḻi mantiraṅkaḷōṭum tēvāram, tiruvācaka, tiruvāy moḻi mutaliya paktip pāṭalkaḷ pāṭappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.  caivak kōyilkaḷil tēvāram tamiḻ paṇkaḷōṭu pāṭuvataṟku ōtuvārkaḷ paṇṭu toṭṭu pāṭuppaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.  ōtuvārkaḷukku kōyiliruntu māṉiyaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.  palaviṭaṅkaḷil tēvāram pāṭacālai niṟuvi makkaḷukkup paṇṉōṭu pāṭa payiṟci aḷikkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.
iḵtaṉṟi ellāk kōyilkaḷilum nākacuram, mēḷam, ottu ūtutal tāḷak karuvikaḷ mīṭṭal tavaṟātu naṭaipeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa.
tirunelvēli nellaiyappar - kāntimati ammaṉ kōyilil tēvāram ōtavum, cāraṅki eṉṉum icaikkaruviyai mīṭṭavum, vaṭamoḻi ākamaṅkaḷai ōtavum nellaiyil icaimaṇi, cā. cuntara ōtuvāriṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ kāñciyiliruntu aḻaittu varappaṭṭaṉar.  avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ vaṭamoḻi ākamaṅkaḷum ōtap payiṉṟiruntatāl avarai ākama caivar eṉṟu avarkaḷukkup paṭṭayam aḷikkap peṟṟiruntatu.  nellai tiru. cuntara ōtuvā mūrttikaḷiṉ tantaiyār cālivāṭīcuvara ōtuvāmūrtti avarkaḷ tēvāram maṭṭum ōtum ōtuvārāka iṉṟi icaiyum tamiḻp pulamaiyum peṟṟavarkaḷāvar.  cumār 100 - āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar tirunelvēli muttamiḻc caṅkattil talaimaip pulavarāyum iruntārkaḷ.  tirucālivāṭīcuvaramūrtti avarkaḷum avarkaḷ makaṉ cuntara ōtuvā mūrtti avarkaḷum tirunelvēli nellaiyappar - kāntimati kōyilil paramparaiyāyt tēvāram pāṭi māṉiyam peṟṟu vanta ōtuvā mūrttikaḷiṉ kuṭumpattaic cērntavarkaḷ āvar.  avarkaḷ kuṭumpattaic cērntavarkaḷ ampā camuttiram, kaṭaiyam mutaliya ūrkaḷil ciṟanta muṟaiyil tēvārattaip paṇṇōṭu pāṭum ōtuvārkaḷākavum cāraṅki mīṭṭum icaippulavarkaḷākavum iruntu vantaṉar.  ippoḻutum āṅkāṅkē avarkaḷ kuṭumpattil palar iruntu varukiṉṟaṉar.  āṉāl kōyilil tēvāram ōtum toḻilaip palar viṭṭu viṭṭaṉar.  āṉālum cuntara ōtuvāmūrttikaḷiṉ pēttimārkaḷ intirāvum, vaḷḷināyakiyum icaippayiṉṟu āṅkāṅkē tēvārak kaccēri ceytu varuvatu caiva camayapaktarkaḷ, ippeṇ makkaḷ taṅkaḷ muṉṉōrkaḷ kaṇṭa tēvāra iṉṉicai marapaip pātukāttu varuvatu kaṇṭumakiḻcci aṭaintu vāḻtti varukiṉṟārkaḷ.
pañca vāttiyam
kēraḷa nāṭṭil kōyilkaḷil naṭaipeṟum uṟcava kālaṅkaḷil pañca vāttiyaṅkaḷaip payaṉpaṭutti varukiṉṟārkaḷ.  pañca vāttiyam tēva vāttiyam eṉpar.  muṉṉāḷil timilai, iṭakkai, caṅku muraḷi (mukavīṇai) mirutaṅkam ākiyavai pañca vāttiyam eṉappaṭum.  paḻaṅkālattil pañca vāttiyattiṉ aṅkamākap pāṭṭum iruntuvantatu.  aimpatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ pañca vāttiyaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟai kēraḷa aṟiñarkaḷ māṟṟiyuḷḷaṉar.  mirutaṅkattiṟkup patilāka cutta mattaḷamum muraḷikkup patilākap pāṇṭi mēḷattiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttum kompum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa.  pañca vāttiyattil tiripuṭatāḷam, nāṉku kālamuḷḷa orupallavi  curampāṭuvatu ākiyavaṟṟai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu pañca vāttiyattiṉ tāḷakkaṭṭu ciṭṭaippaṭuttap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  tiripuṭatāḷattil nāṉku kālamāka pañcavāttiyam icaikkap
paṭṭatu.  mutal kālam 56 eḻuttukkaḷiṉ kālam, iraṇṭām kālam 23 eḻuttukkaḷiṉ kālam, mūṉṟām kālam 14 eḻuttukkaḷiṉ kālam, nāṉkām kālam 7 eḻuttukkaḷiṉ kālam eṉṟu muṟaip
paṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar.  iṟutiyil vāttiyam ēka tāḷattil muṭiyum.
pañca vāttiyattai tiripuṭatāḷattil maṭṭum vācikkappaṭa
vēṇṭumā?  eṉṟu tamiḻnāṭṭil oru viṉā eḻuntatu.  campai rūpakam mutaliya tāḷattilum vācikkalām.  kaḷippūṭṭum intak kūṭṭu vāttiyattai putu vaḻikaḷil vācittu putuvaḻikaḷaik kāṇpatil tavaṟillai pala tamiḻ icaip pulavarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
ippoḻutu vācikkum aintu vāttiyaṅkaḷil kūṭa cila māṟṟaṅkaḷ ceyyalām eṉac cilar karutukiṟārkaḷ.  pañca vāttiyaṅkaḷil kompum cuttamattaḷamum cērttatāl ācuramāka māṟiviṭṭatu.  cuttamattaḷattiṟkup patilāka mīṇṭum mattaḷam iṭam peṟalām.  cuttamattaḷattiṉ nuṉiyiṉ muḻakkattil timilai yiṉ kātukku  iṉiya takāramum tōṅkāramum iṭakkaiyiṉ iṉiya cila camaya cevikkup pulappaṭātu pōyviṭukiṉṟaṉa.  iṉimaiyum mirutuvumāṉa teyvat taṉmai vāynta pañca vāttiyattiṟku mirutaṅkam mikavum ēṟpuṭaittatu.
kompukkup patilāka nākacurattaip payaṉpaṭuttalām.  itil caṅku vēṇṭām.  atu curutikkāka maṭṭum irukkiṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇap
paṭukiṟatu.  atu cariyalla.  tiripuṭai, campai, rūpakam ākiyatāḷaṅ
kaḷil caṅkai vācippatu nallatu.  tiruppatikkōyil caṅkait tiṟamaiyuṭaṉ vācippatai palar aṟintu pārāṭṭi yuḷḷaṉar aṅku ceṉṟu nāmum ataik kaṟṟu varalām.  iṭakkai, timilai, mirutaṅkam, nātacuram , caṅku ākiyavaikaḷ koṇṭa aintu vāttiyaṅkaḷ iṇainta kūṭṭu vāttiyam ciṟantateṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu.
icaikkaruvikaḷ
tamiḻ nāṭṭil caṅka kālattil 1000ttiṟkumēṟpaṭṭa tōṟkaruvikaḷum nūṟṟiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa narampuk karuvikaḷum 50kkum mēṟpaṭṭa kuḻaṟkaruvikaḷum 15kkum mēṟpaṭṭa karuvikaḷum iruntaṉa.  cilappatikāra uraiyāciriyar aṭiyārkku nallār cumār 31 tōṟkaruvikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷaic coṉṉār.  aintu nilattiṟkum aintu narampuk karuvikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷaic coṉṉār.  eṭṭu kuḻalkaḷaiyum, 4 kañcak karuvikaḷaiyum cilar eṭuttukkāṭṭi yuḷḷaṉar.
icaik karuvikaḷ tamiḻakak kōyilkaḷil pūcaik kālattup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vantaṉa.  ivai ki.pi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉpē kōyilkaḷil iruntu vantaṉa.  itaṟku tēvāram tiruvācakam mutaliya nūṟkaḷ cāṉṟu pakarkiṉṟaṉa.  aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟum 31 - tōṟkaruvikaḷil illāta pala tōṟkaruvikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kūṭa tēvāraṅkaḷilum tiruvācakattilum piṟa nāṉku tirumuṟaikaḷilum uḷḷaṉa. kōyilpūcaikku aṣṭāṣa vāttiyaṅkaḷ 18 payaṉ
paṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  avai muṟaiyē 1. pērikai 2. tamarukam 3. tavil 4. tuntiṉā 5. tuntupi 6. kākalam 7. mattaḷam 8. maṭṭukkam 9. nupurā 10. pāṉakam 11. paṭakam 12. captam 13. tāḷam 14. tampuru 15. turiyam 
16. vēṇu 17. vīṇai 18. mirutaṅkam eṉappaṭum.  kōyil muracukaḷ 26.  iliṅkāyata camaya aṟiñarkaḷ vivēka cūṭāmaṇi eṉṉum nūlil pañcamakācaptam, ciriṅkā, vammalatā, caṅkapēri ceyakōcā eṉṟa olikaḷaiyuṭaiyatu eṉpar.  kōyil muracu 26 iṉ peyarkaḷ:  1. pañcamuka vāttiyam 2. nakarā 3. tamarukam 4. uṭukkai 5. tavaṇṭai 6. pampai 7. kaṉakatappaṭṭai 8. uṭal 9. periya uṭal 10. ciṉṉa uṭal 11. vīravaṇṭi 12. cūriyappiṟai, cantirappiṟai 13. timilai 
14. ceṇṭai 15. cutta mattaḷam 16. iṭakkai 17. cakki, 18. muṭṭu 19. takkā 20. vīramattaḷam  21. takki 22. ceya pērikai 23. ṭaṅkā 24. ṭaṅki 25. vīramallāri 26. tappup palakai eṉpaṉavākum.
tōṟkaruvikaḷ pala uḷa.  ivaikaḷai iṟaivaṉē icait
tāṉ eṉṟu tēvāra tiruvācakaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
narampuk karuvikaḷ
paṇṭaik kālat tamiḻarkaṇṭa narampuk karuvikaḷil yāḻ, vīṇai, kiṉṉaram, tumpuru, tampurā, kuḻalcaṅku, kañcakkaruvi pōṉṟavaikaḷ ciṟappāṉa karuvikaḷākum.  yāḻ vīṇai kuḻal pōṉṟa karuvikaḷ makkaḷai maṭṭumalla vilaṅkukaḷiṉ uḷḷattaiyum īrkkavallatu.  yāḻ teyvat taṉmai vāyntatu.  ataṉai aṟiñarkaḷ tīñcuvai nalkum teyva nal yāḻ eṉṟārkaḷ.  civa perumāṉē yāḻai mīṭṭi vantār.  vīṇaiyai icaittu vantār eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  appar aṭikaḷ 
aṟai kalanta mutal montai vīṇai yāḻum
paṇṇoṭu yāḻ vīṇai payiṉṟāyp pōṟṟi
ēḻicai yāḻvīṇaimuralak kaṇṭēṉ
paṇṇārnta vīṇai payiṉṟavaṉē   		(appar)
eṉṟu tiruppācurattālum iṟaivaṉ yāḻmuṟinātaṉ eṉṟum iṟaivi yāḻ moḻiyāḷ , yāḻār moḻimaṅkai eṉṟu iruttalālum iṟaivaṉ vīṇātara taṭcaṇā mūrttiyāyum, tirumāl eppoḻutum tam vāyil vēyṅkuḻalai vaittu ūtik koṇṭiruppatālum umātēvikku ēḻicai vallapi eṉṟa peyar iruttalālum kalaimakaḷ tam kaiyil vīṇaiyait tāṅki iruppatālum piḷḷaiyār kūṭa tam kaiyil vīṇaiyait tāṅki āṭik koṇṭiruppatālum namatu icaik karuvikaḷ teyvat taṉmai vāyntavaikaḷ eṉṟu aṟivatōṭu aṉṉiyar āṭciyi yālum namatu makkaḷiṉ aṟiviṉmaiyālum teyvīka nalyāḻ uḷpaṭa nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa icaikkaruvikaḷai iṉṟu iḻanta nilaiyil irukkiṉṟōm.  mīṇṭum namatu āṭciyil avaṟṟai yellām uyirppikka muyalvōmāka.
nāṭṭiyamum tāṇṭavamum
umātēvi āṭiṉāl atu vāciyam.  civaperumāṉ, āṭiṉār tāṇṭavam.  āṇ, vēṭamiṭṭu āṭuvatu nāṭṭiyam.  āṇ peṇ vēṭamiṭṭum peṇ āṇ vēṭamiṭṭu āṭuvatum niruttam muṟkālattē tamiḻakattil āṅkāṅkē kaṉṉi māṭam amaittu tirumaṇa vāḻkkaiyai virumpāta makaḷirum, iḷavayatil kaimaiyaṭainta mātarum, peṇṇum illātavāṟu aliyāy tōṉṟiya mātarum kaṉṉimāṭattil cērntu vāḻntuvantār.  aṅkuḷḷa kōyilum caiva makkaḷum anta kaṉṉimāṭattiṟku vēṇṭiya utavikaḷ ceytu vantaṉar.  kaṉṉimāṭam caiva maṅkaiyarkaḷ tavira camaṇa maṅkaiyarkaḷaiyō cākkiya camayap peṇmakkaḷaiyō cērttuk koḷḷātu.  teḷivākac coṉṉāl itu caivacamayak kaṉṉiyar maṭam eṉṟu kūṟalām.  ivvāṟu āṭavarkaḷukkum taṉiyē pala ūrkaḷil namatu tiruvāṭutuṟai tiruppaṉantāḻ, tarmapuram pōṉṟu pala maṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa.
kaṉṉiyar maṭattiluḷḷa peṇkaḷ civatīṭcai peṟṟavarkaḷ. atikālaiyil eḻuntu nīril mūḻki kōyilukku eṉṟu niṟuvappaṭṭuḷḷa nantavaṉattil picci mullai iruvācci campaṅki mutaliya malarkaḷaik koytu mālaiyākat toṭuppārkaḷ.  tirukkōyilai meḻukuvārkaḷ.  kōlam pōṭuvārkaḷ.  malar mālaikaḷai iṟaivaṉukkuc cūṭṭumpaṭi pūcārikaḷiṭam koṭuppārkaḷ.  iṟaivaṉmītu paktippāṭalkaḷaip pāṭuvārkaḷ.  iṟaivaṉ cannitāṉattiṉmuṉ āṭuvārkaḷ.  ivarkaḷ puṉitamāṉa vāḻvu naṭattum caiva makaḷir āvar.  ivarkaḷ mītu evarum evvita kuṟṟamum kūṟārkaḷ.  kūṟuvataṟku akkālattu caiva naṉmakkaḷō aracō ciṟitum iṭaṅkoṭātu avarkaḷiṉ tūymaiyāṉa naṭattaiyaiyum puṉita caiva ulakam pārāṭṭivantatu.
taṉakku maṇam pēci irunta oruvar pōril iṟantupaṭṭār eṉpatai aṟinta appar aṭikaḷiṉ akkaiyar tilakavatiyār taṉakkut tirumaṇam vēṇṭām eṉṟu maṇavāḻkkaiyait tuṟantu kaṉṉimāṭattil cērntu aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyum paṭṭāṭai kaḷaiyum pakaṭṭāṉa āṭaikaḷaiyum aṇiyātu eḷiya veṇmai yāṉa āṭaikaḷai aṇintuvantār.  avvammaiyārkku tāyum illai, tantaiyum illai.  tampi oruvar maruḷnīkkiyār maṭṭum uṇṭu.  avar iḷam paruvattilē iḷamaiyum yākkaiyum nilaiyātu eṉṟu eṇṇi nallaṟaṅkaḷ pala ceytu varumpoḻutu camaṇa camaya muṉivarkaḷāl pāṭaliputtiram eṉṟa ūril uḷḷa camaṇa maṭattiṟku aḻaittu avarai camaṇa camayattiṟku cērttu accamaya nūṟkaḷai naṉku payiṟṟuvittu taruma cēṉar eṉṉum ciṟappuppaṭṭamum aḷittaṉar.  tarumacēṉar eṉṟa maruḷnīkkiyār pautta camayattiṉaril orucārārākiya cērarkaḷai vātil veṉṟu camaṇa camayat talaivarkaḷil mēmpaṭṭavarāy uyarntu viḷaṅkiṉār.
tilakavatiyār tiruvatikai tiru vīraṭṭāṉattirukkōyilai aṭaintu civa ciṉṉaṅkaḷ aṇintu tampi maṉam māṟi civa camayattiṟku varumāṟu ceyya nāṭōṟum civaperumāṉai vēṇṭit toḻutu vantār.  tirukkōyilil tiruviḷakkiṭutal, cāṇattāl meḻukutal, malar koytu koṇṭu vantu mālaikaḷ toṭuttal mutaliya tiruppaṇikaḷai ceytu vantār.  taruma cēṉar cūlai nōyāl varunti akkaiyiṭam vantu cērntār. tilakavatiyār vatinta kaṉṉiyarmāṭattiṟku vantu tamakkaiyar kālil viḻuntu vaṇaṅkit taṉ nōyai nīkkumāṟu vēṇṭiṉār.  tilakavatiyār civaṉai niṉaintu ainteḻuttōti tirunīṟṟaik koṭuttaruḷiṉār.  viṭiyaṟkālaiyil tiruvatikai vīraṭṭāṉē cuvarar tirukkōyilukku aḻaittuc ceṉṟār.  iṟaivaṉai vīḻntu vaṇaṅkiṉār.  tamiḻ mālaikaḷākiya tiruppatikaṅkaḷaip pāṭum uṇarvu untiyeḻuntatu.  taṉṉaip pīṭitta cūlai nōy nīṅkiyatu.
ulakai viṭṭu kiṭṭat taṭṭa maṟaintu pōṉa caiva neṟiyai mutalil toṇṭai nāṭṭilum paravac ceyta tarumacēṉarai, tirunāvukkaracar eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ caiva camayat toṇṭarākkiya perumai oru vēḷāḷap peṇmaṇiyākiya tilakavatiyāraiyē cērum.  atēpōl pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil camaṇa camaya iruḷ niṟaintu kiṭanta poḻutu pāṇṭiya aracaṉai campantar mūlam cura nōkki caiva oḷi parappiya perumai maṅkaiyarkkaraciyāraiyē cērum.
tamiḻ nāṭṭup peṇkaḷ piṟanāṭṭup peṇkaḷai viṭa nallavarkaḷ uṟutiyāṉa camayap paṟṟuṭaiyavarkaḷ tūymaiyāṉa oḻukkamum puṉitamāṉa uḷḷamum peṟṟavarkaḷ. tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ turatiṣṭam namatu puṉita kaṉṉimaṭattai cila paṇṭāraṅkaḷum, pūcārip pullarkaḷum civa vēṭam tāṅki civa paktarkaḷiṉ iccaiyait tīrppatum civa tiruppaṇiyil oṉṟu eṉṟu ēmāṟṟi kaṉṉimāṭattil uḷḷa peṇkaḷai vañcittu avarkaḷukkup poṉṉum maṇiyum paṭṭāṭaiyum koṭuttu vañcittu kāntiyaṭikaḷ kūṟiyatu pōl vaḻukki viḻunta mātarkaḷākki - atāvatu potu makaḷirākki kaṉṉimāṭaṅkaḷai aḻittu viṭṭaṉar.  avarkaḷ tirukkōyilukkuc ceyyum paṇiviṭaiyāl tēvaṭiyāḷ eṉṟu peyar peṟṟaṉar.  tēvaṭiyāḷ eṉpatu teyvat toṇṭu ceyyum puṉitap peṇ eṉṟē poruḷpaṭum.  itaṉ ciṟappai aṟintē māmaṉṉaṉākiya irācarācacōḻaṉ maṉaivi mātēvaṭiyāḷ eṉṟum peyarait tāṅki iruntāḷ.  avaḷ pativiratai puṉitamāṉa paṭṭattaraci ērāḷamāṉa celvaṅkaḷai kōyilukku māṉiyamāka aḷittāḷ.  iṉṟu avaḷatu pēttiyō allatu avaḷ vaḻiyil vanta oru aracakulap peṇ ulakappukaḻ peṟṟa māperum maṉṉaṉ irācarācaṉ maṉaiviyiṉ ciṟanta peyarai vaikka nāṇumpaṭi caivap perumakkaḷiṉ tīcceyal tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ peyarai keṭuttuviṭṭatu.  caiva kaṉṉimāṭattil  uḷḷa peṇkaḷ evvaḷavō keṭṭaḻintu pōṉa kālattum avarkaḷ tamiḻicaikkum, nāṭṭiyakkalaikkum āṟṟiya toṇṭai tamiḻulakam maṟakka muṭiyātu.  ippeṇ maṇikaḷ nam camayattiṟku peruntoṇṭāṟṟi avarkaḷiṉ icaic ciṟappālum nāṭṭiyat tiṟamaiyālum iṉṟu kōṭi kōṭiyāy paṇantiraṭṭi ettaṉai makaḷir kallūrikaḷ, nāṭṭiyakkalācālai niṟuviyirukkamuṭiyum.  caiva camaya makkaḷ namatu kaṉṉimāṭap peṇkaḷiṉ kaṟpaic cūṟaiyāṭiyavarkaḷ maṭātipatikaḷāyiṉum, pūcārikaḷāyiṉum, tarumakarttākkaḷāyiṉum avarkaḷ ceyta maṉṉikkamuṭiyāta kuṟṟaṅkaḷukku kaḻuvāyt tēṭiyē tīravēṇṭum. tavaṟu namatu nāyaṉmārkaḷiṭattiliruntālum teyvattiṭam iruntālum inta irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ viññāṉa ulakam avarkaḷiṉ tīya ceyalaik kaṇṭu mauṉiyāy irātu maṉṉippum aḷikkātu.
namatu tirukkōyilkaḷai namatu makkaḷum, aracum kavaṉit
tiruntāl namatu icai aḻintirātu 103 paṇkaḷāvatu nilait
tirukkum.  1000 - icaik karuvikaḷāvatu kōyilil pātukāttu vaikkappaṭṭirukkum.  tāṇṭavamum lākiyamum kūttukaḷum vaḷarcci peṟṟirukkum.
5000 - m āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ ekiptum, mecapottāmiyā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum parmā, tāylāntu, kampōṭiyā, malēciyā, cāvā, pālittīvu, pōrṉiyō kirīṭ, īṭar tīvukaḷilellām paraviya civaneṟi namatu kaṉṉiyarkaḷum āṇkaḷāṉa cāmiyārkaḷum namatu kalaikaḷiṉ mūlam ulakam aṉaittum civaneṟiyait taḻuvi irukkumāṟu ceytiruppārkaḷ.  yūtarkaḷukku erucalēm pōṉṟu kiṟitavarkaḷukkup petlēkam pōṉṟu, mulimkaḷukku mekkā pōṉṟu civaṉai vaḻipaṭum caivarkaḷukku citamparamum, maturaiyum, tirunelvēliyum kāñciyum puṉitanakarkaḷāy kōṭāṉu kōṭi cevvantiyarkaḷum nīkrōvarkaḷum, maṅkōliyarkaḷum, airōppiya veḷḷaiyarkaḷum yūtarkaḷum piṟarum vantu kaḷikkum puṉita talamāka iruntirukkum.  oru āṇṭiṟku intiyāviṟku inta yāttirīkarkaḷāl 1000 kōṭi rūpāy vantu cērntu varum.
nantavaṉam
tirukkōyilkaḷil aṉṟāṭam naṭai pūcaikaḷukkum tiru uruvaṅkaḷukkum tiruviḷakkukaḷukkum mālaikaḷum caraṅkaḷum pantukaḷum viṭupūkkaḷum tēvaiyāy iruntaṉa.  puṭpa vitiyil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa malarkaḷ maṭṭuntāṉ kōyilkaḷil payaṉpaṭuttap
paṭavēṇṭum.  mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattiliruntu nantavaṉaṅkaḷ kōyil uḷḷēyum, veḷiyēyum iruntu vantaṉa.  niṟaivāṉa peruṅkōyilil kōyil kaṭṭum poḻutē tirumatilkaḷukku uṭpuṟam nantavaṉattiṟkeṉṟu taṉi iṭamum pūcceṭikaḷukku nīr pāyccuvataṟku kiṇaṟum teppamum uṇṭu paṇṇiyiruntaṉar.  nāyaṉmārkaḷil āṇkaḷ tirunāvukkaracar, campantar, cuntarar, māṇikkavācakar pōṉṟa camaya kuravarkaḷāka uyarpatavi vakitta nāyaṉmārkaḷum nantavaṉattiṟku nīrpāyccutal kōyilkaḷaic cuttam ceytal mutaliya tiruppaṇi viṭaikaḷait tavaṟātu ceytu vantaṉar.  tirunāvukkaracar eppoḻutum taṉatu kaiyil pul cetukkum karuviyait tāṅkiyiruntār.  uḻavārap paṭai kaiyiluṭaiyāṉ eṉṟu patiṉōṟān tirumuṟaiyil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  avar cellum kōyilkaḷil puṟkaḷaic cetukkum tiruppaṇiyait tavaṟātu ceytuvantār.  iṉṟu cuvāmi tericaṉam ceyya ½ parlāṅku tolaivu cella veḷḷip pallakkil cellum maṭātiparkaḷ itaic cintittu pārkkumpaṭi paṇivāyk kēṭṭuk koḷkiṟēṉ.  namatu camayattai vaḷartta nāyaṉmārkaḷiṉ tiruppaṇiyai niṉaintu avar vaḻiyil celvatē uṇmaiyāṉa paktarkaḷiṉ kaṭamaiyākum.  āṇ makaṉāṉa nāyaṉmārkaḷum paktarkaḷum nantavaṉattiṟku nīr iṟaittu pāycci vantaṉar.  kaṉṉimāṭattiluḷḷa makaḷir pūkkoytu mālaikaḷum pūccaraṅkaḷum pūcceṇṭumāka palavitamākap pūkkaḷait toṭuttu iṟaivaṉaiyaṭaiya tiruuruvaṅkaḷil cāṟṟumpaṭi ceytu vantuḷḷaṉar.  kaṭṭiṭakkalai, paṭimakkalai, ōviyakkalai pōṉṟa 64 kalaikaḷil pūttoṭuttalum oru kalai.  pūt toṭukkum kalaiyai kaṉṉimāṭattil vāḻum makaḷir naṉku kaṟṟiruntaṉar.  atōṭu āṭavum pāṭavum payiṉṟiruntaṉar.
nantavaṉaṅkaḷil pūcaikkutavāta malarkaḷ iṭam peṟātu.  pūcaikkuriya malarc ceṭikaḷai iṉavāriyāka naṭṭu nīrpāycci pūttapiṉ viṭiyaṟkālattilē avaikaḷaip paṟittut toṭuttu kōyilukkuc koṇṭu pōyc cērppar.  pūppaṟittu mālaikaḷ kaṭṭumpaṇi mikavum ciṟanta toṇṭākak karuti pala caiva vēḷāḷarp peṇkaḷ pakti mēlīṭṭāl taṅkaḷai it toṇṭiṟkut  arppaṇittuk koṇṭaṉar.  caiva camayattil civakāmiyāṇṭār eṉpavarum vaiṇavattil periyāḻvārum ippaṇiyil īṭupaṭṭa toṇṭar āvar.  caiva camayattil uḷḷa celvamikka  uyarkulap peṇkaḷ tirumaṇa vāḻkkaiyai veṟuttu  it teyvat tiruppaṇikku taṅkaḷai arppaṇit tuḷḷaṉar.  iṉṟu kattōlik kiṟitava camayattil uḷḷa kaṉṉiyarkaḷaip pōl caiva camayattilum kaṉṉimāṭaṅ
kaḷilum atil tiruppaṇi ceyyum peṇkaḷum ērāḷamāka iruntaṉar.  avarkaḷ eḷiya uṭaiyum, eḷiya naṭaiyum uṭaiyavarkaḷ.  taṅkaḷ celvam aṉaittaiyum nantavaṉaṅkaḷai niṟuvavum kiṇaṟukaḷait tōṇṭavum māṉiyamāka aḷittuviṭṭu piṟa ēḻai eḷiya toṇṭarkaḷaip pōl eḷiya uṭaiyum uṇavum peṟṟu teyvat tiruppaṇiyil īṭupaṭṭu vantaṉar.  nantavaṉam niṟuvavum kiṇaṟukaḷ tōṇṭavum maṉṉarkaḷum celvarkaḷum māṉiyaṅ
kaḷum aḷippatum uṇṭu.  pūkkaḷil kōṭṭuppū, koṭippū, nīrppū, nilappū eṉa nāṉku vakaiyākap pirikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa.  
kōyilkaḷil tiruvamutu ceyvataṟku nañcey, puñcey nilaṅkaḷait tāṉam ceytataṟku pala kalveṭṭukaḷum ceppēṭu kaḷum iṉṟu cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟaṉa.  inta nilaṅkaḷil viḷaiyum nellum pullum itara kūlap poruḷkaḷum naivēttiyattiṟkum tiruppaṇiyil īṭupaṭṭa ūḻiyarkaḷukku piracātamākavum vaḻaṅkap
paṭṭaṉa.  eñciyavaikaḷ mikak kuṟainta vilaiyil ēḻaikaḷukku viṟkappaṭṭaṉa.
kōyilkaḷukku maṉṉarkaḷum, celvarkaḷum māṉiyamāka ērāḷamāṉa nañcey, puñcey nilaṅkaḷai māṉiyamāka viṭṭatāl kōyilkaḷukku nellum ēṉaiya kūlap poruḷkaḷum vaṇṭi vaṇṭiyāy vantu iṟaṅkiṉa.  kūlap poruḷkaḷ malaipōl kuvintaṉa.  eṉavē kōyilkaḷukkeṉṟu kūlak kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.  maturaiyil uḷḷa tirumāliruñ cōlaiyil uḷḷa aḻakar kōyiliṉ kūlak kaḷañciyam kōyilai viṭap periyatu.  vaṭṭamāṉa vaṭivattai yuṭaiyatu.  aḻakāka amain tuḷḷatu.  ivvāṟē tañcāvūril uḷḷa pala kōyilkaḷiṉ kūlak kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ kōyilaiviṭap periyatu.  kōyilkaḷukku āṭu māṭukaḷum avaikaḷ mēyvataṟku nilaṅkaḷum nivantamāka viṭap
paṭṭiruntaṉa.  ataṉāl ney ērāḷamāka vantatu.  ney viḷak
kerikkavum kōyilil tōcai, vaṭai, poṅkal, puḷiyōtarai, carkkaraip poṅkal, laṭṭu mutaliyavaikaḷ  ceyyappaṭṭu makkaḷukkuk kuṟainta vilaiyil viṟkappaṭṭaṉa.  iṉṟum pala kōyilkaḷil cōṟum palakāraṅkaḷum kuṟainta vilaikku viṟkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.  vaiṇavak kōyilil iṉṟum palakāraṅ
kaḷum ciṟṟuṇṭikaḷum tārāḷamākak kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa.  
kalveṭṭukaḷum ceppēṭukaḷum
muṟkālattil namatu muṉṉōrkaḷ nāṭṭu varalāṟu eṉṟu taṉiyē nūl etuvum eḻutavillai.  āṉāl namatu varalāṟṟuc ceytikaḷ pala namatu tirukkōyilkaḷil kalveṭṭukaḷākavum ceppēṭukaḷākavum poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  namatu kōyilkaḷil nūṟāyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa kalveṭṭukaḷ iṉṟu kaṇṭu piṭikkap
paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  kalveṭṭukaḷ kōyil cuvarkaḷilum, paṭikkaṭṭukaḷilum, makkaḷ naṭantu cellum pātaikaḷilum veṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  ceppēṭukaḷ, ōlai ēṭukaḷ pōl cemput takaṭṭāl ceyyap
paṭṭu atil araca muttiraikaḷum patikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  cērar, cōḻar, pāṇṭiyar, pallavar ākiya tamiḻnāṭṭu maṉṉarkaḷ kōyilkaḷukku aḷitta nivantaṅkaḷai ceppēṭukaḷil eḻuti aracarkaḷ muttiraiyum, eḻutiyavar peyarum, cāṭcikaḷum oppamiṭṭu, iṉṟu kāṇappaṭum.  āvaṇakkaḷariyil pativu ceyyappaṭṭa pattiram pōl aracu pativu (Register) ceytu kōyilkaḷil ceppēṭukaḷil pātukāttu vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
aracukaḷiṉ māṟṟattiṉālum, kālak koṭumaiyālum makkaḷiṉ aṟiyāmaiyālum, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa kalveṭṭukaḷum, ceppēṭukaḷum kavaṉiyātu maṟaintu pōyviṭṭaṉa.  āṅkila āṭci nilaviyapoḻutu, aracu taṉiyāka, kalveṭṭiyal (Epigraphy Deapartment) eṉṟu oru taṉit tuṟai niṟuviṉar.  tol eḻuttiyal (Paleography) eṉṟa oru āyvut tuṟaiyai amaittu palvēṟu varivaṭivaṅkaḷil eḻutap peṟṟirukkum eḻuttukkaḷaip paṭikka mutaṉ mutalāka āṅkila aṟiñar puḵlar, intiyat tol eḻuttiyal (Indian Paleograpy) eṉṟa oru nūlai eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār.  ataṟku intiyā avarukku naṉṟi pārāṭṭak kaṭamaippaṭṭuḷḷatu.  intat tuṟai nīṇṭakālamāka āṅkila aṟiñar talaimaiyil nīlakiriyil iyaṅki vantatu.  appāl intiyar talaimaiyil maicūril iyaṅki varukiṟatu.  cumār 20,000 - ttiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa kalveṭṭukaḷ paṭikkappaṭṭu, pala nūl vaṭivam peṟṟu veḷi vantuḷḷaṉa.  intiyā urimai eytiyapiṉ ilaṭcattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa kal veṭṭukaḷum paṭikkappaṭṭu nūlākkappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum.  intiya aracō tamiḻaka aracō pōtiya akkaṟai kāṭṭavillai.  ittuṟai āmai vēkattil metuvāka iyaṅkivarukiṟatu.  āṉāl maṟaintuviṭavillai.  ittuṟaiyai namatu aracu naṉku ceyalpaṭac ceytiruntāl nūṟāyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa cācaṉaṅkaḷum ceppēṭu
kaḷum paṭikkappaṭṭu namatu nāṭṭiṉ iruṇṭa varalāṟṟiṉmītu putiya oḷi ūṭṭap peṟṟirukkum.  cintu veḷi akaḻ āyvum muttiraikaḷum, ulakilē aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ, tirāviṭa makkaḷum, civaṉeṟiyum, uḻavar, paravar, kuravar pōṉṟa makkaḷiṉ aracum, nākarikamum ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyatai eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyatu pōl, teṉintiyak kal veṭṭukaḷ tamiḻar, kēraḷar, āntirar, kaṉṉaṭar oppaṟṟa nākarikattaik kāṇat tuṇaiyāka iruntirukkum.
tamiḻakattil aracakaṭṭilil amarnta teṉ intiya nala urimaic caṅkamō, kāṅkira āṭciyō ti.mu.ka aracō taṅkaḷ pataviyaik kāppāṟṟik koḷḷa muyaṉṟatē yoḻiya ittakaiya nalla tuṟaiyil nāṭṭam celuttavillai.
tolleḻuttiyal
toṉmaiyāṉa kālattil eḻutappeṟṟatu.  ataṉai āyum muṟai tol eḻuttiyal eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.  tol eḻuttu eṉpatu iṉṟu vaḻakkil iruntu varum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ muntaiya vaṭivaṅkaḷum, vaḻakkoḻinta ataṉ paḻaṅkāla eḻuttukkaḷiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷum ām. tol eḻuttukkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ vaḷarcciyum āyvu ceyyap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil mēṉāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷ tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷiṉ toṉmaiyaiyum ataṉ vaḷarcciyaiyum nuṇṇuṇarvuṭaṉ āyntu ataṉai aṉaittulaka iyalāka uyarttiyuḷḷaṉar.  
makkaḷiṉ aṟivu vaḷarccikku aṭikōliyatu eḻuttu kālattaiyum, nāṭṭaiyum, nākarikattaiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvatu eḻuttu.  paṇṭaik kālattil enta nāṭṭil enta iṉa makkaḷ eḻuttaik kaṇṭārkaḷō avarkaḷtāṉ nākarika makkaḷ eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟārkaḷ.  eṇṇum eḻuttum kaṇ eṉat takum eṉpatu auvaip pirāṭṭiyāriṉ amuta moḻi.  eṇṇeṉpa ēṉai eḻutteṉpa ivviraṇṭum kaṇ eṉpa vāḻum uyirkku eṉpatu vaḷḷuvar vākku.
ōviyaṅkaḷ
paṇṭaik kālattil vāḻnta paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷ tam uḷḷak karuttai maṟṟavarkaḷukkup pulappaṭutta kōl koṇṭu taraiyilum, maṇalilum ōviyantīṭṭi uṇarttivantaṉar.  ataṉ mūlam oruvarukkoruvar karuttup parimāṟṟam ceyyum kalai vaḷarntuvantatu.  ōviyamē namatu eḻuttiṟku aṭippaṭai.  ōviya eḻuttaiyē ulakil vāḻum ellā makkaḷum mutaṉ mutalākak kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu, iṉṟu tol eḻuttiyal āyvil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.  aiyāyiram āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻarkaḷ eḻutap payiṉṟiruntaṉar.  eḻuttu eṉṟa tamiḻppatam eḻutu eṉṟa viṉaic colliṉ aṭiyākap piṟanta collākum.  eḻutu eṉṟāl varai eṉap poruḷ tarum.  nikaṇṭil eḻuttu eṉpataṟkup paṭam uruvam ōviyam eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  ātiyil tamiḻ makkaḷ tam varivaṭivaṅkaḷai ōviyak kuṟiyīṭukaḷiṉiṉṟē kaṇṭupiṭittu ataṟku eḻuttu eṉṟu peyar cūṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.  caṅka nūṟkaḷil ōviyattiṟku eḻuttu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  muṟkālattil tirupparaṅ kuṉṟattiluḷḷa murukaṉ kōyilil oru ōviya maṇṭapam iruntatu.  ataṉaik kuṉṟam pūtaṉār eṉṉum pulavar 
niṉ kuṉṟattu
eḻutoḻil ampalam kāmavēḷ ampiṉ
toḻil vīṟṟirunta nakar		     -(paripāṭal)
eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  itaṟku, niṉkuṉṟattu eḻutiya eḻil tarum ampalam eṉṟu parimēlaḻakar poruḷ koḷḷukiṟār.  iṅkuḷḷa cittira māṭattil cila ōviyaṅkaḷai nappaṇṇaṉār eṉṉum pulavar kaṇṭu ataṉai viḷakkik kūṟum poḻutu, 
iṉṉa palapala eḻuttu nilai maṇṭapam       (paripāṭal)
eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  iṅku, cittira māṭattai, eḻuttu nilai maṇṭapam eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
tamiḻakattil muṟkālattil ōviya eḻuttukkaḷ iruntaṉa.  tamiḻarkaḷ paḻaṅkālattil pala vakaiyāṉa eḻuttukkaḷaip payiṉṟu vantuḷḷaṉar.  yāpparuṅkala virutti ilakkaṇak kottu mutaliya nūṟkaḷ mūlam tamiḻ eḻuttu uruveḻuttu uṇarveḻuttu oli eḻuttu taṉmai eḻuttu eṉa nāṉku pirivukaḷākap pirikkap paṭṭiruntatu.
kāṇappaṭṭa vuruvamellām
māṇak kāṭṭum vakaiyai nāṭi
vaḻuvi vōviyaṉ kaiviṉai pōla
eḻutappaṭuvatu uruveḻuttākum	
(yāpparuṅkala virutti mēṟkōḷ) 
uruvē yuṇarvē yoliyē taṉmai
iruvakai yeḻuttu mīriraṇ ṭākum	
				(ilakkaṇak kottu - mēṟkōḷ)
eṉṟu kūṟap peṟṟuḷḷatu.
camavayaṅka cutta eṉṉum camaṇanūl ki.mu. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻuntatu.  itil 18 vitamāṉa eḻuttukkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  ippaṭṭiyalil tamiḻi eṉṟu tamiḻakattil vaḻaṅkiya eḻuttum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  pirāmi piṟakkum poḻutē tamiḻiyum piṟantatu;  vaḻakkil iruntatu.
lalita vitāram eṉṉum nūlil 64 vakai eḻuttukkaḷ kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.   itil tirāviṭi eṉṉum patamum kāṇap paṭukiṟatu.  tirāviṭi eṉpatu teluṅku ,kaṉṉaṭam, kirantam ākiya eḻuttukkaḷaik kuṟippiṭuvatākum.  ki.pi. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻ varivaṭivaṅkaḷ māṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
1903 -ām āṇṭil varivaṭiva āyvāḷar tiru.veṅkōparāv mutaṉ mutalil kīḻvaḷavu eṉṉum iṭattil tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭittār.  1906 - ām āṇṭil tirunelvēli māvaṭṭa atikāri (District Collector) kemaiṭu eṉṉum āṅkilēyar maṟukāl eṉṉum iṭattil tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ iruppataik kaṇṭupiṭittār.   ataṉ piṉṉar āṇṭippaṭṭiyil īyak kācil tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟaṉa.  appāl tamiḻ āciriyar araccalūril irācu eṉpavar tamiḻ vaṭivaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭittār.  appāl tiruvāta ūril ai. makātēvaṉum koṟkaiyil tamiḻakat tol poruḷ āyvuttuṟaiyaic cērnta majittum, piṟarum tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭittaṉar.  maturaiyaic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa kuṉṟu
kaḷilum, kuṟṟālattiluḷḷa kuṉṟukaḷilum tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
tol  eḻuttukkaḷ
tamiḻ nāṭṭu varalāṟu, kalai, paṇpāṭu, araciyal, nākarikam mutaliyavaikaḷai aṟivataṟkuk kalveṭṭukaḷ aṭippaṭaic cāṉṟu
kaḷākat tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.  varivaṭivaṅkaḷ arumpuvataṟku muṉṉar karuttu veḷiyīṭṭiṟkāka ekiptu, cumēriyā, intiyā, cīṉā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil cila muṟaikaḷ piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu iṉṟu tol poruḷ āyvut tuṟaiyiṉarum, eḻuttiyal nipuṇarkaḷum piṉvarum muṟaikaḷ piṉpaṟṟappaṭṭatākak kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.
1. kuṟiyīṭṭu moḻi (Sign Language) peruviyaṉ muṟai (Perunrium quipus) kuḻū uk F¿fŸ(Joten Signs) 
2. ōviya eḻuttumuṟai (Pictographs) ōviya eḻuttukkaḷiṉ muṉṉōṭikaḷ - vaṭa amerikka intiyarkaḷiṉ vāmpum kaccai (Wampum belt) kalumeṭ kuḻal (Calumet Peace Pipe) karuttup paravalē mukkiya nōkkamāy amaintatu.
3. karuttup paṭak kuṟiyīṭukaḷ.  (Pidegrams)
4. col allatu eḻuttukkup patilāka niṟkum poruḷ vaṭivam (Hieroglyphs) col vēṟupāṭukaḷ, hīrō kiḷipciṉ vakaikaḷ.
5. āppuk kuṟi eḻuttumuṟai (Cunciforum Writing)
6. cīṉa eḻuttumuṟai kiyāṅ ciṅ (Hsiang hsing) cih cih (Chich Shih)
ātiyil ulakameṅkum makkaḷ tam karuttait terivikka ōviyaṅkaḷaiyē payaṉpaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar.  intiyāvil uḷḷa mattiyap piratēcattiluḷḷa pimpaṭkār kuṉṟil kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭa pīṭuṟṟa ōviyaṅkaḷ nūṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ vāḻnta mutirāp paṇpuṭaiya mutumakkaḷ tīṭṭiya ōviyamākum.  makātēva malaiyil uḷḷa maṅkā ōviyaṅkaḷum mīrcāppūril kaṇṭa ariya ōviyaṅkaḷum, takkāṇattil reyccūr māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa koppal, pikkiḷikkal, maki, mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa ōviyaṅkaḷum, vintiya malaikaḷil uḷḷa ōviyaṅkaḷum intiyāvil eḻuttup piṟappataṟku ēṟṟa aṭippaṭaiyāy iruntaṉa.
ōviya eḻuttukkaḷ
ōviyaṅkaḷ eḻuttukkaḷ ākā.  tīṭṭiya uruvai  maṭṭum ōviyam kuṟikkumēyoḻiya vēṟu etaiyum kuṟikkātu.  makkaḷ malaikaḷilum kāṭukaḷilum tirintalaintatai viṭṭu, āṟṟaṅ
karaikaḷil pala makkaḷ iṇaintu kuṭiyēṟi, payirt toḻilai vaḷarttu nakara vāḻkkai naṭattiyapoḻutu taṅkaḷ āṭu māṭu, ēṉaiya poruḷkaḷaik kuṟikka kuṟiyīṭukaḷai uruvākkat terintaṉar.  makkaḷ teyvaṅkaḷukku uṟaiviṭamākak kōyilkaḷai uruvākkiṉar.  ūrt teyvaṅkaḷukku makkaḷ kāṇikkaiyāka āṭu māṭu pōṉṟa
vaṟṟai vaḻaṅkiṉar.  avaikaḷ ērāḷamāka perukiṉa.  avaikaḷaik kaṇakkiṭap pala kuṟiyīṭukaḷ tēvaiyāyiṉa.  eṭuttukkāṭṭākak kōyilkaḷiṉ cuvarkaḷilō allatu kaṟkaḷilō māṭṭiṉ uruvaṅkaḷait tīṭṭi pakkattil oṉṟu, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu eṉpataik kāṭṭa kōṭukaḷait tīṭṭiṉar.  akkuṟikaḷ kōyilkaḷukku ettaṉai māṭukaḷ uḷa eṉpatait terivittaṉar.  kōṭukaḷ iṉṟi puḷḷikaḷum iṭappaṭṭatu eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  ivaṟṟai ōviya eḻuttu eṉṟaṉar.  mecapottāmiyāviṟ maṇ ōṭukaḷil ittakaiya ōviya eḻuttukkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭiruppatu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu.  mīṉōcūṉ kiriṭē eṉṉum paḻam pakutiyilum inta eḻuttukkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
karutteḻuttukkaḷ
 ōviya eḻuttukkaḷ vaḷarcci peṟṟu karutteḻuttukkaḷākap pariṇamitteḻuntaṉa.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ñāyiṟṟai maṭṭum kuṟikkum oru vaṭṭam ōviya eḻuttu eṉappaṭṭatu.  ataṉ tōṟṟam, ataṉ veppa nilai mutaliyavaṟṟaik kuṟippiṭum kuṟikaḷ karutteḻuttu eṉappaṭṭatu.   iḵtaṉṟi māṭṭiṉ kālkaḷaiyō kompukaḷaiyō varaintu māṭṭaik kuṟikka payaṉpaṭuttiṉar itaṉaik karuttu eḻuttu (Ideogram) eṉpar.  ōviya eḻuttilum, karutteḻuttilum iṭappaṭṭa kuṟikaḷ olikkappaṭum ataṉ peyarukkum evvita campantamum illai.
oli eḻuttukkaḷ
makkaḷ nākarika vaḷarcci peṟṟapiṉ oli eḻuttai uruvākkiṉar.  oru poruḷukku peyarum, ataṉ oliyai eḻuta oli eḻuttum kaṇṭaṉar.  ātiyil oru vilaṅku, allatu paṟavai, maram, vīṭu pōṉṟavaṟṟiṟku orē eḻuttai uruvākkiyuḷḷaṉar.  eṭuttuk
kāṭṭāka ā=pacu, i=itu, ī=vaṇṭu, ū=ūṉ, ē=pāṇam,  ai=aracaṉ, ō=maṭai, kā=kāttal, kī=kiḷikkural, kū=pūmi, kai=karam, kō=aracaṉ, cā=cāveṉṉēvil, cī=ilakkumi, cē=civappu, cō=araṇ eṉṟu poruḷ tarum muṟaiyil pala eḻuttukkaḷ uḷa.  tā, tī, tū, tē, tai, ta, tō, nā, nī, nō, pā, pū, pē, pai, pō, mā, mī, mū, mai, vā, vī, vē, vai pōṉṟaṉa. appāl acai, ācai, icai, īkai, uri, ūr, eli, ēṭu, jaṉam, okai, ōṭu, kaṇ, kāṭu, kiḷi, kīri, kukai, kūṭu, keṭu, kēḷ, kaitai, koṭi, kōṭu, kauri pōṉṟa īreḻuttuṭaiya coṟkaḷ tamiḻil eḻuntuḷḷaṉa.  iḵtaṉṟi cū, cūriyaṉ, mā-māṭu, ā-āṭu eṉṟa muṟaiyil mutaṉmutalāka eḻuttukkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.  itē pōṉṟu poruṭkaḷai ataṉ uruvaṅkaḷai eḻutiṉar nāḷaṭaivil ac coṟkaḷiṉ mutal oliyai maṭṭum kuṟikkavum toṭaṅkiṉar.  immuṟaikku, mutal oli eḻuttu muṟai (Acrophony) eṉṟu peyar.  itu pōṉṟa vaḷarcci
kaḷāl oliyeḻuttukkaḷ uruppeṟṟaṉa.  oli eḻuttukkaḷ poruḷaiyō karuttaiyō kuṟiyātu kuṟippiṭṭa oliyaiyē kuṟittaṉa.  oli eḻuttu enta moḻiyil varaiyappaṭṭatō am moḻiyāltāṉ atai aṟiya muṭiyum.  uruvattiṟkum olikkum toṭarpu iṉṟi iruntālum avvuru avvoliyai maṭṭum kuṟikka eḻuntatāy nilai peṟṟuviṭṭatu.  inta eḻuttu oli eḻuttu (Phonatic Writing) eṉṟa peyaraip peṟṟatu.
oli eḻuttu akaravakai eḻuttu,  uyir eḻuttu eṉa iruvakaiyākap pirikkappaṭṭatu.  uyir eḻuttu, meyyeḻuttu eṉṟu āṅkilattil eḻuntaṉa.  tamiḻil uyir eḻuttu, meyyeḻuttu, uyirmeyeḻuttu, eṇkaḷ mutaliya niral peṟa eḻuntatu.  mikap piṟkālattil eḻunta āṅkila moḻi, tamiḻ  eḻuttaip pōṉṟu oḻuṅkupeṟa amaikkappaṭavillai.  tamiḻil uyir eḻuttu a, i, u, e, ai, o, ōḷa eṉṟu oḻuṅkupeṟa amaikkap paṭṭiruppatuṭaṉ, neṭil eḻuttum amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  āṅkila moḻiyil V(A) pi (B) ci (C) ṭi (D) <(E) eṉṟu amaintirukkiṟatēyoḻiya ē, ī, ai, ō, yū (A.E.I.O.U) eṉṟu amaiyavillai.  atōṭu āṅkilattil eṇ intiyāviṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ vāṅkap peṟṟuḷḷa eṇṇēyākum.
intiya nāṭṭil uḷḷa cintu veḷiyil ki.mu.3000 - m āṇṭiṟkumuṉ eḻuttukkaḷ eḻuntaṉa.  avai ōviya eḻuttuk
kaḷākap piṟantaṉa.  ivai muḻuvatum ōviya eḻuttukkaḷ alla.  cila oli eḻuttukkaḷum uḷa.  ivaṟṟai aṟiñarkaḷ ōviya -oli eḻuttukkaḷ eṉṟu uraippar.
cintu veḷi cittira eḻuttukkaḷē intiya eḻuttukkaḷukku tāy eḻuttukkaḷ ākum.  itiṉiṉṟu vaṭa pirāmiyum, teṉ pirāmiyum karōṣṭiyum tōṉṟiṉa.  teṉpirāmiyiṉiṉṟu tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.  tamiḻakattil nilaviya toṉmaiyāṉa eḻuttu tamiḻi eṉap peyar peṟṟatu.  paṇṭaiyat tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷai tamiḻi, tirāviṭi eṉṟu pirākirutamoḻiyiṉar muṟkālattil aḻaittu vantaṉar.  cila aṟiñarkaḷ pirāmi tōṉṟiya poḻutē tamiḻi eḻuttukkaḷum tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa eṉṟum, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭa, tuḷuva eḻuttukkaḷ tirāviṭi eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
kukai eḻuttukkaḷ
tamiḻ nāṭṭil kiṟittava ūḻikku muṉṉuḷḷa tamiḻk kalveṭṭukkaḷ perumpālum kukaiyiṉ mēṟpakutiyil veṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa veṉṟu terikiṟatu.  araccalūril uḷḷa kalveṭṭu icai eḻuttukkaḷaik kuṟippaṉavāy uḷḷatu.  kukaik kalveṭṭukkāṉa pāṉai ōṭukaḷilum ivveḻuttukkaḷ kiṭait tuḷḷaṉa.  arikkaṉ mēṭṭil pautta camayat toṭarpuḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  āṇṭippaṭṭiyil kiṭaitta caṅkakāla īyak kācukaḷil pirāmi vari vaṭivil tiṉṉaṉ etirāṉ cēntaṉ ā eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  itu ki.mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭaic cārnta eḻuttāka eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  
tamiḻ eḻuttu
ki.pi. mūṉṟāṇṭiliruntu tamiḻ moḻiyai eḻuta tamiḻi eḻuttaiyum, vaṭṭeḻuttaiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.  itaṉai tamiḻ tol poruḷ ārāyccit tuṟai iyakkunar tiru. irā. nākacāmi kalveṭṭiyal eṉṉum nūlil teḷivāy viḷakkiyuḷḷār.  pallavarkaḷ maṉṉaṉ cimma viṣṇu kālac ceppēṭukaḷ tamiḻ eḻuttilum kiranta eḻuttilum eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
muṟkālac cōḻar kalveṭṭukaḷ tamiḻil aḻakākap piḻai
kaḷiṉṟi veṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  piṟkālac cōḻar kalveṭṭukaḷ atāvatu irācarācaṉ kālattiṟkup piṉṉar veṭṭiya kalveṭṭukaḷ ciṟappākak kāṇappaṭavillai.  vicaya nakara nāyakka maṉṉar kālattuk kalveṭṭukkaḷum aḻakiṉṟip piḻaikaḷ malintuḷḷaṉavāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  mutal pallava maṉṉarkaḷiṉ ceppēṭukaḷ teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭa, kiranta eḻuttukaḷil uḷḷaṉa.   pala kalveṭṭukaḷ kiranta eḻuttilum nākarika eḻuttilum uḷḷaṉa.
maturaip pāṇṭiyarkaḷ ki.pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu 
9 - ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvarai eḻutiya kalveṭṭukaḷ tamiḻilum kirantattilum uḷḷaṉa.  ceppēṭukaḷ vaṭṭeḻuttil uḷḷaṉa.  cēra nāṭṭil uḷḷa pala kalveṭṭukaḷ vaṭṭeḻuttil uḷḷaṉa.  atiyarkaḷiṉ kalveṭṭukaḷ pallavar kiranta eḻuttilum vaṭṭeḻuttilum uḷḷaṉa.  kuṟṟāla nātar kōyil kalveṭṭum vaṭṭeḻuttilē uḷḷaṉa.  tiru. kōpināt rāv tiṟaṉuṭaiya paṇṭaiya tamiḻ eḻuttiṉiṉṟu vaṭṭeḻuttut tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
kōyilkaḷum kalveṭṭukaḷum
paṇṭaik kālattil tāḷkaḷ kiṭaiyātu.  ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ellām paṉa ōlaikaḷilē kūriya muṉaiyaiyuṭaiya eḵku eḻuttāṇi koṇṭē eḻutivantaṉar.  ōlaiyil eḻutappaṭum ēṭukaḷ oru nūṟu āṇṭukaḷ kūṭa nilaittiruppatillai.  vēṟu cila nāṭukaḷil marappaṭṭaikaḷil eḻuti vaittiruntaṉar.  atuvum nīṇṭa kālam nilaittu niṟpatillai.  cila nāṭukaḷil elumpukaḷil kūṭa eḻuti vantaṉar.  cila nāṭkaḷil paippira eṉṉum oru vakai nāṇaṟ pullil eḻuti vantaṉar.
cila nāṭukaḷil tōlil eḻuti vantaṉar.  periya ilakkiyaṅ kaḷaiyum maṟainūṟkaḷaiyum 1000 -kkaṇakkāṉa tōlkaḷil eḻutum paṭi nērntatu.  cātāraṇa makkaḷ ittakaiya tōl nūlkaḷai vilai koṭuttu vāṅka muṭiyātiruntatu.  ituvum 100 allatu 150 āṇṭukaḷukku mēl nilaittu niṟka muṭiyātiruntatu.  mutaṉ mutalāka paipiḷ eppaṭi eḻutappaṭṭatu eṉṟu ciṅkappūri liruntu veḷivantuḷḷa tamiḻ nāḷ ēṭoṉṟil paṭittēṉ.  atu, aṭiyil varumāṟākum.
accu eḻuttukkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭitta kūṭaṉpark tāṉ mutaṉ mutalākap paipiḷai acciṭṭu veḷiyiṭṭār.  ulakilēyē mutaṉ mutal acciṭappaṭṭa puttakamum paipiḷtāṉ.
itu lattīṉ moḻiyil acciṭap peṟṟatu.  acciṭappaṭṭa āṇṭu ki.pi. 1455.
mottam 200 piratikaḷē acciṭappaṭṭaṉa.  ovvoru piratiyilum 1282 pakkaṅkaḷē iruntaṉa.  ovvoru pakkamum 36 varikaḷaiyuṭaiyatāy iruntaṉa.
intap puttakaṅkaḷ kākitaṅkaḷil acciṭap peṟavillai.  tāḷkaḷ kiṭaiyātu.  eṉavē āṭumāṭukaḷiṉ tōlkaḷil tāṉ vētap puttakam acciṭappaṭṭaṉa.  anta vaḻakkappaṭi paipiḷum kaṉṟukkuṭṭiyiṉ tōlil tāṉ acciṭappaṭṭatu.  itaṟkāka cumār 10,000kku mēṟpaṭṭa kaṉṟukkuṭṭikaḷ kollappaṭṭaṉa.
200 piratikaḷil 47 piratikaḷ (mutalil tōlil acciṭappaṭṭa cattiya vētākama nūliṉ paṭikaḷ)  ippoḻutu cermaṉiyil uḷḷa tol poruḷ kāṭci cālaikaḷil (Museums) irukkiṉṟaṉa.
5000 -ām āṇṭukaḷukku cumēriyāviṉ kaḷi maṇ takaṭukaḷil ciṟu kuccukaḷ koṇṭu eḻuti pala ēṭukaḷ vaittuḷḷaṉar.  
ekiptu nāṭṭil ōviya eḻuttukkaḷ (Hieroghph) eṉṉum ekiptiyar kaṇṭa maṟai poruṭ kuṟiyīṭukaḷai kallil tōṇṭi vācittu vantaṉar.  5000  -māṇṭukaṭku muṉ cintu veḷiyil kallil cila uruva eḻuttukkaḷait tōṇṭiyum cemput takaṭukaḷil kūṟiya  eḻuttāṇi koṇṭum eḻuti vantaṉar.
tamiḻ nāṭṭil 2000 -m āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻarkaḷ tirukkōyilkaḷukku viṭum māṉiyaṅkaḷai kallil eḻutiyum ceppēṭukaḷil eḻutiyum  uḷḷaṉar. ceppēṭu iṉṟaiyap pattirap pativu ceyvatu pōl āvaṇak kaḷariyil pativu ceyyappeṟṟatu.  atil eḻutiyavarkaḷ peyar māṉiyam aḷitta aracaṉiṉ muttiraiyum iṭappaṭṭatu.  attakaiya ceppēṭukaḷ tamiḻakattil nūṟāyirattiṟku mēl uṇṭu.  avaikaḷil oṉṟiṉ uruvappaṭattai ittuṭaṉ iṇaittuḷḷōm.  paṭam kāṇka. akkālattil eḻutap peṟṟa kalveṭṭu cācaṉam oṉṟiṉ vācakamum ittuṭaṉ tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu kāṇka:-
namatu nāṭu mikat toṉmaiyāṉatu.  innāṭṭil makkaḷ nūṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ iṅku vāḻntu varukiṉṟārkaḷ.  ki.mu. 3000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē payirtoḻilai vaḷarttum kait toḻilkaḷ pala kaṇṭupiṭittum periya nakaraṅkaḷaiyum, akaṉṟu nīṇṭa terukkaḷaiyum mūṉṟu nāṉku ēḻu māṭaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya vīṭukaḷaiyum, māḷikaikaḷaiyum, araṇmaṉaikaḷaiyum kōyil kaḷaiyum amaittuḷḷārkaḷ.  eḵkuppōṉṟa uṟutiyāṉa uyarnta araṇkaḷai, amaittuc cīrpeṟa amaittuḷḷārkaḷ.  glF
