āṉāl avarkaḷ tam nāṭṭu varalāṟukaḷai eḻuti
vaikkavillai.  cintu veḷi makkaḷ varalāṟu avarkaḷ ciṟu
kaṟkaḷil tīṭṭiya muttirai eḻuttukkaḷ mūlam cemput takaṭukaḷil (tāyattukaḷil) eḻutiya eḻuttu mūlamāyum ōraḷavu aṟintu varukiṟōm.  tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟu akanāṉūṟu, puṟanāṉūṟu, pattuppāṭṭu, cilappatikāram pōṉṟa tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ mūlamāyum namatu kōyilkaḷilum, piṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum kiṭaikkum kalveṭṭukaḷ, ceppēṭukaḷ, meykkīrttikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ
mūlam oruvāṟu aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.  tamiḻ nāṭṭu maṉṉarkaḷ vaṭakkē imayam varai ceṉṟu etirppaṭṭu aracarkaḷaiyellām veṉṟu imayamalai mītu taṅkaḷatu kuṭaiyaiyum, ciṉṉaṅkaḷaiyum poṟittu vantuḷḷārkaḷ.  ilaṅkai mītu paṭaiyeṭuttapōtu tirukkōṇamalai mītu tam koṭiyaip poṟittu vantuḷḷārkaḷ.  irācarāca cōḻaṉ taṉ makaṉ irācēntira cōḻaṉ talaimaiyil kaṭaṟpaṭaiyai aṉuppi kaṭāram, malaiyam, ciṅkapuram, tāylāntu, kampōṭiyam cāvakamum, cirivicayam, pāli, pōṉiyō pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷaiyellām veṉṟu āṅkāṅkē kōyilkaḷ eḻuppi kalveṭṭukaḷ poṟittum vantuḷḷāṉ.  tamiḻ nāṭṭu maṉṉarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ piṟa nāṭṭiṉ mītu veṟṟiyīṭṭiya poḻutellām tam nāṭṭil periya kōyilkaḷ eḻuppiyum, kōyilkaḷil periya tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ amaittum, teyvat tiruuruvaṅkaḷukku vākaṉaṅkaḷum aṇikalaṉkaḷum, tiruviḷakkukaḷum, kuṭaṅkaḷum, vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷārkaḷ.  ataṟkāka ērāḷamāṉa poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, pittaḷai ulōkaṅkaḷai vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷārkaḷ.  aracarkaḷum, aracikaḷum iḷavaracarkaḷum, amaiccarkaḷum, pirapukkaḷum ērāḷamāṉa celvaṅkaḷaiyum nilaṅkaḷaiyum nivantamāka aḷittuḷḷaṉar.  tirukkōyilkaḷil ney viḷakku erikka āṭukaḷum, māṭukaḷum aḷittuḷḷaṉar.  avaikaḷ mēyvataṟku pul niṟainta mēyccal taraikaḷum nel vayalkaḷum tāṉam aḷittuḷḷaṉar.  kōyil paṇiyāṭkaḷukkum, kōyilil āṭalum pāṭalum iyaṟṟum tēvatācikaḷukku vīṭukaḷum, vayalkaḷum vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷaṉar.  ivaikaḷaip paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ kōyil cuvarkaḷilum taraiyilum uḷḷa kaṟkaḷil akkālattil vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷil poṟittuḷḷārkaḷ.  iṉṟaiyap pattiraṅkaḷ pōṉṟu ceppēṭukaḷil avarkaḷ māṉipamāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa nilaṅkaḷiṉ vivaraṅkaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭu avaṟṟil aracamuttiraikaḷum, eḻutiyavar peyarum, cāṭcikaḷum peyar kaḷum eḻuti āvaṇak kaḷaṟiyil pativu ceyyap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
intak kalveṭṭukaḷ ceppēṭukaḷ, ōlaiccuvaṭikaḷ mūlam akkālattil vāḻnta maṉṉarkaḷ peyar avarkaḷ āṭci ceyta kālam, veṟṟi peṟṟa nāṭukaḷ, maṉṉarkaḷiṉ maṉaivikaḷ, makkaḷ, cēṉātipatikaḷ peyarkaḷ veṟṟi  peṟṟa nāṭukaḷ, tōlviyuṟṟa aracarkaḷ, avar maṉaivi, makkaḷ, mutaliyavarkaḷiṉ viparaṅkaḷ terikiṉṟaṉa.  maṉṉarkaḷ kōyilukku vaḻaṅkiya aṇikalaṉkaḷ, veṇkalam, veḷḷi, poṉ pōṉṟa ulōkaṅkaḷil ceytu koṭutta kuṭam, cempu, kopparai, keṇṭi, kiṇṇam, taṭṭu, tāmpāḷam, kaiviḷakku, nilai viḷakku, kuttuviḷakku, kiḷai viḷakku, nantā viḷakku, tūṇ viḷakku, tūkku viḷakku, laṭca tīpam, pōṉṟavaikaḷaiyum nila aḷavai muṟaikaḷaiyum aṟiyalām.  maṉṉarkaḷiṉ peyaraiyum paṭṭattaiyum, perumaiyaiyum avarkaḷatu meykkīrttikaḷ mūlam aṟiyalām.
kalveṭṭukaḷ perumpālum tiruuṇṇāḻikaiyiṉ (karppak kirakattiṉ) veḷic cuvarkaḷil veṭṭappaṭṭirukkum.  cila, tūṇkaḷ, maṇṭapaṅkaḷ, pakkac cuvarkaḷ, paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ, taraiyil patippikkappaṭṭirukkum kaṟkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟil poṟikkap paṭṭirukkum.  ikkalveṭṭukaḷiluḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷiṉ mātiriyaik koṇṭē kōyilkaḷ eḻunta kālattai oruvāṟu kaṇikka muṭiyum.
kalveṭṭukaḷ vati srī eṉṉum coṟṟoṭaril toṭaṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  pallavarkaḷ ceppēṭukaḷ cilavaṟṟil jitampa kavatā eṉṟa coṟṟoṭar kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  āy maṉṉaṉ aḷitta ceppēṭukaḷ cila namō nārāyaṇāya eṉṟu toṭaṅkukiṟatu.  cila ōm vati srī eṉṟu toṭaṅkukiṟatu.  vēṟu cila, hari naṉṟākak kuruvē tuṇai. haricupamatu hari srī kaṇapatiyē nama, civayam eṉṟum toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.  appāl koṭai aḷikkum aracaṉiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ purāṇa marapum varalāṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  appāl koṭai koṭukkum kālattil āṭcipurinta aracaṉ, avaṉ āṭci arumpiya āṇṭum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  cōḻar kalveṭṭukaḷil aracaṉ peyar, avaṉ āṭci toṭaṅkiya āṇṭu, avaṉatu veṟṟip piratāpam, paṭṭam mutaliyavaikaḷum kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  mutalām irācarācaṉ kālam mutal mutaṉmutalāka aracarkaḷatu meykkīrttiyum cērkkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  meykkīrttiyil aracaṉiṉ vīracceyalkaḷ, paṭṭattaracikaḷ peyar mutaliyavai iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
nōkkaṅkaḷ
perumpālāṉa kalveṭṭukaḷ aracarkaḷ kōyilkaḷukku nilaṅkaḷ tāṉamāka vaḻaṅkiyatum, kōyilkaḷukku nontā viḷakku aḷittu, viḷakku erikkum neykkāka cāvā mūvāp pērāṭu, pacu allatu erumai vaḻaṅkiyataik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa.  nilaṅkaḷ kōyilukkō allatu taṉippaṭṭa naparkaḷukkō viṟṟāl annilattiṉ parappu ataṉ nāṟpuṟa ellaikaḷ, anta nilattaic cārntirukkum piṟa poruḷkaḷ ellām kuṟippiṭappaṭum.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka in nāṟpāṟ ellaikku uṭpaṭṭa uṇṇilam oḻiviṉṟi  uṭumpoṭi āmai tavaḻum iṭam aṉaittum kīḻnōkkiya kiṇaṟum mēl nōkkiya maramum, mīṉpaṭu paḷḷamum, tēṉpaṭu potumpum puṟṟum teṟṟiyum ākiya ellām viṟkap
paṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  atōṭu, aḷikkum koṭai cantira cūriyar uḷḷaḷavum nilai peṟṟirukkum eṉṟum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  iṟutiyil paṉmāhēvarar rakṣai eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭu viṭṭu inta aṟakkoṭaikku tīṅku niṉaittālō allatu iṭai ceytālō kaṅkaik karaiyilē kārām pacuvaik koṉṟa tōcattai aṭaivārkaḷ eṉṟō. ittarmattiṟku akitam niṉaittār kaṅkai iṭai kumariyiṭai eḻunūṟṟuk kātattilum ceytār ceyta pāvattup paṭuvār eṉṟum ittarmat
tiṟku ātāmoruvar picaku paṇṇiṉāl civa turōkiyumāy mōvāy pukku mulaiyeḻuttāṉumāy oruvaṉukkum oruttikkum piṟantāṉumallāṉāy kōmāṅki cattai pucippāṉumāy, kaṅkai yiṭai kumariyiṭai kurāl pacu koṉṟāṉ pāvam koḷvāṉ eṉṟum kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu.  kalveṭṭukaḷil aracaṉ tēvar, perumāḷ, nāyaṉār eṉṟum araci nampirāṭṭiyār eṉṟum aracakulamakaḷir āḻvār, nācciyār eṉṟellām kuṟikkap
paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
iṟantupōṉa maṉṉaṉaip putaikkum iṭam paḷḷippaṭai eṉṟu kalveṭṭukaḷil kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  aracaṉaip putaitta iṭattil kōyilkaḷ eḻuppum paḻakkamum akkālattil uḷḷatu.  aracaṉuṭaiya āṇai, tirumantira ōlai eṉṉum atikāri eḻuti iṟutiyil tirumantira ōlai nāyakam eṉṟa atikāriyāl oppu nōkkappaṭukiṟatu.  piṉṉar viṭaiyil atikāri vari eṉṟa ēṭṭil eḻutum paṭi āṇaiyiṭuvār.  naṭuvirukkai atikāri atai eḻutum paṭi ceyvār.  puravu varitiṇaikkaḷam, paṭṭōlai ākiya atikārikaḷ oppamiṭṭu koṭai aḷikkappaṭṭa nilam irukkum ūrukku anta āṇai eṭuttuc cellappaṭum.
mātirikkāka iṅku cila kalveṭṭukkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  avai aṭiyil varumāṟu:-
irājarājaṉ kalveṭṭu
(ki.pi. 1010)
(ikkalveṭṭu tañcāvūr māvaṭṭam, tañcāvūrp peruvuṭaiyār)
vati. srī. ētat vicva nrupacrēṇi mauli mālōpalālitam cācaṉam rājēya rājakēsari varmaṇa:
tirumakaḷ pōlap perunilac celviyum taṉakkē urimai pūṇṭamai maṉakkoḷa kāntaḷūrc cālai kalam aruttaruḷi vēṅkai nāṭum kaṅka pāṭiyum taṭikai pāṭiyum nuḷampa pāṭiyum kuṭamalai nāṭum, kollamum kaliṅkamum eṇticai pukaḻ tara īḻamaṇṭalamuma iraṭṭapāṭi ēḻarai ilakkamum, tiṇtiṟal veṉṟit taṇṭāl koṇṭa taṉ eḻil vaḷar ūḻiyuḷ ellā yāṇṭum toḻutaka viḷaṅkum yāṇṭē, ceḻiyarait tōcukoḷ kō rāja kēcaripanmarāṉa srīrājarāja tēvarkku yāṇṭu irupat tāṟāvatu nāḷ irupatiṉāl uṭaiyār srīrāja rāja tēvar tañcāvūrk kōyiliṉuḷḷāl, irumaṭi cōḻaṉiṉ kīḻait tirumañcaṉa cālai tāṉañ ceytaruḷāliruntu, pāṇṭiya kulācaṉi vaḷanāṭṭu, tañcāvūrk kūṟṟattut tañcāvūr nām eṭuppicca tirukkaṟṟaḷi srīrājarājīcvara muṭaiyārkku nām kuṭuttaṉavum, akkaṉ kuṭuttaṉavum nam peṇṭukaḷ kuṭuttaṉavum, maṟṟum kuṭuttār kuṭuttaṉavum srī vimāṉattil kallilē veṭṭuka!  eṉṟu tiruvāy moḻiñcaruḷa veṭṭiṉa.  (1) yāṇṭu irupattaiñcāvatu nāḷ muṉṉūṟṟirupattiraṇṭiṉāl uṭaiyār srīrājarājatēvar kuṭutta srīpali eḻuntaruḷum poṉṉiṉ koḷkai tēvar oruvar, āṭavallāṉ eṉṉum kallāl niṟai eṇṇūṟṟirupat toṉpatiṉ kaḻañ cēy mukkālēy mūṉṟu mañcāṭi (2) nāḷatiṉālēy kuṭutta poṉṉiṉ patmācaṉa srīpalit tālam oṉṟu, mēṟpaṭi kallāl niṟai  toḷāyirattut toṇṇūṟṟūṟṟaiṅ kaḻañcaraiyē nālu mañcāṭi (3) yāṇṭu irupattāṟāvatu nāḷ patiṉālil uṭaiyār srīrāja tēvar kuṭutta poṉṉiṉ tiruppaṭṭam oṉṟu, āṭavallāṉ, eṉṉum kallāl niṟai nāṉūṟṟut toṇṇūṟṟoṉpatiṉ kaḻañcu (4) nāḷatiṉālēy kuṭutta poṉṉiṉ tiruppaṭṭam oṉṟu, mēṟpaṭi kallāl niṟai nāṉūṟṟut toṇṇūṟṟu nāṟkaḻañ caraiyēy iraṇṭu mañcāṭi (5) nāḷatiṉālēy kuṭutta poṉṉiṉ tiruppaṭṭam oṉṟu, mēṟpaṭi kallāl niṟai nāṉūṟṟu eṇpattu nāṟkaḻañ cē kāl.
itai aṭuttu, mēlum, kāḷaṅkaḷ, tiruppaḷḷit toṅkal, tavaḷac cattiram, tirukkoṟṟakkuṭai, īccōppikai, veṇcāmarakkai, kāḷañci mutaliyavaikaḷum,
aṇikalaṉkaḷil ciri muṭi, vīrappaṭṭam, tiruutarappantaṉam tiruvaṭikkārai, tiruppaṭṭikai, captacari, pañcacari tirukkutampai, tōṭu, irācavarttam, tiraḷ maṇivaṭam, tālimaṇivaṭam, ciricantam mutaliya poṉ aṇikaḷum, 
vaṭukavāḷi, ēkavali, muttiṉ cūṭakam, tirukkāl vaṭam, mutaliya muttu aṇikaḷum, 
kaṇṭanāṇ, pullikaik kaṇṭanāṇ, pācamālai, māṇikkattiṉ tāli, cirivākuvalayam, patakkam, irattiṉa vaḷaiyam, irattiṉa kaṭakam, irattiṉamōtiram, navarattiṉa mōtiram, piruṣṭa kaṇṭikai mutaliya maṇikaḷ patitta aṇikalaṉkaḷum, 
oṭṭu vaṭṭil, kalacam, kuṭam, taṭṭam, kuṟumaṭal, kiṭāram, mutaliya veḷḷi poṉ pōṉṟa tātup poruḷkaḷāl ceyta parikalaṅkaḷum nipantamāka aḷikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
pallavar ceppēṭu
pallava cimmavarmaṉ (ki.pi. 6 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu)
ikkalveṭṭu tañcai māvaṭṭam tiruttuṟaip pūṇṭi vaṭṭāram paḷḷaṉ kōyil eṉṟa ūril kiṭaitta ceppēṭukaḷ
kō vicaya cimhavarmaṟku yāṇṭu āṟāvatu veṇkoṟṟak kōṭṭattup perunakara nāṭṭu nāṭṭār kāṇka.  namnāṭṭu amaṇ cērkkai paruttikkuṉṟil vajranantik kuravarkkup paḷḷic cantamākak koṭuttōm.  tāṅkaḷum paṭākai naṭantu kallum kaḷḷiyum nāṭṭi aṟaiyōlai ceytu kuṭuttuviṭutaka?  eṉṟa nāṭṭārkkut tirumukam viṭa, nāṭṭārum tirumukam kaṇṭu, toḻutu, talaikku vaittu paṭākai naṭantu, kallum kaḷḷiyum nāṭṭi nāṭār viṭutta aṟaiyōlaip paṭikku ellai;  kīḻpāl ellai ēnta lēriyiṉ kīḻaikkaṭaṟṟiṉ mēṟkum, naṭaik kollai ellai;  iṉṉum teṉpāl ellai iṉṉum nilapāṭi ellai, iṉṉum vilāṭaṉkuṟṟēt tattu ellai;  iṉṉum mēlpāl ellai.  māvēt tattuvatiyiṉ kiḻakkum, mummūlai ēttattu ellai iṉṉum āṟṟellai;  iṉṉum vaṭapāl ellai peruṅ kollaiyiṉ teṟku iṉṉum pāṇṭiyaṉ kayattu ellai.  iṉṉum murukkaṅ kēṇi ellai ivvicaitta perunāṉku ellaiyakattum akappaṭṭa nilam, nīr nilamum puṉceyyum kaḷarum kaṉṟu mēypāḻum kāṭum pīṭilikaiyum kiṭaṅkum kēṇiyum maṉaiyum, maṉaippaṭappum, uṭumpōṭi āmai tavaḻntatu ellām uṇṇilaṉ oḻiviṉṟi kōvum poṟiyum māṟṟu, kuṭinīkki, ivvūr peṟṟa vajra nantik kuravarkkē paḷḷic cantamāka tāmarilum patiṉāṟaraippaṭṭi nilam koṭuttōm innilattiṟkellai;  kīḻpāl ellai pulikiḻār paṭṭiyiṉ mēṟkum teṉpāl ellai muḷḷai pāttellaiyiṉ vaṭakkum mēlpāl ellai pōkkuvāyc ceṟuviṉ kiḻakkum, vaṭapāl ellai pallāṅkuḷik kāliṉ teṟkum, innāṉku ellaiyakattum akappaṭṭa pūmiyum paruttik kuṉṟiṉ vajra nantik kuravarkkē paḷḷic cantamāka paratatti ceṉṟatu, narapayaṉ kaṇattiyāl sittam.
irāmanātapuram kalveṭṭu
(paḻam pāṇṭiya maṉṉaṉ māṟaṉ caṭaiyaṉ)
(ki.pi. 10 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu)
1. srī ko māṟañcaṭaiyaṉoṭu cōḻa nāṭṭiṭavai yāt
2. tirai ceyta parāntakap paḷḷa veḷāṉākiṉa nak
3. kam puḷḷaṉ ṟaṉ perāṟ puḷḷaṉeri eṉṟu
4. kuḷa mākkik kaṟkotik kumuḻi ceyvittuk kuṟai
5. ppaṇi niṉṟatu muṟṟup peṟuttāṉ puḷḷana
6. kkaṉ ṉatuceyta taccaṉ vaṭukaṅ kūṟṟaṉ a
7. vaṉ makaṉ kuṟaip paṇi muṟṟuvikka puḷḷa na
8. kkaṉ avaṉukkuk kāṇiyāka aṭṭiṉa pūmip paṉ
9. ṉi nāṭ ṭiraṇṭu kūṟṟilum ūrkūḷattuk kīḻ
10. tarila nīr pāṭu kāl loro vayal patak
11. ku nel
irāmanātapuram tiṇṭukkal tālukāvil uḷḷatu.  tiṇṭukkal vakaiyaṟāl cakkāmpaṭṭi jamīṉtār cēkaram eṉṟu itu makkeṉciyiṉ piratiyil kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  ūrukku vaṭapuṟam mantaiyiṉ paṭapāṟaiyiṉ mēl cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  (eṇ 600 of 1905) itu 10 - ām nūṟṟāṇṭu tamiḻ kalveṭṭu, itu - pāṇṭiyaṉ māṟaṉ caṭaiyaṉ āṭciyil veṭṭappaṭṭatu.  aracaṉ cōḻanāṭṭaic cērnta iṭavaiyiṉ mītu naṭattiya irāṇuvat tākkutaliṉ poḻutu varaiyappaṭṭa kalveṭṭākum.
parāntakap paḷḷi vēlaṉ eṉṟa nakkaṉ puḷḷaṉ;  aracaṉ māṟaṉ caṭaiyaṉōṭu, cōḻanāṭṭiluḷḷa iṭavay mīta eṭutta paṭai
yeṭuppil kalantu koṇṭāṉ.  ivaṉ taṉ peyarāl puḷḷaṉēri kuḷattai veṭṭiṉāṉ.  kaṟkaḷ patippittāṉ.  ūṟṟait tōṇṭiṉāṉ.  āṉāl avai niṟaivu peṟavillai.  āṉāl avaṉ makaṉ puḷḷa nakkaṉ eṉpavaṉāl niṟaivu ceyyap peṟṟatu.  kaltaccaṉ vaṭukkaṉ kuṟṟaṉ eṉpavaṉ antap paṇiyaic ceytu muṭittāṉ.  puḷḷaṉ nakkaṉ avaṉukkuk kāṇiyāka paṇṇi nāṭu paṭakkunāṭu eṉṟa irupakutikaḷilum vayalkaḷ aḷittu intak kuḷattiṉ talaimai paṇiyiliruntu kālvāymūlam nīrpāyccap peṟṟatu.
muṟkālattil aracarkaḷ, tamiḻ eḻuttilum (vaṭṭeḻut tilum) kiranta eḻuttilum, pirāmi eḻuttilum, vaṭamoḻi eḻuttilum mukkiyamāṉa aracu āṇaikaḷai ōlaikaḷil eḻuti vantārkaḷ.  ataṉ mītu araca muttiraikaḷ iṭappaṭṭu vantaṉa.  piṉṉar nīṇṭa nāḷ irukkavēṇṭiya pattiraṅkaḷai ceppēṭu
kaḷilum kalveṭṭukaḷilum eḻutappaṭṭuvantaṉa.  pala ceppēṭukaḷai aṟiyāmaiyāl aḻittuviṭṭārkaḷ.  pala leyṭaṉ, piriṭṭaṉ mutaliya nāṭukaḷil uḷḷa tol poruḷ kāṭci cālaiyil pātukāttu vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  cilar kōyil cuvarkaḷilum, paṭikaḷilumuḷḷa kalveṭṭukaḷ, kōyilkaḷai iṭittuk kaṭṭum poḻutu ataṉ arumai teriyātu putaittum aḻittum viṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ.
kalveṭṭukkaḷai, kallil cetukkumuṉ maiyāl kallil eḻuti, piṉṉar uḷikoṇṭu cetukkuvārkaḷ.  mutalil civappu maiyāl eḻutuvatu marapu.  cila kōyilkaḷil uḷḷa kalveṭṭukkaḷ civappu mai koṇṭu eḻuti eḻuttukkaḷ veṭṭat toṭaṅki niṟaivu peṟāmal irukkiṉṟaṉa.  civappu maiyāl eḻutiya eḻuttukkaḷ cila veṭṭappaṭṭum cila veṭṭap paṭāmalum irukkiṉṟaṉa.  civappu mai eḻuttukkaḷ 700, 800 - āṇṭukaḷukku mēlākiyum iṉṉum maḻaiyum, veyilum, kāṟṟum tākkiyum civappu mai maṅkātu maṟaiyātu puttam putitu pōl tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.
ippoḻutu orulaṭcattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa kalveṭṭukaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.  cumār 25 - āyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa kalveṭṭu
kaḷum, ceppēṭukaḷum paṭiyeṭuttu vācikkap peṟṟu nūlvaṭivam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
nūṟṟaimpatu āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ aṟiñar mekkaṉci  eṉṉum āṅkilēyar pala kalveṭṭukaḷaiyum, ceppēṭukaḷaiyum tēṭi tokuttu āyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭavaikaḷai nūlakattil pātukāttu vaittuḷḷār.
mutaṉ mutalāka 1892il āṅkilattil teṉ intiya cācaṉaṅkaḷ eṉṉum peyarāl ṭākṭar i. halṭc pi.eyc. ṭi. (Dr. E. Hultzsch Ph. D) avarkaḷ pala pakutikaḷai veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār.  tiru. ti. nā. cuppiramaṇiyam pōṉṟavarkaḷ aritiṉ muyaṉṟu palacācaṉaṅkaḷaic cēkarittup paṭittu nūl ākkam ceytuḷḷārkaḷ.  (South Indian Temple Inscriprtions) 1929. putuk kōṭṭai māvaṭṭac cācaṉaṅkaḷum mūlam paṭikkappaṭṭu veḷivantuḷḷaṉa.  tiru. ci. civarāmamūrtti iṇṭiyaṉ epikirāpi aṇṭ cavut iṇṭiyaṉ kiripṭ eṉṟa nūliṉ mūlamākavum cila kalveṭṭukaḷ paṭikkappaṭṭu 1966 - il nūl vaṭivam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
nantavaṉam
aṉṟāṭam naṭaipeṟum pūcaikaḷukkum tiruviḻākkaḷukkum malarkaḷ tēvaippaṭuvatāl, puṣpavitiyil niyamikkappaṭṭa malarkaḷ kōyilkaḷukku kiṭaippataṟkāka nantavaṉaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  mikat toṉmaiyāṉa kālattiliruntē tamiḻakattil ālayaṅkaḷukkāka nantavaṉaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭuttap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  nantavaṉaṅkaḷil pūcaikku ākā malarkaḷ iṭam peṟā.  pūcaikkuriya malarc ceṭikaḷai varicaiyāka naṭṭi nīrpāycci pūtta malarkaḷai viṭiyaṟ kālattilē paṟittu toṭuttuk kōyilukkuc cērppikkap paṭum.  pūp paṟittu mālaikaḷ kaṭṭum paṇi mikavum ciṟanta toṇṭākak karutip pala peṇkaḷ taṅkaḷai ip paṇikku arppaṇittuḷḷaṉar.  caivattil camayattil civakāmiyāṇṭār eṉpavarum vaiṇavattil periyāḻvārum ippaṇiyil īṭupaṭṭa toṇṭarkaḷākum.  pūppaṟittu mālai toṭuttalum tirukkōyil meḻukutal, kōlam pōṭutal, tēvāram pāṭutal naṭaṉamāṭutal eṉṟa teyvap paṇikku pala uyarkulap peṇkaḷ taṅkaḷai arppaṇittu iṉṟu kattōlikka camayattiluḷḷa kaṉṉiyarkaḷ pōl iruntu vantaṉar.  āṇkaḷilum ivvaṟappaṇikkut taṅkaḷai arppaṇittuk koṇṭa aṭiyavarkaḷ palar uṇṭu.  nantavaṉaṅkaḷai niṟuvi kiṇaṟukaḷait tōṇṭi taṇṇīr pāycci malar paṟitta mālai toṭukkum tiruppaṇikku eṉṟum tavaṟātu naṭaipeṟuvataṟku vēṇṭiya nilaṅkaḷ māṉiyam aḷitta aracarkaḷum, vaṇikarkaḷum uṇṭu.  pūkkaḷ kōṭṭuppū, koṭippū, nirppū, nilappū eṉa nāṉku vakaiyākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.  ivaikaḷukku taṇṇīr pāyccu
vataṟkuk kēṇikaḷum taṭākaṅkaḷum ēṟpaṭutta paṭṭiruntaṉa.  cila nantavaṉaṅkaḷ mā, palā, vāḻai, teṉṉai, kamuku mutaliya maraṅkaḷai vaittu vaḷarttumvaruvar.  kōyilkaḷil tiruvamutu ceytavaṟku naṉcey, puṉcey nilaṅkaḷait tāṉam ceytatāka kalveṭṭukaḷum ceppēṭukaḷum cāṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.  inta nilaṅkaḷil viḷaiyum nellum itara tāṉaṅkaḷum naivēttiyaṅ
kaḷukkum tiruppaṇiyil īṭupaṭṭa ūḻiyarkaḷiṉ piracātaṅ
kaḷukkum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  tāṉiyaṅkaḷaic cēkarittu vaikkap periya kaḷañciyaṅkaḷaiyum kaṭṭuvar.  tirukkōyilkaḷil naivēttiyaṅkaḷ tayār ceyya maṭappaḷḷi kaḷum uḷa.


inta atikāram eḻuta 
ātaravāka irunta nūṟkaḷ
1. pāṇṭiyaṉ ceppēṭukaḷ pattu : tamiḻ varalāṟṟuk kaḻakam ceṉṉai mutaṟpatippu 1967.
2. kalveṭṭiyal āciriyar: irā. nākacāmi, naṭaṉa kācinātaṉ maṟṟumiruvar ceṉṉai 1972.
3. ceṅkam naṭukaṟkaḷ - irā. nākacāmi, ceṉṉai 1974
4. cōḻaṉ pūrva paṭṭayam: ceṉṉai aracuk kīḻtticaik kaḻaka veḷiyīṭu eṇ V 1950
5. kalveṭṭu viḷakka urai - vittuvāṉ vai. cuntarēca vāṇṭaiyār uraiyuṭaṉ kaṭalūr 1955
6. cācaṉa mālai  veḷiyiṭṭavar e. rājam ceṉṉai 1966
7. ilakkiya ārāycciyum kalveṭṭukkaḷum - catāciva paṇṭārattār ceṉṉai 1961

ālayattiṉ tattuva viḷakkam
kōyiliṉ tattuvam
kōyil uṭampai virittuk kuṟippiṭuvatāl carīrap piratāram eṉappaṭum.  irutayattai virittuk kuṟippiṭuvatāl irutayap piratāram eṉappaṭum.  ivviruvakaippaṭṭa kōyilil uṭampiṉ pañcakōcam mutalāṉavaikaḷaik kāṭṭuvatu eṉṟum uḷḷattil collum poruḷumāyuḷḷa vitattait kuṟippittuk kāṭṭuvatum eṉak kōyilkaḷai .iruvitamāka namatu muṉṉōrkaḷ kaṇṭaṉar.
tattuva nūṟkaḷiṉ viḷakkam
evvuyirum parāparaṉ cannitiyatākum
	ilaṅku muyir uṭalaṉaittum īcaṉkōyil
eṉṟum 
uḷḷam peruṅkōyil ūṉuṭam pērālayam
kaḷḷap pulaṉaintum kāḷā maṇiviḷakkē
eṉṟum, 
kuṟikaḷum aṭaiyāḷamuṅ kōyilum
neṟikaḷu malar niṉṟatōr nērmaiyum
eṉṟum tattuva ñāṉikaḷ ālayattai iṟaivaṉ uṟaiyum kōyilākavum uyirai avaṉatu cannitiyākavum kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
kōyilil karppak kiraham, arttamaṇṭapam, makā maṇṭapam, nāṉa maṇṭapam, alaṅkāra maṇṭapam, capā maṇṭapam eṉṉum āṟu maṇṭapaṅkaḷum uṭampiluḷḷa, 1 mūlātāram,  2 cuvātiṭṭāṉam, 3 maṇipūrakam, 4 aṉākatam, 5. vicutti, 6 ākñai eṉṉum āṟu ātāraṅkaḷaiyum uṇarttuvatākum.
ālayattil aintu pirakāraṅkaḷ uṇṭu.  avaikaḷil mutalām āvaraṇam kaṇṇukkup pulappaṭum aṉṉamaya kōcattaiyum 2ām āvaraṇam kaṇmēntiriyaṅkaḷum pirāṇaṉuṅ kūṭi niṟpatākiya pirāṇamaya kōcattaiyum mūṉṟām āvaraṇam ñāṉēntiriyaṅ kaḷum maṉatuṅ kūṭiyuḷḷa maṉōmaya kōcattaiyum nālām āvaraṇam ñāṉēntiriyaṅkaḷum puttiyuṅkūṭiyuḷḷa viññāṉa maya kōcattaiyum aintām āvaraṇam antakaraṇa viruttiyum āṉantamum kūṭiya āṉantamaya kōcattaiyum kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa.  
ēḻu pirakāraṅkaḷ uṇṭu.  eṉil avaikaḷ tōl, irattam, māmicam, mētai elumpu, maccai, cukkilam eṉṉum eḻuvakait tātukkaḷaiyum kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpataik kāṇka.  katavukaḷ mūṭuṅkāl civaliṅkat tirumēṉiyai maṟaippatālum tiṟakkuṅkāl civatericaṉam nikaḻtalāṉum atupōl kōpam, ācai āṇavam eṉpaṉa maṟaikkil āṉmaliṅkam kāṇamuṭiyātu pōtaliṉ avittaiyōṭu avai nīṅkiyaviṭattu āṉmaliṅkaṅ kāṇap
paṭalāṉum maṟaippaiyum tiṟappaiyum kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
irācacapai, tēvacapai, nirutta capai, kaṉakacapai, ciṟcapai pōṉṟa capaikaḷ pirāṇamayam maṉōmayam viññāṉamayam, āṉantamayam eṉṉum 5 kōcaṅkaḷaiyuṅ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa.
tūpi allatu kumpam itu irutaya kamalamāy viḷaṅkuvatu itaṉuṭaiya kīḻiṭam pirutivita tattuvam, mēl nāḷam aṉpu mutalāṉa irupattumūṉṟu tattuvaṅkaḷām;  nāḷattiluḷḷa muṭkaḷ amarēcaṉ mutal cikētaṉ īṟākiya eḻupattiraṇṭu puvaṉaṅkaḷāy, eṭṭu itaḻkaḷ eṭṭut tattuvaṅkaḷām aṟupattu nāṉkāyuḷḷaṉa kēcara vaṭivaṅkaḷām.  īcuram catācivam eṉṉum iraṇṭu tattuvaṅkaḷ nāḷamām pū eṉpavaṟṟiṉ nuṉi aṭimuṭiccu ākiyavaikaḷ māyaiyām.  pokuṭṭu caktiyām.  appokuṭṭil tōṉṟum aimpattoru vaṭivam civa tattuvaṅkaḷām.  kalacaṅkaḷ cattikaḷām.  itaṉāl irutaya kamalattil paramacivaṉ tikaḻkiṉṟa uṇmai kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu.
uṭampiṉ piramarantira mattiya kapālattuvāramē uttara kōpuravāyilām.  kāṟperuviraliṉ muṭipum kaiyiṉ naṭuviralkaḷiṉ muṭipum kutattuvāramum takkaṇakōpura vāyilām.  puruvamattiyamē antarāḷa kōpura vāyilām uttiyē palipiṭamām.  irutayam tuvacat tampam, pañcakōcam antak karaṇam puruṭaṉ eṉṟum kaṇṭam nanti eṉṉum aṟivar civayōkikaḷ.
tuvacat tampam cumumuṉānāṭiyām iṭaikalai piṅkalai nāṭikaḷ itiṟceṉṟu acaivaṟṟut tampam pōl niṟkun taṉmai varutaliṉ ataṟkoppāyiṟṟu iṇṭalicatti pōṉṟatu.  tuvacattampam eṉṟu kūṟuvārum uḷar .
itai, 
āyapatitāṉ aruṭ civaliṅkam
āya pacuvu maṭalēṟeṉa niṟkum
āya palipīṭa mākunaṟ pācamām
āya varaṉilai yāyntu koḷvārkkē
eṉpataṉāṟ ṟeḷika.
tiruvāci piraṇava vaṭivamām.  itaṉāl aṉaittum piraṇavattiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟi oṭuṅkum eṉpatum, maṟṟum piraṇavam ellāt tēvarkaḷukkum piṟappiṭa māyum paracivattiṟku iruppiṭa māyum uḷḷa teṉpatum kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
itai,
ōṅkāramē naṟṟiruvāci yuṟṟataṉi
ṉīṅkā veḻuttē niṟai cuṭarā - māṅkān
maṟṟā raṟivaraṇi yampalattā ṉāṭalatu
peṟṟār piṟappaṟṟār piṉ
eṉpataṉālum piṟavāṟṟāṉum uṇarka.
kōyilil tiruvalakiṭal maṇattiṉpāluḷḷa kāmam vekuḷi mutalāṉa kuppaikaḷaiyum toṇṇūṟṟāṟu tattuvaṅkaḷeṉṉum kuppaikaḷaiyum kaḷaitalaik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu.
itai, 
kāmam vekuḷi kaḻiperum poyyeṉum
tūymaiyil kuppai tolaiviṉṟik kiṭantatai
yātikaiḻntu pōkki
eṉpataṉālum
poyttuṟu kuppai kiḷain toṇi poṅku
muttai yaḷitteṉa muttai taṉakku
eṉpataṉālum piṟavāṟṟāṉām uṇarka.
tiru meḻukkiṭal mēṟkūṟip pōntavāṟē kuppaikaḷaik kaḷainta uṭaṉ tattuva ñāṉameṉum naṟuñ cāṇattaik koṇṭu aṉpeṉṉum nīruṭaṉ kalantu meḻukil, maṉōlayan tikaḻumiṭattu takarācūravoḷiyākiya civaliṅkam iṉitu viḷaṅkum eṉpataik kuṟikkiṟatu. 
itai,
ciṉa mutaliya mayaltīra māṟṟupu
 viṉaitapu vāymai meḻukkai yaṉpeṉum
puṉaloṭu viraviyē pūci ṉallatu
maṉameṉu maṉaiyiṭai varāti liṅkamē
eṉpataṉālum,
porutiṟa ṉamaiyiruḷ niṟattu
matamuṭai yaṭuciṉattai vakaikkaṭāvum
yāppaviḻt takaṟṟi yampukoṭu meḻuki
eṉpataṉālum, piṟavāṟṟāṉu muṇarka.
tiruviḷakkiṭal maṉam eṉṉum takaḻiyil vivēka meṉṉum neyyai vārttu uyir eṉṉum tiriyaip pōṭṭu pirāṇaṉākiya kāṟṟai niṟutti āṇavamākiya iruḷai nīkki civañāṉa oḷiyaip parappuntaṉmaiyaik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu.
uṭampeṉu maṉaiyakattuḷ uḷḷamē takaḻiyāka
maṭampaṭu muṇar neyyaṭṭi yuyireṉun tirimayakki
yiṭampaṭu ñāṉattīyāl erikoḷaviruntu nōkkiṟ
kaṭampamar kāḷaitātai kaḻalaṭi kāṇalāmē
eṉavum ñāṉa viḷakkiṉai yēṟṟi eṉavum aruḷ viḷakkukaḷēṟṟit tupai viruḷait tuṟantu muṉpuṟa meyyeṉum vitāṉam virittu eṉavum piṟavum kūṟumāṟṟāṉaṟika.
tiraic cīlaipōl, māyai eṉṉum tiraiyē civattai aṟiya voṭṭāmal maṟait tirukkiṉṟa teṉṟuṇarntu, ataṉai amamāyāviyākiya paramacivaṉatu tiruvaruḷāl (vāḷāl) kiḻitteṟintu viṭṭu ñāṉak kaṇṇāl civamākiya iliṅkattai nāṭi nōkkal vēṇṭum eṉpataik kuṟikkiṉṟatu tiraiccilai.
itai, māyap paṭalaṅkīṟit tūya ñāṉa naṭaṉam peṟṟaṉaḷ eṉavum māyait tiraiyai nīkki  niṉṉai yāraṟiya vallār eṉavum piṟar kūṟumāṟṟāṉaṟika.
itaṉāṟ pōnta poruḷ eṉṉaiyō veṉiṉ?  tēvālayaṅkaḷai tēvataikaḷākavē pāvittu vaḻipaṭal vēṇṭum eṉpatām.  eṉavē civālayattai civamākavē cintittut taricittu vaḻipaṭal vēṇṭum eṉpatu koḷḷak kiṭantamai kāṇka..
aṟṟākaliṉṟē, ālayantāṉum araṉeṇat toḻumē1 eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷai yiṭukiṉṟatu civañāṉa pōtta tirumaṟaiyum.
 eṉavē kōyil eṉpatu uṭal eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭum.  kōyil iṟaivaṉ uṟaiyum illattiṉ puṟacciṉṉamāka eṇṇap paṭum.  puṟac ciṉṉattai uṭal amaippiṟkum ataṉ tattuvattiṟkum ēṟpa amaikkavēṇṭum eṉṟu ākamaṅkaḷ aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa.  tamiḻ ākamam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum tirumantiram uṭalai naṭamāṭum kōyil eṉṟu nayampaṭa uraikkiṉṟāṉ.
paṭamāṭak kōyiṟ pakavark koṉṟīyil
naṭamāṭak kōyil nampark kaṅkākā
naṭamāṭak kōyil nampark koṉṟīyil
paṭamāṭak kōyiṟ pakavark kāmē
caiva camaya tattuvam āṟānilaiyum piṇṭavaṭiviṟ kāṭṭum paṭam pōl viḷaṅkuvatu tirukkōyil.  itu niṉṟu payaṉ aḷikkum aruḷ nilaiyam.  civaṉai māṟā niṉaivuṭaṉ yāṇṭum iyaṅkum makkaḷiṉ uṭal naṭamāṭum nalla tirukkōyil avartam maṟavar niṉaiviṟ kuriya iṭaṅkaḷ eṭṭu.  avai niṟṟal, iruttal, kiṭattal, naṭattal, mellal, tuyilal, viḻittal, imaittal eṉpaṉa.  makkaḷ uṭal naṭamāṭum kōyil eṉṟu payaṉaruḷum ciṟappuṭaittatu.  it tirukkōyilukkuc ceyyum ciṟappu muḻuvatum paṭam pōṉṟa inta illil irukkum paramaṉaic cārum.  āṉāl paṭamāṭak kōyil vaḻipāṭu āṇṭē taṅki naṭamāṭakkōyilai naṇṇuvatillai.  ivaiyiraṇṭum muṟaiyē kal eḻuttum karuttu eḻuttum ākum.  itaṉ karuttu māṭaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya kōyilāṉatu tattuvaṅkaḷaik kāṭṭum paṭam pōṉṟa illil irukkum.  ātāraṅkaḷākiya māṭaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya naṭamāṭum uṭampāṉa kōyil irukkum.
ciṟpa ācāriyar
peruṅkōyilkaḷaiyum vilaimatikka oṇṇāta kaṟ cilaikaḷaiyum, veṅkalap paṭimaṅkaḷaiyum paṭaitta ciṟpa vallunarkaḷ.  ivarkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyāḷarkaḷāl tapatikaḷ (tapatikaḷ) eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭaṉar.
ciṟpa ācāriyarkaḷil poṟkollarkaḷ, kaṉṉārkaḷ, marattaccarkaḷ, kaltaccarkaḷ, irumpuvēlaiceyyum kollarkaḷ eṉṟu aintu pirivukaḷ uṇṭu.  ivarkaḷil kōyilkaḷ, cilaikaḷ veṇkalap paṭimaṅkaḷ ceyyum taṭṭārkaḷ ciṟpikaḷ patikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar.  ivarkaḷ ciṟpa nūṟkaḷ payiṉṟavarkaḷ.  ākamaṅkaḷiṉ nuṇukkamum aṟintavarkaḷ.
ivarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷ;  paṇṭaiyat tamiḻnāṭṭil makkaḷ, avarkaḷ vāḻum nilam paṟṟi, kuṟiñci nilamākkaḷ, mullai nilaimākkaḷ, marutanilamākkaḷ, neyta nilamākkaḷ eṉṟu nāṉila mākkaḷāka vakukkap peṟṟiruntaṉar.  marutanilattil, ellā makkaḷukkum iṉṟiyamaiyāta uṇavu uṟpatti unti eḻuntatu.  ātiyil innilamakkaḷ kallāl nilattaik kiṇṭik kiḷaṟi payirttoḻil ceytuvantaṉar.  innilattil irumpu kiṭaittatu.  ataṉ payaṉāka pala irumpuk karuvikaḷ ceyyap peṟṟaṉa.  uḷi, cuttiyal, vāḷ, tamar ūci, arivāḷ, kōṭari, maṇveṭṭi, iḻaippuḷi pōṉṟa karuvikaḷ ceyyap peṟṟaṉa.  irumpuk karuvikaḷ eḻuntatiṉ payaṉāka uḻavut toḻil vaḷarntu vaḷam peṟṟaṉa.  arivāḷ, maṇveṭṭi, kalappai, kōṭari mutaliya uḻutoḻil ceytavaṟkuriya irumpu vēlai ceyyum kollar toḻilum maravēlai ceyyum taccar toḻilum eḻuntatu.  makkaḷ uḻutal ceyvataṟku, māṭukaḷaip paḻakkiṉar.  māṭukaḷ uḻuvataṟkum, maram aṭikkavum, pōr aṭikkavum, poti cumakkavum, vaṇṭi iḻukkavum, vākaṉamākavum payiṟṟappaṭṭaṉa.  nāḷaṭaivil cempu, pittaḷai, veḷḷi, ulōkaṅkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa.  ātiyil kaḷimaṇṇāl pāṉaikaḷum, caṭṭikaḷum, kutilkaḷum ceṅkalkaḷum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.  appāl teyva uruvaṅkaḷum icaik karuvikaḷāṉa mattaḷamum, iṭai curuṅkiya paṟaikaḷum ceṅkalkaḷum cuṭumaṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu nīṇṭanāḷ nilaittu niṟka vaḻikāṇappaṭṭatu.  nāḷaṭaivil paṭippaṭiyāy veṇkala pāttiraṅkaḷum, kal maravaikaḷum, kal caṭṭikaḷum, kal viḷakkukaḷumāka kaṉṉār toḻilum, kal taccar toḻilum eḻuntaṉa.  uṇavup poruḷ
kaḷum, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum, veṅkala pāttiraṅkaḷum, marattiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa maravaikaḷum, viḷaiyāṭṭup poruḷum, ulōkattiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa uṇṇum pāttiraṅkaḷum, uṇavup poruḷkaḷaiyum nīraiyum cēmittu vaikkum vaṭṭil, cempu, kopparai, aṇṭā mutaliya pāttiraṅkaḷum kalliṉāl ceyyap
paṭṭa pāṉai, caṭṭi, viḷakkukaḷum ceyyappaṭṭatāl, vaṇikam perukiṉa.  piṟa nilaṅkaḷil vāḻum makkaḷukkum, maruta nila
makkaḷukkup paṇṭa māṟṟāka nel, eḷ, kampu, kēḷvaraku, payaṟu, uḷuntu, mutaliya uṇavup poruḷkaḷaiyum, arivāḷ, kōṭari, pāṉai, caṭṭi pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷai viṟṟu vaṇikam vaḷarttaṉar.  itaṉpiṉ maruta nilam vaḷam peṟṟu eḻuntatu.  aṅku māṭa
māḷikaikaḷum kūṭa kōpuramum, kōyilum, araṇmaṉaiyum tōṉṟiyatu.  eṅkum ciṟṟūrkaḷum, nakaramum, paṭṭiṉamum eḻuntaṉa.  kaṭal kaṭantu toḻilāṟṟum vaṇikam vaḷarttaṉar.  nilam celvac ceḻippuṟṟatu.  kait toḻilkaḷ pala tuḷirttaṉa.  maruta nilattil pala toḻilāḷarkaḷ perukiṉar.
toḻil vaḷarcciyiṉ payaṉāy maruta nilam poruḷātāra vaḷam peṟṟatu.  aṅku aṟivu vaḷarcci eḻuntatu.  kalvi tuḷirttatu.  kalai vaḷarntatu.  camayam tōṉṟiyatu cāttiraṅkaḷ malarntatu.  icaiyum, eḻuttum piṟantatu.  aḻakuṇarcci arumpiyatu.  teyvaṅkaḷ tiruviḷaiyāṭal purintaṉa.
poruḷātārac ceḻippiṉ payaṉāy kalaikaḷ kaṇakkilātu kiḷarnteḻuntaṉa.  ciṟpattiṟaṉ mikka vallunarkaḷ palar tōṉṟiṉar.  maṇ, kal, cuṇṇam, maram, ulōkam, tantam mutaliya poruḷkaḷāl eṇṇaṟṟa kaiviṉaip poruḷkaḷ ceyyap paṭṭaṉa.  cempu, poṉ, veḷḷi, pittaḷai, veṇkalam, īyam, nākam mutaliya tātup poruḷkaḷāl pala teyvat tiruuruvaṅkaḷ vārppaṭattu vārttum uḻi koṇṭu cetukkiyum aḻakuṟac ceyyap peṟṟaṉa.  pala uyir ōviyaṅkaḷ uruppeṟṟeḻuntaṉa.  teyvat tiruuruvaṅkaḷ tiṟampaṭac ceytamaiyālum, aruḷ poliyum tirukkōyilkaḷ pala āṇṭavaṉukku amaittatiṉ payaṉākavum ivarkaḷ teyva kammiyar eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉar.  mayaṉ, vicuvakarmā pōṉṟa teyva kammiyarkaḷ ivarkaḷ marapil tōṉṟiṉar.
ivarkaḷil maṇṇāṟ pāvaikaḷaic ceypavarkaḷ maṇṇīṭṭāḷar eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭaṉar.  tātup poruḷ kaḷiṉāl paṭimaṅkaḷaiyum kalliṉāṟcilaikaḷaiyum, ceṅkalli ṉālum, karuṅkalliṉālum, paḷiṅkuk kaṟkaḷālum, veṇmai yāṉa cutaikaḷiṉālum, maraṅkaḷiṉālum, tiruuruvaṅkaḷaiyum, tirukkōyilkaḷaiyum camaippavarkaḷ kammiyar, ciṟpikaḷ, tapatikaḷ, kaltaccarkaḷ eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭaṉar.  kaṭṭiṭak kalai amaippil tamiḻ nāṭṭil uḷḷa ciṟpikaḷ paṇṭu  toṭṭu ulakilē oppuvamaiyaṟṟavarkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟalām.  cintu veḷiyil iṟṟaikku aiyāyiram āṇṭukaṭṭu muṉṉar periya nakaraṅkaḷaiyum, makōṉṉatamāṉa māperum māḷikaikaḷaiyum, ulakilē uyarnta upparikkaikaḷaiyum, mūṭucākkaṭaikaḷaiyum, ulakam kaṇṭum kēṭṭum aṟiyāta cey kuḷaṅkaḷaiyum eṭuppittavarkaḷ tamiḻakattuc ciṟpikaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷēyāvar.  vicuvakarmāviṉ vaḻivantavarkaḷēyāvar.  avarkaḷ tirāviṭarkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatāl vēṟu iṉattavarkaḷ ākiviṭamuṭiyātu.  tirāviṭa camūkattiṉ muṉṉōṭikaḷ tamiḻar eṉṟu varalāṟṟup pulamai  mikka aṟiñarkaḷ colvatiṉ mūlam cintu veḷi nākarikattaiyum aṅku kāṇappeṟṟa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷaiyum kōyilkaḷaiyum, kuḷattaiyum civaṉ, civaliṅkam, umātēvi, āṭavallāṉ, nilatteyvam, nīrt teyvam pōṉṟa tiruuruvaṅkaḷaiyum paṭaittavarkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭuc ciṟpikaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ eṉpatil eḷḷaḷavum aiyam illai.
ulakilē uyarnta nākarikamāka cumār 4000 m āṇṭiṟku muṉ eḻunta ekiptiya nākarikamum, pāpilōṉ nākarikamum, kirīṭ nākarikamum tirāviṭa ciṟpat toṭarṭaiyatu eṉṟum, cintu veḷi nākarikattai aḷitta āriya maṟavarkaḷē ekiptu, pāpilōṉ, kirīṭ  nākarikattaiyum aḷittārkaḷ eṉṟu iṉṟu pala varalāṟṟup pulavarkaḷ kūṟuvatōṭu tamiḻarkaḷum, avarkaḷiṉ teyvamākiya civaṉum, civaneṟiyum kalaiyum orukālattil ulakam muḻuvatum paraviyuḷḷatu eṉpataṟku cāṉṟu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.  iṉṟillā viṭiṉum viraivil ulakil uyarnta kaṭṭiṭak kalaikku aṭikōliyavarkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭuc ciṟpikaḷ eṉṟu ulaka varalāṟṟilum kaṭṭiṭakkalai varalāṟṟilum mutal iṭattil poṉ eḻuttāl eḻutappaṭum eṉṟu nampa iṭam uṇṭu.
iṉṟu, ulakil, cintu veḷi nākarikattai uruvākkiya ciṟpikaḷiṉ vaḻivantavarkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟik koḷḷum ēṟṟam uṭaiyavarkaḷāy viḷaṅkupavarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭuc ciṟpikaḷēyākum.  avarkaḷ amaitta tañcaip peruvuṭaiyār kōyil vimāṉamum, tiruk kaṭampūr karakkōyilum, peṇṇāṭakam tūṅkāṉai māṭak kōyilum, āvuṭaiyār kōyil koṭuṅkaiyum, tiruvīḻilmiḻalai vauvāl nattu maṇṭapamum, citamparattil āṭum  āṭavallāṉiṉ aḻaku uruvap paṭimum, kōnērirāyapuram aimpoṉṉāṟ ceyta periya ampalak kūttaṉ paṭimamum, māmallapuram māmallapurattil uḷḷa eṇṇilā oṟṟaikkal tēr uruvaṅkaḷum, (mātirik kōyilkaḷum) maturai, tirunelvēli, kiruṣṇāpuram āḷvārtirunakari, cucīntiram kōyilil uḷḷa cilaikaḷum icait tūṇkaḷum, kuḻal tūṇkaḷum, aṇiveṭṭukkālkaḷum nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.
ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭilil iruntu, cōḻarkaḷum, pāṇṭiyarkaḷum, pallavarkaḷum, cērarkaḷum, vicaya nakara nāyakkamaṉṉarkaḷum tamiḻ nāṭṭil pala kaṟkōyilkaḷai eḻuppi tamiḻ nāṭṭu ciṟpikaḷiṉ kaivaṇṇattaik kāṭṭum cantarppattai aḷittuḷḷārkaḷ.  ataṉ piṉṉar nāṭṭil eḻunta āṭci yāḷarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭu ciṟpikaḷiṉ tiṟaṉaik kāṭṭum vaḻikaḷil kōyilkaḷō māḷikaikaḷō amaikkavillai.  ataṉāl ciṟpat toḻil kuṉṟiviṭṭatu.  ciṟpikaḷ vēṟu toḻilkaḷil īṭu paṭṭuḷḷaṉar.  ciṟpak kalai vaḷarātu pōyviṭṭatu.
ariya kalai
tañcai māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa āviṭaiyār kōyil koṭuṅkaiyil oru tāḷiṉ (kākitaṉ) parumaṉ irukkumāṟu kallai melliyatāka, nuṇukkamākat tamiḻakac ciṟpikaḷ cetukki irukkiṉṟārkaḷ.  kallai meṉmaiyāṉa ulōkat takaṭu pōl iḻaikkum nuḻaipulam vāynta nuṇṇaṟivum paṭṭaṟivum, kaivaṇṇamum meyvarap peṟṟavarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭuc ciṟpikaḷ eṉpataṟku āviṭaiyār kōyil kaṟpaṇikaḷ cāṉṟu pakarkiṉṟaṉa.
tañcai māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa tiruvīḻi miḻalaiyil uḷḷa vauvāl nattu maṇṭapattil uḷḷa periya vaḷaivukaḷai amaittu māperum maṇimaṇṭapattai kaṭṭi muṭitta makattāṉa ciṟpa vallunarkaḷiṉ ciṟanta poṟiyiyal aṟivu eṉṟum pōṟṟattakkatu eṉṟu ciṟpak kalai vallunarkaḷ aṉaivarum pōṟṟi varukiṉṟārkaḷ.  tiruvalañcuḷiyil uḷḷa tirukkōyiliṉ palakaṇi vēlai mika nuṇukkamāṉa ciṟpa vēlaippāṭukaḷ amaintatu.  aṅku ciṟpikaḷ kallai maram pōl nūl iḻai eṇṇac cetukki ulakam uḷḷaḷavum taṅkaḷ pukaḻ maṅkātu ceytuḷḷaṉar.  koṅku nāṭṭil tāramaṅkalam, tāṭik kompu eṉṉum ūril uḷḷa ciṟpa vēlaippāṭukaḷum aṟputa māṉavaikaḷēyākum.  oppaṟṟatu, ulakam kaṇṭu viyakkattakkatu.
intiyāvilē mikap periya atāvatu 218  -aṭi uyaramuḷḷa vimāṉattai ki.pi. 10 - ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil tañcaiyil cōḻarkaḷ kallāl ciṟpac ciṟapput tikaḻak kaṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.  ataṉ ucciyil uḷḷa vimāṉattiṉ mītu ēṟṟappaṭṭirukkum piramāntirak kal 80  - ṭaṉ eṭaiyuḷḷatu.  nalla ciṟpa vēlaippāṭu niṟaintatu.  960  -āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ paḻuvāṉa poruḷkaḷait tūkkum poṟikaḷai (Crane -I) kaṇṭum kēṭṭum aṟiyāta paḻaṅkālattil ipperuṅkōyilai amaittu, viṇmuṭṭum vimāṉattai eḻuppi orē kalliṉālāṉa 80 - ṭaṉ eṭaiyuḷḷa peruṅkallait tūkki oru nūl aḷavum vilakātu oru ciṟu pakutiyum uṭaiyātu ēṟṟuvittuḷḷatu ciṟpa vallunarkaḷ mūḷaikku oru putirāka iruntu varukiṉṟatu.
intac ciṟpa vēlaip pāṭukaḷ ceyaṟkariya nuṇukkam vāyntavai eṉṟu iṉṟu ellāc ciṟpikaḷum ēṟṟuk koṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ.  iṉṟu kōyilkaḷ kaṭṭivarum ciṟpikaḷ kōyil kaṭṭum celvarkaḷōṭu ceyyum oppantattil, tiru valañcuḻip palakaṇi, tiruvīḻimiḻalai vauvāl nattu maṇṭapam, āviṭaiyār kōyil koṭuṅkai, tāramaṅkalam, tāḻk kompu eṉṉum iru ūril uḷḷa ciṟpaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟait tavirttu ēṉaiya ikkōyilaic ciṟanta amaippilum, ciṟanta ciṟpa vaṭivilum aḻakiyatāy, uṟutiyāy, amaittut tarukiṟōm eṉṟu uṟuti moḻi kūṟi uṭaṉpaṭikkai eḻuti oppantam ceytu koṭuppatu vaḻakkamāka iruntu varukiṟatu.
itaṉ mūlam paṇṭaik kālattu ciṟpācāriyarkaḷiṉ ēṟṟam kuṟippiṭap paṭuvatai nām uṇaraṟpālatu.  itaṉaik koṇṭu ikkāla ciṟpikaḷ muṟkālac ciṟpācāriyarkaḷaiviṭa toḻil tiṟamaiyil piṉtaṅkiyuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇuvataṟkillai.  cantarppam aḷittāl nañcentamiḻ nāṭṭil vāḻum iṉṟaiyac ciṟpikaḷ tamatu muṉṉōrkaḷai viṭa ciṟanta ciṟpa nuṇukkam aṟintavarkaḷāy uḷḷavarkaḷ eṉpataik kāṭṭattayarāy irukkiṉṟārkaḷ paṇṭaik kālac ciṟpikaḷai viṭa iṉṟaiya ciṟpikaḷ viññāṉa rītiyāṉa arumperum āyutaṅkaḷaiyellām peṟṟuḷḷārkaḷ.
cumār patiṉāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē tamiḻarkaḷ kaṭṭiṭakkalaiyil ciṟappuṟṟiruntaṉar eṉṟu paṇṭaiyat tamiḻnāṭṭup pulavarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷatai mikaippaṭuttik kūṟappaṭṭatu.  iṉṟu āṅkilam payiṉṟa varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ cilar maṟutta pōtilum mokañtarō nākarikattiṟku muṉṉar atāvatu 5000 -m āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē tamiḻarkaḷ uyarnta māṭa māḷikaikaḷaiyum kūṭa kōpuraṅkaḷaiyum kaṭṭi vāḻntaṉar eṉpatai evarum maṟukkār eṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟēṉ.  paṇṭaiyat tamiḻ nūṟkaḷil ciṟpat toḻilukku iṉṟiyamaiyāta uṟuppukaḷāka pattup poruḷkaḷ koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.  atāvatu kal, ulōkam, ceṅkal, maram, maṇ, cutai, tantam, vaṇṇam, kaṇṭa carukkarai, meḻuku ākiyavaikaḷ ākum.  tivākara cūttiram, 
kallu mulōkamum ceṅkalu maramum
maṇṇuñ cutaiyum tantamum vaṇṇamum
kaṇṭacark karaiyu meḻuku meṉṟivai
pattē ciṟpat toḻiṟkuṟup pāvaṉa		(tivākaram)
kūṟiyiruppatu tamiḻarkaḷ kaṇṭa ciṟpa uṟuppai meyp pikkat takka cāṉṟākum.  mēlum kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉattilirunta māḷikaiyil cuṭumaṇṇāl kaṭṭappaṭṭa uyarnta māṭikaḷaiyuṭaiya illaṅkaḷilellām veṇcutaiyāl tīṭṭappeṟṟa uyir oviyaṅkaḷum, maṇṇiṇālum, kalliṉālum marattiṉālum ceyyappaṭṭa aḻakiya ciṟpa vaṭivaṅkaḷ pala amaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.  eṉṟu maṇimēkalaiyil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  avai varumāṟu,
vampa mākkaḷ kampalai mūtūr

veṇcutai viḷakkattu vittakar iyaṟṟiya

kaṇṇiya teyvatam kāṭṭunar vakukka	   
nāḷ ceytal
kōṉ vāḻum kōyilkaḷum celvarkaḷ vāḻum māṭaṅ kaḷum teyvaṅkaḷ uṟaiyum tirukkōyilkaḷum maṇṭapaṅkaḷum ciṟpak kalaiyiṟ ciṟanta cīriya pulavarkaḷāl nāḷum kōḷum naṉkaṟintu nēraṟi kayiṟiṭṭu ticaikaḷaiyum tikkukaḷaiyum atticaikaḷil niṟkum teyvaṅkaḷaiyum maṇṇaiyum atil uḷḷa ceṭikaḷaiyum nakairarāyntu kaṭṭiṭam amaikkum paṇiyait toṭaṅkuvar.  iṭattait tērntu paṭattait tērntu aḷavaik kaṇakkiṭṭu kayiṟukaḷiṉ tuṇaiyāl naṉkaḷantu iṟaivaṉai vēṇṭi tēṅkāy paḻam pū mutaliyavai paṭaittu pūcai ceytu tamatu paṇiyai ārampippar.
orutiṟañ cārā varainā ḷamaiyattu
nūlaṟi pulavar nuṇṇitiṟ kayiṟiṭṭu
tēeṅ koṇṭu teyva nōkkip 
perumpeyar maṉṉark koppaṉai vakuttu							(neṭunalvāṭai75 - 78)
aṟakkaḷat tantaṇa rācāṉ peruṅkaṇi
ciṟappuṭaik kammiyar tammoṭuñ ceṉṟu
mēlōr viḻaiyu nūneṟi mākkaḷ
pāl peṟa vakutta pattiṉik kōṭṭam		
				(cilap. naṭunaṟkātai 28 : 222- 225)
kalaimutalvarkaḷ
ciṟpak kalaikku mūla mutalvarkaḷ mayaṉ eṉṟum vicva karmā eṉṟum collappaṭupavarkaḷ.  ivarkaḷ vaḻi vantavarkaḷē tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kammiyarkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭupavarkaḷ.  ivarkaḷē ciṟpak kalaikaḷiṉ mūla nūṟkaḷai yāttavarkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu.
mayaṉ
ivarkaḷil mayaṉ acura taccaṉ eṉṟu cilar kūṟuvar.  cila nūṟkaḷil ivaṉ teyvakammiyaṉ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟāṉ.  kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil uḷḷa uvavaṉattiṉ paḷikkaṟaiyil uḷḷa putta pīṭikaiyaiyum cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭattaiyum kantiṟpāvaiyaiyum ivaṉē iyaṟṟiṉāṉ eṉṟu maṇimēkalai eṉṟa tamiḻk kāppiya nūl kūṟukiṉṟatu.  pāratam iṉṉum vaṭamoḻi itikācattil mayaṉ kāṇṭava vaṉattilirunta arccuṉaṉāl kāppāṟṟappaṭṭavaṉ eṉṟum ivaṉ pāṇṭavarkaḷukku intirap piratam eṉṉum nakaraiyum pāṇṭavarkaḷ aracu vīṟṟirukkat takka capā maṇṭapattaiyum kaṭṭiyavaṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
ivaṉ titiyiṉ makaṉ eṉṟum vicuvakarmaṉ makaṉ eṉṟum vicciravā eṉpavaṉ makaṉ eṉṟum palavāṟu nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.  mayaṉuṭaiya makkaḷ māyāvi, tuntupi, maṉu mutaliyōr eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  ivaṉ makaḷākiya maṇṭōtariyai irāvaṇaṉ maṇam ceytu koṇṭatāl irāvaṇaṉukku oppaṟṟa aḻakiya araṇmaṉaiyaiyum kōṭṭaikaḷaiyum āyutaṅkaḷaiyum mayaṉ ākkik koṭuttāṉ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
mayaṉ nākar kulattaic cērntavaṉ.  nākattai vaḻipaṭupavaṉ.  irāvaṇaṉ iyakkaṉ civaṉai vaḻipaṭupavaṉ.  intac campantattāl nākar camayamum civaneṟiyum iṇaintaṉa.  civaṉ nākattai talaimītum kaḻuttilum, kaiyilum, iṭuppilum aṇintu koṇṭār.  civaliṅkattiṉ mītu ēḻutalai nākam paṭam eṭuttu niṉṟu niḻal tarukiṉṟatu.  palavita iliṅkaṅkaḷil nākaliṅkamum oṉṟu.
nāka nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu (atāvatu ilaṅkaiyil uḷḷa nākat tīviṉiṉṟu) civaneṟiyait taḻuviya nākarkaḷ iṉṟu tamiḻakattil vāḻum kammiyarkaḷ eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu.  nākarkaḷ civa neṟiyait taḻuvi tamiḻarkaḷōṭu iraṇṭaṟak kalantu viṭṭaṉar.  eṉṟālum tamiḻakattil vāḻum kammiyarkaḷ illattil naṭaipeṟum tirumāḷ viḻāvil nākavallimuḷuttam pōṉṟa caṭaṅkukaḷ iruntu varuvatu avarkaḷiṉ paḻaiya marapu vaḻakkattaik kāṭṭuvatāka irukkiṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  nākar kulamaṅkaiyum irāvaṇaṉ maṉaiviyumāṉa kammāḷar kula maṅkai maṇṭōtari ulakil uḷḷa 10 pativirataikaḷil mutal iṭattaip peṟṟavarkaḷ.  avaḷ ciṟanta civa pakti vāyntavaḷ.  tēvāra āciriyarkaḷāl ārkalituḻ teṉ ilaṅkai aḻakar maṇṭōtarikkup pēr aruḷa aḷitta pirāṉ eṉṟu pōṟṟap peṟum ēṟṟam peṟṟavarkaḷ.  irāmaṉ maṉaivi cītaiyai viṭa maṇṭōtari aḻakilum, aṟivilum, paktiyilum, kaṟpilum kuṟṟam kūṟa muṭiyāta mācu maruvaṟṟa pattarai māṟṟuc cokkat taṅkamāvāḷ.
vicvakarmaṉ
vicuvakarmā māntai nakaril vāḻum ciṟpikaḷiṉ teyvamākum.  vicuvakarmāviṉ aintumukaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu maṉu (kollar) mayaṉ(taccar) tuvaṣṭā(kaṉṉār) cilpā(ciṟpi) vicuvaṉṉā (taṭṭār) ākiya aintu ciṟpa iṉattavarkaḷum tōṉṟiṉārkaḷ eṉṟu vicuva purāṇamum, māntaip pāl, vicayatarma nāṭakam pōṉṟa nūṟkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.  intac ciṟpikaḷ muṟkālattil māntaiyil āṭcipurinta nākar kulattaic cērntavarkaḷāvar.  intac ciṟpa kulattavarkaḷ pērāṟṟalum, miku perumaiyum vāyntavarkaḷ.  ivarkaḷ nāka vaḻipāṭu iyaṟṟum nākar neṟiyait taḻuviyavarkaḷ;  naṉi ciṟanta nākarikam peṟṟavarkaḷ.  itaṉāl ilaṅkaiyil āṭcipurinta iyakka aracarkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ paṭṭattaraci
kaḷai inta māntai nakaril vāḻnta nākarkulattil uḷḷa ōviyar kuṭumpattil uḷḷa ciṟpikaḷiṉ peṇmakkaḷiṉiṉṟē tērnteṭuttaṉar.  ilaṅkaiyil āṭcipurinta māperum acura aracaṉ cūrapaṉmaṉiṉ (cuppiramaṇiyaṉāl veṉṟu kollappaṭṭa acuraṉiṉ) paṭṭattaraci patuma kōmaḷāvum (teyva kammiyaṉiṉ tirumakaḷum) iyakkar kulattiṉ iṇaiyaṟṟa ēntalāy ilaṅkaiyai āṇṭa irāvaṇaṉiṉ (irāmaṉāl veṉṟu kollappaṭṭavaṉ)  maṉaivi maṇṭōtariyum -mayaṉ eṉṉum nākar kula araca makaḷir
kaḷēyāvar.  nākar aracaṉiṉ (ciṟpakulattavarkaḷiṉ) marumakaṉāy iruntatāl ilaṅkāpurattil irāvaṇaṉukku aḻakiya araṇmaṉaiyum, uyarnta uṟutiyāṉa araṇkaḷaiyum, puṣpaka vimāṉattaiyum amaittuk koṭuttaṉar irāvaṇaṉuṭaiya ciṟṟappaṉ kupēraṉuṭaiya maṉaivi cittiralēkā maṟṟoru teyva kammiyaṉiṉ tirumakaḷ.1
ciṟpikaḷiṉ mūtātaiyarāka vicvakarmāviṉ vaḻiyil vanta nākarkula ōviyarkuṭumpattiṉiṉṟu ilaṅkaiyai āṇṭa iyakkarkula aracarkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ perumaikkuriya aracikaḷait tērnteṭuttu maṇam purintu koṇṭaṉar.  iyakka aracarkaḷiṉ, ilaṅkāpurattiluḷḷa māntai nakar vicuvakarmāviṉ vaḻivanta aracikaḷ uṟainta mānakarāy viḷaṅkiyatu.  eṉavē innakaril nākar kulac ciṟpikaḷ palar kuṭiyēṟiyuḷḷaṉar.  itaṉai māntaip paḷḷum vicuvavicayatarma nāṭakamum vicuva purāṇamum naṉku eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.  vicuvarmā iyakkar aracarkku māntaiyil irumpuk kōṭṭaikaḷai amaittuk koṭuttaṉar.
(a) irupatu kaiyuṭaiyāṉ ṟaṉakku maṇṭōtariyai
	īntaruṇ mayaṉār paṇṇaip paḷḷikaḷāṇṭē
(ā) cūrapaṉmaṉ maṉaivi patuma kōmaḷai tantai
	curar puṉaivaṟ paṇintu kūvāy kuyilē
(i) cittira rēkai yeṉṉum taṉatiṉ makiḻ tēviyē
	teyva kammiyaṉ peṇṇeṉṟē kūvāy kuyilē
						(māntai pak 8-24)
collarumaṭai kaṭōṟum tulaṅkiya māntaiyeyti
cittira mēṭai cūḻnta ṭillinaṉ kairaṭutta
uttira tikkiṉmikka vūcik kāntattiṉālē
cuṟṟilumakala nālain tōcaṉai tūramuḷḷa
muttirai mikunta kōṭṭai ciruṭṭittār muttiṉattil

kāntamalaik kōṭṭai mēviya
kāvalaṉ pukaḻ kūṟavē			-vicayatarma nāṭakam.
kānta muṟun taṭa matilum kaḻumalarp poḻilumuṟṟa
māntai nakar				      -māntaippaḷ
nākarkaḷāṉa ciṟpācāriyarkaḷ varalāṟu purāṇakkataikaḷ āka iruppataiyuṉṉip palar aṟiñarkaḷ ēṟṟuk koḷvatillai.  mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ purāṇak kataikaḷai kaṟpaṉaik katai (myth)  eṉṟa poruḷil vaittu purāṇattai mutaltaraccāṉṟākak koḷva tillai.  āṉāl tamiḻ nāṭṭuc ciṟpikaḷiṉ pērāṟṟalukkum, peruñ ceyalukkum kalai nayattiṟkum kaṭṭiṭak kalai mēmpāṭṭiṟkum purāṇac cāṉṟu maṭṭumalla varalāṟṟu cāṉṟumuṇṭu.  intiyāvil uḷḷa cāraṉāta pautta palkalaik kaḻakattil pautta camaya aṟiya vanta hivāṉ cāṅ (Hiouen Ihsang) teṉ intiyāviṟkum, vēṟu pala iṭaṅkaḷukkuñ ceṉṟu pautta camaya vaḷarcciyaip paṟṟi eḻutiya kuṟippukaḷil nāka nāṭṭu naṅkaiyarkaḷiṉ aḻakum, avarkaḷ ayal nāṭṭuk kappalkārarkaḷai ēmāṟṟi aṭitta kaṭaṟkoḷḷaikaḷaiyum ciṟappāka nākanāṭṭu eḵku kōṭṭaiyaiyum paṟṟi kūṟiyiruppatu nāka nāṭṭiṉ ciṟpac ciṟappiṟkum nākarikattiṟkum ēṟṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭāka irukkiṟatu.1
nākarkaḷ eṭuppitta eḵku araṇ ilaṅkaiyiṉ kaṭal vaṇika vaḷarccikku iṭaiyūṟāka iruppatai aṟintu ilaṅkai aracaṉ vicayaṉ atai aḻittu viṭṭāṉ eṉṟu cīṉa vaḻippōkkaṉ yuvāṅ cuvāṅ kūṟiyuḷḷāṉ. 2 ki.pi. mutalām nūṟṟāṇṭil muṟkālac cōḻa aracaṉ karikāṟ cōḻaṉāl aḻikkappaṭṭatu eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  karikālaṉ ilaṅkaiyai veṉṟu makkaḷiṟ palarai ciṟai piṭittuc ceṉṟu kāvirik karaiyai uyartta maṇ cumakkac ceytuḷḷāṉ.3 karikāṟ cōḻaṉ īḻa nāṭṭu eḵku araṇai maṭṭumiṉṟi toṅkum pittaḷai araṇaiyum, toṅkum cempu araṇaiyum aḻittu kirēkkarkaḷ kūṟum tāmpira paraṇi eṉṉum īḻattai veṉṟu cempiyaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭattai cūṭṭiyirukkalām eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.  tāmpiram eṉpatu cempu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatāl, tāmpiraparaṇi nāṭṭaikkaip paṟṟi tāmpirak kōṭṭaiyai aḻittu karikālaṉ cempiyaṉ paṭṭam cūṭiyirukkalām eṉṟum toṅkum cempu, pittaḷai araṇkaḷai avaṉ aḻittuḷḷataṟkut tamiḻ ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷ uḷateṉṟum cilar kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.1
vicuvakarmāviṉ vaḻivanta ciṟpikaḷ poṟkollarkaḷ muṟkālattil māntaiyil nāṭu nakar amaittu eḵku cempu kōṭṭaikaḷ amaittu āṭci naṭatti vantuḷḷaṉar.  piritām eṉṉum āciriyar eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār eṉṟu itaṉai tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷum eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉa.
ciṟpa nūṟkaḷ muppattiraṇṭu uṇṭeṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.  ivaikaḷ aṉaittum vaṭamoḻiyil eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  camīpakālattil ciṟpanūṟkaḷ cila tamiḻil moḻiyākkam ceyyap peṟṟu veḷivantuḷḷaṉa.  cila āṅkilattil kūṭa veḷiyiṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  ciṟpa nūṟkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ aṭiyil varumāṟākum.

mēṟkaṇṭa ciṟpa nūṟkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭuc ciṟpikaḷāl payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu varum ciṟpa nūṟkaḷākak koḷḷalām.  āṉāl vaṭa intiya ciṟpikaḷāl payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum nūṟkaḷ cumār pattu ākum.  avaikaḷ aṭiyil varuvaṉavākum.

ciṟpikaḷ
matcaya purāṇattil muṟkālattil ciṟappuṟṟirunta 18 ciṟpikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  avarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ aṭiyil varumāṟākum:
1. pirku				10 piramaṉ
2. attiri				11 kumāraṉ
3. vaciṣṭaṉ				12 nantīcaṉ
4. viccuvakarmaṉ		13 caṉakaṉ
5. mayaṉ				14 kākā
6. nārataṉ				15 vācutēvaṉ
7. nākaṉa cittu			16 aṉiruttirā
8. vicālakcā			17 cukarā
9. purantaraṉa				18 pirukapati
ivarkaḷiṉ ciṟpa teyvamākap pōṟṟappaṭupavarkaḷ vicuvakarmāvum, mayaṉum āvar.
ciṟpikaḷ kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭuvataṟku ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil āṟuvakai campiratāyaṅkaḷ kaiyāḷappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  avai varumāṟu.
1. viccuvakarma matam 2. vicvamparamatam 3. piṅkaḷa matam 4. utpala matam  5. mayatam 6. parimaḷamatam.
purāṇaṅkaḷilum ākamaṅkaḷilum ciṟpa nūṟkaḷilum ciṟpa nūlāciriyarkaḷ 18 eṉṟum 32 eṉṟum 81 eṉṟum palavakaip peyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ciṟpikaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkavarkaḷ vicuvakarmāvum mayaṉum āvar.  vicuvakarmar tēvarkaḷiṉ ciṟpi mayaṉ acurarkaḷiṉ ciṟpi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum.  maya matam ippoḻutu muṟṟilum orē nūlāka ippoḻutu kiṭaikkiṟatu.  āṉāl vicuvakarmāviṉuṭaiya nūl pala pakutikaḷāka antantap pakutic ciṟpat toḻilāḷikkeṉṟē taṉiyākat toṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  viccuva karma vātu cātiram eṉṟa nūlai tañcai caracuvati makāl nūlnilaiyam cila āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu.  maṟṟac cila pakutikaḷum, eḷitil kiṭaikkum. tiruvāṅkūr aracu, vātu vityar 2 maṉuṣyalaya cantirikā 3 -l parattiṉam , 4. mayamatam. 5. yuktikalpataru, 6. pirukat camhitai. 7. camaṇa ñāṉa sacatratāra 8. viccuva karmap pirakācam pōṉṟa nūṟkaḷai veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. tañcai caracuvati makāl veḷiyīṭṭāḷarum, cakaḷāti kāram, vivakarma vātu cātiram, mayamatam, kāciyapa cilpam, māṉacāram, cilparattiṉam, pirāmmiya cittira kāma cātiram, pōṉṟa nūlkaḷai veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.  iṉṉum avarkaḷ pala nūṟkaḷai veḷiyiṭum muyaṟciyil toṭarntu ceyaṟpaṭṭu varukiṉṟārkaḷ.
pañcāp camakiruta nūl veḷiyīṭṭuk kaḻakam nīṇṭa nāḷ kaḷāka ciṟpa nūṟkaḷai veḷiyiṭuvatil atika akkaṟai eṭuttu varukiṟatu.
vaṅkāḷattil kalai āyvuttuṟai atika vaḷarcci peṟṟuḷḷatu.  aṅku pala aṟiñarkaḷ naṉku payiṉṟu intiyak kalai vaḷarccik keṉṟu urūpam (Rupam) kīḻkkāṭṭuk kalai (Cercental Art)   eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa pattirikaikaḷai veḷiyiṭṭum ciṟpa nūṟkaḷaip patippittum cīriya toṇṭāṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉar
ciṟpikaḷiṉ peyar
namatu nāṭṭuc ciṟpikaḷum, pulavarkaḷum tam peyarkaḷai muṟkālattil viḷamparam ceytu koḷḷa eṇṇiyatillai.  pala uyarnta nūṟkaḷil eḻutiyavarkaḷ peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭavillai.  cila nūṟkaḷil āciriyar peyarkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  āṉāl avarkaḷ taṅkaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa perumpulavarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷilē nūṟkaḷai eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ.  atē pōṉṟu namatu nāṭṭil peruṅkōyilkaḷaiyum uyariya upparikkaikaḷaiyum viṇmūṭṭum vimāṉaṅkaḷaiyum, vilaimatikka voṇṇāta veṇkalap paṭimaṅkaḷaiyum cilaikaḷaiyum paṭaitta ciṟpikaḷ taṅkaḷ peyarkaḷaik kuṟippiṭātu vāḻntu maṟaintuḷḷaṉar.  iṭaik kālattil kōyil kaṭṭuvataṟkup paṇam aḷitta maṉṉarkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum, nivantaṅkaḷ aḷitta aracar, aracikaḷ, araciḷaṅkumarikaḷ, celvarkaḷ peyarkaḷ kalveṭṭukaḷilum, ceppēṭukaḷilum tīṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  āṉāl ciṟpikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭavillai.  camīpattil cila kōyilkaḷil uḷḷa kalveṭṭukaḷiṉ mūlam cila ciṟpikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar.
ceṉṉaiyai aṭuttuḷḷa māmallapurattil uḷḷa mātirik kōyilkaḷai eṭuppitta tapatikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ teṉ intiyak kalveṭṭukaḷ (South Indian Inscription) eṉṟa nūṟkaḷiṉ mūlam  kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  intap peyarkaḷ māmallapurattil uḷḷa makōṉṉata ciṟpac ciṟappu niṟainta cīriya ciṟpak karuvūlaṅkaḷai tamiḻulakiṟku aḷitta ciṟpac celvaṅkaḷiṉ tirup peyarkaḷākak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  appeyarkaḷ aṭiyil varumāṟu. 
(1) kēvāta peruntaccaṉ (2) kuṇamallaṉ (3) payyamiḻippāṉ (4) cāta mukkiyaṉ (5) kaliyāṇi (6) tiruvoṟṟiyūr apācaṉ (7) kollaṉ cēmakaṉ.1
ilalitāyar eṉṉum ciṟpāciriyaraip paṟṟi taṇṭi eṉṉum āciriyar ki.pi. 8 ām āṇṭil eḻutiya avanti cuntari eṉṉum camakiruta nūlil ciṟappāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.  ilalitāyar māmallapurattu ciṟpa ācāriyaril oruvar.2
ki.pi. 8 ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyil vikkiramātittaṉ 11, eṉṟa aracaṉ kāñciyai veṉṟu kaippaṟṟi piṉ aṅkuḷḷa ciṟpi kuṇṭaṉ eṉpavarai aḻaittuk koṇṭu pōy paṭṭakkal eṉṉum nakaril paramaṉukku oru aḻakiya kōyilai eṭuppittāṉ.  ippoḻutu akkōyil iṭapavākaṉar kōyil eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭukiṟatu.  inta aracaṉ iraṇṭām vikkiramātittaṉ ciṟpācāriyāraic ciṟappittu tiripuvaṉācāriyar eṉṉum paṭṭam cūṭṭiṉāṉ.  ivarukku aṉavarata āccāri eṉṟa peyarum uṇṭu.
mutalām kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉiṉ āṭciyil puricai nakaril tiruppaṭakkāruṭaiya mātēvar kōyil aṭippaṭaiyiṭṭuk kaṭṭappaṭṭatu.  itaṉaik kaṭṭiya ciṟpi cantira cēkaraṉ ravi eṉṉum cōḻēntira cimma āccāri eṉappaṭum.3
cōḻap pēraracil pērum perumaiyum peṟṟu viḷaṅkiya māmaṉṉaṉ irācēntiraṉ tiruvoṟṟiyūr mūlak kōyilaip paḻutu pārttup putuppittāṉ.  ikkōyilaip paḻutupārttup putuppitta ciṟpiyiṉ peyar vīra cōḻa taccaṉ (iravi) eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭār.  tillai āṭavallāṉ kōyil vaṭakkuk kōpurattaiyum uḷ cuvaril kōpurattai amaitta ciṟpiyiṉ peyarum uruvamum eḻuta muṭivu ceytu, viruttakiri, kēcavap perumāḷ makaṉ vicu tiruppiṟaik kōṭai ācāri tirumaruṅkaṉ.  avar tampi cāri eṉṟu eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷatu. 
tirunelvēli māvaṭṭam, ampācamuttiram vaṭṭattil uḷḷa piramatēcattil uḷḷa kalāca nāta cuvāmi kōyil vicuvakarmāviṉāl nirmāṇikkappaṭṭatu teṉ intiya kōyiṟ cācaṉattil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.1

ēlē! aṇṇāṭci vantākaḷā? eṉṉa coṉṉāka? eṉakkut
tāṉ ayittup pōccu? - eṅkāvatu intat tamiḻaik kēṭṭāl atu nellai māvaṭṭattār kural eṉṟu teriya vēṇṭum. inta māvaṭṭattiltāṉ kuṟṟāla aruviyiṉ koñcum icai! kiruṭṭiṉāpurattu cilaikaḷiṉ pēraḻaku! āṟupaṭai vīṭukaḷil oṉṟāṉa tiruccentūr! taṉ porunaiyē nākarikattiṉ talaivāyil eṉṟu pōṟṟappaṭum ātittanallūr!
civañāṉa muṉivar, kumarakuruparar, tirumaṅkaiyāḻvār, kulacēkaraāḻvār, nammāḻvār, ativīrarāmapāṇṭiyar, vaṇṇaccarapam taṇṭapāṇi aṭikaḷār, eṉṟi ālparṭṭu kiruṭṭiṉār, tirukūṭarācappak kavirāyar .. iṉṉum iṉṉum.. putumaippittaṉ, mātavaiyā, kā.cuppiramaṇiyaṉār, vaiyāpuriyār, pūraṇaliṅkaṉār, cōmacuntara pāratiyār, pāratiyār, umaṟuppulavar.. iṉṉum iṉṉum tamiḻ mīṭpar moḻiñāyiṟu tēvanēyap pāvāṇar- pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷiṉ aṇivakuppu immāvaṭṭattiṉ ciṟappēyākum. ō!
nuṇkalaic celvar cāttaṉkuḷam a.irākavaṉārum piṟantu perumai cērttatu immāvaṭṭamē! nākacuramum, villuppāṭṭum iṅku pukaḻ cērkkum kalaikaḷākum.
cāttāṉkuḷam nellaiyiliruntu tiruccentūr cellum vaḻiyil uḷḷa ūrākum. vaṭivēṟkavirāyar, kaṉakacapāpatik kavirāyar, kuḻantai vēlcāmi pōṉṟa kaviñarkaḷ piṟantu pukaḻpeṟṟa ūrākum.
nuṇkalaic celvar a.irākavaṉār avarkaḷ 22.04.1902 ām āṇṭu tōṉṟiṉār. ivartam peṟṟōr tiruvāḷar aruṇācalak kavirāyar, tiruvāṭṭi āvuṭaiyammāḷ āvar. ivaratu pāṭṭaṉār vaṭivēṟkavirāyar ciṟanta muttamiḻppulavar. ivaratu muppāṭṭaṉār vīrapāku ōtuvār mikacciṟanta kaviñar; atōṭu vaḻakkaṟiñarum kūṭa! ivaratu muppāṭṭaṉāriṉ tampi aruṇācalak kavirāyar tiruvaṉantapuram araṇmaṉaip pulavarāka oḷi vīciṉār eṉpatu maṭṭumiṉṟi kuṭṭik kavirāyar eṉṟa aṭaimoḻiyōṭum vāḻntavar.
nuṇkalaic celvariṉ piḷḷait tiruppeyar iratṉa irākava utaya mārttāṇṭaṉ kavirāyar eṉpatākum. piṉṉartāṉ irākavaṉ eṉṟu maṭṭumē aḻaikkappaṭṭār.
iḷantaip paruvattil piṟanta cāttāṉkuḷattilēyē toṭakkakkalvi payiṉṟār. nellai araciṉar payiṟcip paḷḷiyil āciriyam payiṉṟu āciriyarāṉār. cuppiramaṇiyak kavirāyariṭam tamiḻ muṭutaṟak kaṟṟum, toṇṭaimāṉ piramma muttaiyaṉiṭam ōviyamum kaṟṟu ciṟanta ōviyarāka viḷaṅkiṉār.

kuṭumpattiṉ viḷaivil
cāttāṉkuḷam aruṇācalakkavirāyarukkum āvuṭaiyammāḷukkum makkaḷ aṟuvar. ivarē mutal makaṉ. kuṭumpattiṟkum .. koñcu tamiḻukkum ivarē mutalvar. 2. kumāra vaṭivammāḷ 3. vaṭivēl (iḷantaiyilē maṟaintār) 4. kumāracāmi 5. kāntimati ma.paramacivaṉ .. orē oru peṇmakaḷ. avartām ca. vīralaṭcumi ammaiyār. irākavaṉār tāymāmaṉ makaḷaiyē tiruvāṭṭi cuntarattammaiyārai maṇantu iṉiya illaṟattiṉ naṉkoṭaiyāka mallikā, cutantirā,tamiḻaraci, irā.mativāṇaṉ eṉa mūṉṟu peṇmakkaḷaiyum, oru āṇmakaṉaiyum vaḻaṅkiṉār.
taṉ makkaḷai palar maṟuttapōtum tamiḻa vaḻik kalviyilēyē cērttup paṭikka vaittuḷḷār. taṉ taṅkaiyār tirumaṇam nīṅkalāka ellāt tirumaṇaṅkaḷaiyum cīrtiruttat tirumaṇamākavē naṭattit tamiḻukkup perumai cērttirukkiṟār. tāyiṟ ciṟantoru kōvilumillai eṉṟu aṭikkaṭi colliyum tāyum mītu mikunta pācam koṇṭiruntār. cīvāṉantam, cāntam cuntaravaṭivēlu- pōṉṟa ivarōṭu naṭpuk koṇṭu tamiḻil koṇṭuḷḷa aiyappāṭukaḷai aṟintu koḷ
vārkaḷām. kuṟaḷ oppuvittāl - kācu koṭuttu ūkkappaṭuttip pārāṭṭuvatu ivaratu iṉiya vāḻviyalākum. atu iḷañciṟār
kaḷāyiruttal vēṇṭumām!
ayalakak kaḷippil
ilaṅkai, malēciyā, ciṅkappūr pōṉṟa ayalakaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu mīṇṭa nuṇkalaic celvar tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷāṉa eṅkum cellum kācu.. kā.cuppiramaṇiyaṉār, tīntamiḻppulavar tiru.vi.kaliyāṇa
cuntaraṉār, tantai periyār, tārūl iculam āciriyar pā.tāvūtcā, ilaṅkai mutalamaiccar paṇṭāranāyaka, kaṇṭi mēyar cārc-ṭi-celvā, amaiccar peri.cuntaram , mēyar caravaṇamuttu, tamiḻaka camaṉmaic cintaṉaiyāḷar pa.cīvāṉantam pōṉṟōrōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpu avarkaḷāl paṉmuṟai pārāṭṭap peṟṟār.
1935 ām āṇṭu periyāriṭamiruntu vilaki tōḻar pa.cīvāṉantattōṭu cērntu cuyamariyātai camatarmak kaṭci eṉṟoru kaṭciyai niṟuvi aṟivu eṉṉum tiṅkaḷēṭṭiṟkam āciriyarāṉār. 1932il ilaṇṭaṉ pakuttaṟivu caṅkam, viṭutalaic cintaṉaiyāḷar maṉṟam, amerikkāviluḷḷa uṇmai nāṭuvōr kaḻakam,pōṉṟa amaippuk
kaḷōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭu uṟuppiṉarāki palvēṟu nūlkaḷai moḻiyākkam ceytu tantār. ilaṅkaiyil maṉitaruḷ māṇikkam nēru perumakaṉukku āṅkilattilum, tamiḻilum niṉaivu malar veḷiyiṭap peritum utaviṉār. ciṅkappūriliruntu veḷivarum tamiḻ muracu eṉṟa itaḻil toṭarkaṭṭurai eḻuti vara ataṉ āciriyā kō.cāraṅkapāṇi vēṇṭiṉār. tamiḻar paṇpāṭṭil tāmarai eṉṟa ivaratu nūl iḷaṅkalai vakuppukaḷukkup pāṭanūlāka maturaip palkalaikkaḻakam ēṟṟup pukaḻ koṇṭatu.
āyviṉ kūrmaiyil
avaratu ūrukku aṇmaiyiluḷḷa ātittanallūrukkuc ceṉṟum, palvēṟu poruḷkaḷaip pārvaiyiṭṭum, ceṉṉai aruṅkāṭciyakam ceṉṟum āyvu ceytutāṉ ātittanallūrum porunaiveḷi nākarikamum eṉṟa nūlai eḻutiyuḷḷār.
cintu veḷi nākarikattiṟku oppiṭṭup pārttu ērāḷamāṉa karuvikaḷ, poruḷkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ paṭaṅkaḷai, aṇikalaṉkaḷ, viḷakkukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ paṭaṅkaḷait tokuttu veḷiyiṭṭiruppatu .. ivaratu paṉimalai muyaṟcip pāṅkiṉai- namakku uṇarttukiṟatu.
yārumē ceyyāta putumaiyāka nuṇkalaic celvar avarkaḷ taṉ maṉaiviyār tiruvāṭṭi cuntarattammaiyāraik koṇṭu kalainūṟ patippakam niṟuvi pala nūlkaḷaiyum veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻp paṇi ceytār eṉpatu ulaka varalāṟṟil putumaiyākum.
tammiṭamirunta ērāḷamāṉa kācukaḷ, nūlkaḷ, ariya poruḷkaḷ - ākiyavaṟṟait tirucciyiluḷḷa īpar kaṇkāṇiyar kalvi niṟuvaṉat
tiṟku vaḻaṅkiṉar eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm. avai ariya kalaip poruḷkaḷākat tulaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ilaṅkaiyil oru ciṉṉa nūl antak kālattil nūṟu urūpā koṭuttu vāṅkiyuḷḷār eṉil avaratu aṟivut tākam namakkup pulappaṭum.
pārāṭṭut tēṉmaḻaiyil
1966 ām āṇṭu teṉṉintiya tamiḻc caṅkam, caṭṭap pēravait talaivar māṇpatai cellappāṇṭiyaṉār talaimaiyil tamiḻttiru kuṉṟakkuṭi aṭikaḷār ivarukkup poṉṉāṭai pōrtti nuṇkalaic celvar eṉṟa paṭṭattai vaḻaṅkip pārāṭṭiyatu.
1937ām āṇṭu ilaṅkaiyil naṭaipeṟṟa mukammatu napi ulaka camātāṉattiṟkuc ceyta toṇṭu eṉṟa talaippil naṭaipeṟṟa kaṭṭuraip pōṭṭiyil ivar taṅkap patakkam peṟṟār. 
1966-ulakat tamiḻ mānāṭu malēciyāvil naṭaipeṟṟa pōtu tamiḻnāṭṭuk kācukaḷ eṉṟa kaṭṭurai mikac ciṟanta kaṭṭuraiyākap pōṟṟappaṭṭatu.
ceṉṉaiyil naṭaipeṟṟa ulakat tamiḻmānāṭṭil paḻaṅkāla marakkalaṅkaḷ 46 vaṭivaṅkaḷai neymavaṇṇattil (Oil paint) varaiyap peṟṟuk kāṭcikkākki pēraṟiñar aṇṇāvē peritum pōṟṟiyum, pārāṭṭiyum makiḻntār.
tamiḻvaḷarcci maṉṟa uṟuppiṉarāki tamiḻnāṭṭu varalāṟu tol
paḻaṅkālam nūl veḷiyiṭum kuḻuvil ivarum oru uṟuppiṉar eṉpatu niṉainta pōṟṟattakkatu.

1.	kāñcippurāṇam
1.	  This shrine, which is dedicated to the worship of Shiva (“warahareswara literally means the god who wards off fever”), lies between the Ekambardesvara and pandavaperumal temple;  it is easily distinguished  from the others by circular garbhagraha (akanāḻikai) originally built of cut granite without cement, it has had to be repaired by the Archacological survey of India. 
	   Some of the Characteristie decorative features of later chola art, such as the kumbha panjaram (a kind of plaster with an ornate vase below) and the tarangabodigai (a curved corbel) are found in this temple.  Both the Shrine and the mahamandpa may be assigned to the reign of kulottunga 111 (1178 - 1226) - KANCHI An Introduction to its Architecture Miss Dr. C. Meenakshi M.A.Ph.D. (Published by The publication Division govt of India New Delhi. February 1954.

He also referred to the similar head (with a trident) decorating the Southern end of the roof of Ganesa ratha.  He was also of opinion that the worship of the trident head was probably a contemporary culb.  A guide to Mamallapuram - Miss. P. T. Narayanan B.A.B.T (Madras) -1957 P 48.)

1.	Dravida buildings exist today not only in Dravida - desha (country itself but also in the Kanarese districts of Decan where in fact, the temples are several centuries older than the earliest structural temples of the Pallavas in Mamallapuram and Conjeevaram” - The Arts and Crafts of Travancore - (Chap 1. Dravida and Kerala) - Dr. Stella Kramrish (London) 1948 Page.20.
1.	The tamil races were perhaps the greatest Temple builders in the world..... the great pagoda of  Tanjore..... by far the grandest temple in India”.... “Architecture” - Encyclopaedia of Britanica 9th edition 11. P.396.
1.	   “The great Temple of Tanjore”.  This style arose under the chola or Tanjore kings in 11th century A.D when nearly all the great temples to Siva in South India were built and it continued in use in the 12th and 18th 
	centuries during which the great temples to Vishnu were erected Rep to the begining of the 16th century these temples remained almost unchanged, but at that time all South India became subject to the kings of Vijayanagar and one of these named Krishna Raya (1509-30) re built or added to most of the great temples of the South.  The chief feature of the architecture of this later period is the construction of the enormous gopurams which are so conpienos
	at Kancheepuram Chidambaram and Srirangam.  All these were built by Krishna - raya : they do not form part of the original style, but were intended as fortifications to protect the shrines from foreign unvaders and certain plunder and desecration, as the Hindu first discovered on the Muhammadan invasion of 1310 A.D also vide, “Three Main styles of Temple Architecture” - Burnell CA Bulletin of the Madras Museum.

1.	Ancient Jaffna - Mudaliar C. Rasanayagam C.C.S (Cylon) 1926 Madras.
1.	   “The Records of the Buddhist religion say:- In the middle of  a great iron city of this Ratnadvipa (Pao - Chu) was the dwelling of the Rakohasi women (Lo - l’sa).  On the towers of this city they erected two high flagstaffs with  lucky or unlucky signals which the exhibited (to allow manners) according to circumstances when merchants came to the island (Ratnadvipa) Then they changed themselves into beautiful women holding flowers and scents, and with the sound of Music they went forth to meet theries and caressingly invited them to enter the iron city then having shared with them all sorts pleasures they shut them up in an iron prison and devoured them at their lesisure”
Hiouen M.L.R Vol : i p : 148.
